--==============================================================
-- DIAGNOSTICS / PACKING
--==============================================================

local function log(message)
	print("[ChatSelector] " .. tostring(message))
end

local function warnScript(message)
	warn("[ChatSelector] " .. tostring(message))
end

local function traceback(message)
	if debug and debug.traceback then
		return debug.traceback(tostring(message), 2)
	end
	return tostring(message)
end

local unpackValues = table.unpack or unpack

local function packValues(...)
	return table.pack(...)
end

--==============================================================
-- SIGNAL COMPATIBILITY
--==============================================================

local function createSignal()
	local listeners = {}
	local signal = {}

	function signal:Connect(callback)
		assert(type(callback) == "function", "[ChatSelector] Callback must be a function.")
		local entry = { Callback = callback, Connected = true }
		local connection = {}

		function connection:Disconnect()
			entry.Connected = false
		end
		connection.disconnect = connection.Disconnect

		table.insert(listeners, entry)
		return connection
	end
	signal.connect = signal.Connect

	function signal:Fire(...)
		local arguments = packValues(...)
		for _, entry in ipairs(listeners) do
			if entry.Connected then
				local ok, err = xpcall(function()
					entry.Callback(unpackValues(arguments, 1, arguments.n))
				end, traceback)
				if not ok then
					warnScript("Signal callback failed: " .. tostring(err))
				end
			end
		end
	end
	signal.fire = signal.Fire

	return signal
end

local function connectSignal(source, callback)
	if not source then return nil end
	local ok, method = pcall(function()
		return source.Connect or source.connect
	end)
	if not ok or type(method) ~= "function" then return nil end
	local connected, connection = pcall(method, source, callback)
	return connected and connection or nil
end

local function fireSignal(signal, ...)
	if not signal then return end
	local ok, method = pcall(function()
		return signal.Fire or signal.fire
	end)
	if not ok or type(method) ~= "function" then return end

	local arguments = packValues(...)
	local fired, err = pcall(function()
		method(signal, unpackValues(arguments, 1, arguments.n))
	end)
	if not fired then
		warnScript("Could not fire signal: " .. tostring(err))
	end
end

--==============================================================
-- CHAT SETTINGS / PUBLIC API
--==============================================================

local function readLegacyBoolean(propertyName, defaultValue)
	local ok, value = pcall(function()
		return Players[propertyName]
	end)
	if ok and type(value) == "boolean" then return value end
	return defaultValue
end

local ClassicChatEnabled = readLegacyBoolean("ClassicChat", true)
local BubbleChatEnabled = readLegacyBoolean("BubbleChat", false)

local backend
local backendReady = false
local backendFailed = false
local startupStarted = false
local state = { Visible = true }
local backendConnections = {}
local queuedSystemMessages = {}
local MAX_QUEUED_SYSTEM_MESSAGES = 100

local interface = {
	ChatBarFocusChanged = createSignal(),
	VisibilityStateChanged = createSignal(),
	MessagesChanged = createSignal(),
	BubbleChatOnlySet = createSignal(),
	ChatDisabled = createSignal(),
	IncomingMessage = createSignal(),
	OutgoingMessage = createSignal(),
	SpecialKeyPressed = createSignal(),
	TextChannelsChanged = createSignal(),
	NativeChatEnabled = nil,
}

--==============================================================
-- BACKEND INVOCATION (Chat.lua ONLY)
--==============================================================

local function invokeBackend(methodName, ...)
	if not backend then return false, false, nil end

	local ok, method = pcall(function()
		return backend[methodName]
	end)
	if not ok or type(method) ~= "function" then
		return false, false, nil
	end

	local arguments = packValues(...)
	local results = packValues(xpcall(function()
		return method(backend, unpackValues(arguments, 1, arguments.n))
	end, traceback))

	if not results[1] then
		warnScript("Chat." .. methodName .. " failed: " .. tostring(results[2]))
		return true, false, results[2]
	end

	return true, true, unpackValues(results, 2, results.n)
end

local function forwardBackendSignal(signalName)
	if not backend then return end

	local ok, source = pcall(function()
		return backend[signalName]
	end)
	if not ok or not source then return end

	local connection = connectSignal(source, function(...)
		if signalName == "VisibilityStateChanged" then
			local visible = ...
			if type(visible) == "boolean" then state.Visible = visible end
		end
		fireSignal(interface[signalName], ...)
	end)

	if connection then table.insert(backendConnections, connection) end
end

local function connectBackendSignals()
	for _, name in ipairs({
		"ChatBarFocusChanged",
		"VisibilityStateChanged",
		"MessagesChanged",
		"BubbleChatOnlySet",
		"ChatDisabled",
		"IncomingMessage",
		"OutgoingMessage",
		"OutgoingMessageFailed",
		"SpecialKeyPressed",
		"TextChannelsChanged",
		}) do
		forwardBackendSignal(name)
	end
end

--==============================================================
-- VISIBILITY / FOCUS API
--==============================================================

local function queryBackendVisibility()
	local found, success, visible = invokeBackend("GetVisibility")
	if found and success and type(visible) == "boolean" then
		return visible, true
	end
	return state.Visible, false
end

local function queryLegacyGuiVisibility()
	local player = Players.LocalPlayer
	local playerGui = player and player:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui")
	local chatGui = playerGui and playerGui:FindFirstChild("Chat")

	if not chatGui or not chatGui:IsA("ScreenGui") then return nil end
	if not chatGui.Enabled then return false end

	local chatWindow = chatGui:FindFirstChild("ChatWindow")
	if chatWindow and chatWindow:IsA("GuiObject") then
		return chatWindow.Visible
	end
	return chatGui.Enabled
end

function interface:SetVisible(visible)
	if type(visible) ~= "boolean" then
		warnScript("SetVisible expects a boolean.")
		return false
	end

	if not backendReady or not backend then
		local changed = state.Visible ~= visible
		state.Visible = visible
		if changed then fireSignal(interface.VisibilityStateChanged, visible) end
		return true
	end

	local found, success, result = invokeBackend("SetVisible", visible)
	if not found or not success then
		warnScript("Chat.SetVisible did not complete.")
		return false
	end

	local actual, hasActual = queryBackendVisibility()
	if hasActual then
		local changed = state.Visible ~= actual
		state.Visible = actual
		if changed then fireSignal(interface.VisibilityStateChanged, actual) end
		return actual == visible and result ~= false
	end

	state.Visible = visible
	return result ~= false
end

-- Historical alias used by some SettingsHub revisions.
function interface:SetVisibility(visible)
	return self:SetVisible(visible)
end

function interface:ToggleVisibility()
	return self:SetVisible(not self:GetVisibility())
end

function interface:GetVisibility()
	local visible, hasActual = queryBackendVisibility()
	if hasActual then state.Visible = visible end

	if backendReady then
		local guiVisible = queryLegacyGuiVisibility()
		if guiVisible ~= nil then state.Visible = guiVisible end
	end
	return state.Visible
end

function interface:FocusChatBar()
	local found, success, result = invokeBackend("FocusChatBar")
	if not found or not success then return false end
	-- Historical implementations may return nil on successful focus.
	return result ~= false
end

function interface:GetMessageCount()
	local found, success, count = invokeBackend("GetMessageCount")
	if found and success and type(count) == "number" then return count end
	return 0
end

function interface:TopbarEnabledChanged(...)
	local found, success, result = invokeBackend("TopbarEnabledChanged", ...)
	return found and success and result ~= false
end

function interface:IsFocused(useWasFocused)
	local found, success, focused = invokeBackend("IsFocused", useWasFocused)
	return found and success and type(focused) == "boolean" and focused or false
end

function interface:ClassicChatEnabled()
	local found, success, value = invokeBackend("ClassicChatEnabled")
	if found and success and type(value) == "boolean" then return value end
	return ClassicChatEnabled
end

function interface:IsBubbleChatOnly()
	local found, success, value = invokeBackend("IsBubbleChatOnly")
	if found and success and type(value) == "boolean" then return value end
	return BubbleChatEnabled and not ClassicChatEnabled
end

function interface:IsDisabled()
	local found, success, value = invokeBackend("IsDisabled")
	if found and success and type(value) == "boolean" then return value end
	return not (ClassicChatEnabled or BubbleChatEnabled)
end

function interface:SendMessage(text, channelName, metadata)
	local found, success, result, detail = invokeBackend(
		"SendMessage", text, channelName, metadata
	)
	if found then return success and result ~= false, detail or result end
	return false, "Chat.lua has not finished initializing."
end

function interface:DisplaySystemMessage(text, channelName, metadata)
	local found, success, result, detail = invokeBackend(
		"DisplaySystemMessage", text, channelName, metadata
	)
	if found then return success and result ~= false, detail or result end

	-- The frontend can still display ordinary legacy system messages without
	-- requiring NewChat from the selector.
	local fallbackOptions = type(metadata) == "table" and metadata or nil
	local addFound, addSuccess, addResult = invokeBackend(
		"AddSystemChatMessage", text, false, fallbackOptions
	)
	if addFound and addSuccess then return addResult ~= false, addResult end
	return false, "Chat.lua cannot display the requested system message."
end

function interface:SetCore(name, value)
	local found, success, result = invokeBackend("SetCore", name, value)
	return found and success and result ~= false
end

function interface:GetCore(name, ...)
	local found, success, result = invokeBackend("GetCore", name, ...)
	if found and success then return result end
	return nil
end

function interface:GetChatGui()
	local found, success, result = invokeBackend("GetChatGui")
	if found and success then return result end
	return nil
end

function interface:GetChatWindowGui()
	local found, success, result = invokeBackend("GetChatWindowGui")
	if found and success then return result end
	return nil
end

function interface:GetChatBarTextBox()
	local found, success, result = invokeBackend("GetChatBarTextBox")
	if found and success then return result end
	return nil
end

--==============================================================
-- CORE API COMPATIBILITY
--==============================================================

local function tryRegisterSetCore(name, callback)
	local ok, err = pcall(function()
		StarterGui:RegisterSetCore(name, callback)
	end)
	if not ok then
		warnScript("RegisterSetCore(" .. name .. ") unavailable: " .. tostring(err))
	end
	return ok
end

local function tryRegisterGetCore(name, callback)
	local ok, err = pcall(function()
		StarterGui:RegisterGetCore(name, callback)
	end)
	if not ok then
		warnScript("RegisterGetCore(" .. name .. ") unavailable: " .. tostring(err))
	end
	return ok
end

local function queueSystemMessage(data)
	if #queuedSystemMessages &gt;= MAX_QUEUED_SYSTEM_MESSAGES then
		table.remove(queuedSystemMessages, 1)
		warnScript("System-message queue full; dropped its oldest entry.")
	end
	table.insert(queuedSystemMessages, data)
end

local function deliverSystemMessage(data)
	if not backendReady or not backend then
		queueSystemMessage(data)
		return
	end

	local found, success = invokeBackend("SetCore", "ChatMakeSystemMessage", data)
	if not found or not success then
		-- Chat.lua can render this as a developer system message even if NewChat
		-- has not registered the historical SetCore handler yet.
		local addFound, addSuccess = invokeBackend("AddSystemChatMessage", data, false)
		if not addFound or not addSuccess then queueSystemMessage(data) end
	end
end

local function flushSystemMessages()
	if not backendReady then return end
	local pending = queuedSystemMessages
	queuedSystemMessages = {}
	for _, data in ipairs(pending) do deliverSystemMessage(data) end
end

tryRegisterSetCore("ChatMakeSystemMessage", deliverSystemMessage)
tryRegisterSetCore("ChatActive", function(visible) interface:SetVisible(visible) end)
tryRegisterGetCore("ChatActive", function() return interface:GetVisibility() end)
tryRegisterGetCore("UseNewLuaChat", function() return true end)

--==============================================================
-- CHAT FRONTEND STARTUP
--==============================================================

local function initializeBackend()
	if startupStarted then return end
	startupStarted = true

	local chatModule = Modules:FindFirstChild("Chat")
	if not chatModule or not chatModule:IsA("ModuleScript") then
		backendFailed = true
		warnScript("Modules.Chat ModuleScript is missing.")
		return
	end

	log("Starting 2016-style frontend: " .. chatModule:GetFullName())

	local ok, result = xpcall(function()
		return require(chatModule)
	end, traceback)

	if not ok then
		backendFailed = true
		warnScript("Chat module failed:\n" .. tostring(result))
		return
	end
	if type(result) ~= "table" then
		backendFailed = true
		warnScript("Chat must return an API table; received " .. typeof(result))
		return
	end

	backend = result
	backendReady = true
	connectBackendSignals()

	-- Apply the last visibility request made while Chat.lua was loading.
	invokeBackend("SetVisible", state.Visible)
	flushSystemMessages()

	log("Chat.lua compatibility API initialized.")
	log("NewChat is accessed only internally by Chat.lua.")
end

-- The selector can return immediately while the frontend finishes loading.
task.spawn(initializeBackend)

--==============================================================
-- READ-ONLY NATIVE CHAT STATE
--==============================================================

local function refreshNativeChatState()
	local ok, enabled = pcall(function()
		return StarterGui:GetCoreGuiEnabled(Enum.CoreGuiType.Chat)
	end)
	if ok and type(enabled) == "boolean" then
		interface.NativeChatEnabled = enabled
	end
end

refreshNativeChatState()

local changedOK, changedSignal = pcall(function()
	return StarterGui.CoreGuiChangedSignal
end)
if changedOK and changedSignal then
	local connection = connectSignal(changedSignal, function(coreGuiType, enabled)
		if coreGuiType == Enum.CoreGuiType.Chat then
			interface.NativeChatEnabled = enabled == true
		elseif coreGuiType == Enum.CoreGuiType.All then
			refreshNativeChatState()
		end
	end)
	if connection then table.insert(backendConnections, connection) end
end

--==============================================================
-- STATUS / RETURN
--==============================================================

function interface:GetInitializationStatus()
	if backendReady then return "Ready" end
	if backendFailed then return "Failed" end
	return "Initializing"
end

log("Chat selector initialized; target=Modules.Chat.")
return interface

                                <int64 name="SourceAssetId">-339</int64>
                                <BinaryString name="Tags" />
                            </Properties>
                        </Item>
                        <Item class="ModuleScript" referent="RBX417">
                            <Properties>

                                </ContentId>

                                <string name="ScriptGuid">{A9E0E7FA-13AD-4CF5-BAD6-5BD130DFABAD}</string>
                                <BinaryString name="SerializedOverrides" />
                                <ProtectedString name="Source">--[[
    FileName: NewChat.lua
    Version: 2.1.0 - Chat.lua frontend bridge hardening

    Intended hierarchy:
        CoreGui.NewRobloxGui.Modules.NewChat
        CoreGui.NewRobloxGui.Modules.Chat

    NewChat owns TextChatService transport and the historical compatibility API.
    Chat.lua owns the legacy-rendered chat window and chat bar. The renderer
    registers its BindableEvents/BindableFunctions using RegisterCoreGuiConnections.

    This module does not create a GUI, alter native CoreGui flags, use
    Players:Chat(), or modify TextChatService's visual configuration.
]]

local TextChatService = game:GetService("TextChatService")
local StarterGui = game:GetService("StarterGui")
local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
local CoreGui = game:GetService("CoreGui")

assert(RunService:IsClient(), "[NewChat] This module must run on the client.")

local Modules = script.Parent
local RobloxGui = Modules and Modules.Parent
assert(RobloxGui and RobloxGui.Name == "NewRobloxGui" and RobloxGui.Parent == CoreGui,
	"[NewChat] Expected CoreGui.NewRobloxGui.Modules.NewChat.")

local function log(message)
	print("[NewChat] " .. tostring(message))
end

local function warnChat(message)
	warn("[NewChat] " .. tostring(message))
end

local function traceError(message)
	if debug and debug.traceback then
		return debug.traceback(tostring(message), 2)
	end
	return tostring(message)
end

local unpackValues = table.unpack or unpack

-- Lightweight signal with both modern and historical spellings.
local function createSignal()
	local listeners = {}
	local signal = {}

	function signal:Connect(callback)
		assert(type(callback) == "function", "Signal callback must be a function")
		local entry = { Callback = callback, Connected = true }
		local connection = {}
		function connection:Disconnect()
			entry.Connected = false
		end
		connection.disconnect = connection.Disconnect
		table.insert(listeners, entry)
		return connection
	end
	signal.connect = signal.Connect

	function signal:Fire(...)
		local arguments = table.pack(...)
		for _, entry in ipairs(listeners) do
			if entry.Connected then
				local ok, err = xpcall(function()
					entry.Callback(unpackValues(arguments, 1, arguments.n))
				end, traceError)
				if not ok then
					warnChat("Signal callback failed: " .. tostring(err))
				end
			end
		end
	end
	signal.fire = signal.Fire
	return signal
end

local function fireSignal(signal, ...)
	if not signal then return end
	local callback = signal.Fire or signal.fire
	if type(callback) ~= "function" then return end
	local ok, err = pcall(callback, signal, ...)
	if not ok then
		warnChat("Could not fire signal: " .. tostring(err))
	end
end

local function disconnectAll(connections)
	for _, connection in ipairs(connections) do
		pcall(function() connection:Disconnect() end)
	end
	table.clear(connections)
end

local function findInCollectionByKeyAndType(collection, key, className)
	if type(collection) ~= "table" then return nil end
	local instance = collection[key]
	if typeof(instance) == "Instance" and instance:IsA(className) then
		return instance
	end
	return nil
end

-- ------------------------------------------------------------
-- TextChannel discovery
-- ------------------------------------------------------------

local function getTextChannelsFolder()
	local folder = TextChatService:FindFirstChild("TextChannels")
	return folder and folder:IsA("Folder") and folder or nil
end

local function getTextChannels()
	local channels, seen = {}, {}
	local function addChannel(instance)
		if instance:IsA("TextChannel") and not seen[instance] then
			seen[instance] = true
			table.insert(channels, instance)
		end
	end

	local folder = getTextChannelsFolder()
	if folder then
		for _, child in ipairs(folder:GetChildren()) do addChannel(child) end
	end
	for _, child in ipairs(TextChatService:GetChildren()) do addChannel(child) end
	return channels
end

local function findTextChannel(channelName)
	if typeof(channelName) == "Instance" then
		if channelName:IsA("TextChannel") and channelName:IsDescendantOf(TextChatService) then
			return channelName
		end
		return nil
	end

	local folder = getTextChannelsFolder()
	if type(channelName) == "string" then
		local channel = folder and folder:FindFirstChild(channelName)
		if channel and channel:IsA("TextChannel") then return channel end
		channel = TextChatService:FindFirstChild(channelName)
		if channel and channel:IsA("TextChannel") then return channel end
		return nil
	end

	local general = findTextChannel("RBXGeneral")
	if general then return general end
	return getTextChannels()[1]
end

local function hasModernTextChatBackend()
	if #getTextChannels() &gt; 0 then return true end
	local ok, createDefaults = pcall(function()
		return TextChatService.CreateDefaultTextChannels
	end)
	return ok and createDefaults == true
end

local function getBubbleChatEnabled()
	local configuration = TextChatService:FindFirstChild("BubbleChatConfiguration")
	if not configuration then return false end
	local ok, enabled = pcall(function() return configuration.Enabled end)
	return ok and enabled == true
end

-- ------------------------------------------------------------
-- Compatibility API and state
-- ------------------------------------------------------------

local moduleApiTable = {}
local ChatWindowState = {
	Visible = true,
	MessageCount = 0,
	TopbarEnabled = true,
	CoreGuiEnabled = true,
	ChatBarDisabled = false,
}

local communicationsConnections = {
	ChatWindow = {},
	SetCore = {},
	GetCore = {},
}
local frontendConnections = {}
local runtimeConnections = {}
local SetCoreCache = {}
local ChatTypesSet = false
local BubbleChatEnabled = getBubbleChatEnabled()
local MAX_SETCORE_QUEUE = 100

moduleApiTable.ChatBarFocusChanged = createSignal()
moduleApiTable.VisibilityStateChanged = createSignal()
moduleApiTable.MessagesChanged = createSignal()
moduleApiTable.BubbleChatOnlySet = createSignal()
moduleApiTable.ChatDisabled = createSignal()
moduleApiTable.IncomingMessage = createSignal()
moduleApiTable.OutgoingMessage = createSignal()
moduleApiTable.OutgoingMessageFailed = createSignal()
moduleApiTable.SpecialKeyPressed = createSignal()
moduleApiTable.TextChannelsChanged = createSignal()

function moduleApiTable:ClassicChatEnabled()
	-- Historical callers use this to decide whether the custom Chat.lua
	-- renderer has a transport behind it; TextChatService supplies that transport.
	return hasModernTextChatBackend()
end

function moduleApiTable:IsBubbleChatOnly()
	-- The custom Chat.lua renderer is available whenever TextChatService has
	-- channels. BubbleChatConfiguration being enabled alone does not mean that
	-- the legacy text window should disappear.
	return getBubbleChatEnabled() and not hasModernTextChatBackend()
end

function moduleApiTable:IsDisabled()
	return not hasModernTextChatBackend() and not getBubbleChatEnabled()
end

local function setInitialChatTypes(chatTypesTable)
	if ChatTypesSet then return end
	ChatTypesSet = true
	BubbleChatEnabled = getBubbleChatEnabled()
	if not TextChatService:FindFirstChild("BubbleChatConfiguration")
		and type(chatTypesTable) == "table"
		and type(chatTypesTable.BubbleChatEnabled) == "boolean" then
		BubbleChatEnabled = chatTypesTable.BubbleChatEnabled
	end

	if not hasModernTextChatBackend() and not BubbleChatEnabled then
		fireSignal(moduleApiTable.ChatDisabled)
	elseif BubbleChatEnabled and not hasModernTextChatBackend() then
		fireSignal(moduleApiTable.BubbleChatOnlySet)
	end
end

local function dispatchEvent(eventName, ...)
	local event = findInCollectionByKeyAndType(
		communicationsConnections.ChatWindow, eventName, "BindableEvent")
	if not event then return false end

	local arguments = table.pack(...)
	local ok, err = pcall(function()
		event:Fire(unpackValues(arguments, 1, arguments.n))
	end)
	if not ok then
		warnChat("UI event " .. eventName .. " failed: " .. tostring(err))
		return false
	end
	return true
end

local function attemptInvokeFunction(functionName, ...)
	local callback = findInCollectionByKeyAndType(
		communicationsConnections.ChatWindow, functionName, "BindableFunction")
	if not callback then return false, nil end

	local arguments = table.pack(...)
	local results = table.pack(xpcall(function()
		return callback:Invoke(unpackValues(arguments, 1, arguments.n))
	end, traceError))
	if not results[1] then
		warnChat("UI query " .. functionName .. " failed: " .. tostring(results[2]))
		return false, nil
	end
	return true, unpackValues(results, 2, results.n)
end

function moduleApiTable:ToggleVisibility()
	local visible = not self:GetVisibility()
	ChatWindowState.Visible = visible
	-- Preserve the historical ToggleVisibility bindable for older callers.
	-- If this frontend exposes only SetVisible, use that as a compatibility fallback.
	if not dispatchEvent("ToggleVisibility") then
		if not dispatchEvent("SetVisible", visible) then
			fireSignal(self.VisibilityStateChanged, visible)
		end
	end
end

function moduleApiTable:SetVisible(visible)
	if type(visible) ~= "boolean" then
		warnChat("SetVisible expects a boolean.")
		return false
	end
	ChatWindowState.Visible = visible
	if not dispatchEvent("SetVisible", visible) then
		fireSignal(self.VisibilityStateChanged, visible)
	end
	return true
end

function moduleApiTable:FocusChatBar()
	dispatchEvent("FocusChatBar")
end

function moduleApiTable:GetVisibility()
	local ok, value = attemptInvokeFunction("GetVisibility")
	if ok and type(value) == "boolean" then
		ChatWindowState.Visible = value
	end
	return ChatWindowState.Visible
end

function moduleApiTable:GetMessageCount()
	local ok, value = attemptInvokeFunction("GetMessageCount")
	if ok and type(value) == "number" then return value end
	return ChatWindowState.MessageCount
end

function moduleApiTable:TopbarEnabledChanged(enabled)
	if type(enabled) == "boolean" then
		ChatWindowState.TopbarEnabled = enabled
	end
	dispatchEvent("TopbarEnabledChanged", ChatWindowState.TopbarEnabled)
end

function moduleApiTable:IsFocused(useWasFocused)
	local ok, value = attemptInvokeFunction("IsFocused", useWasFocused)
	return ok and type(value) == "boolean" and value or false
end

-- ------------------------------------------------------------
-- TextChatService message send/display API
-- ------------------------------------------------------------

function moduleApiTable:SendMessage(text, channelName, metadata)
	if type(text) ~= "string" or text:match("^%s*$") then
		return false, "Message must be a non-empty string."
	end
	local channel = findTextChannel(channelName)
	if not channel then return false, "No TextChannel is currently available." end
	if metadata ~= nil and type(metadata) ~= "string" then
		return false, "Metadata must be a string or nil."
	end

	-- Do not block the ChatBar FocusLost/input callback while Roblox
	-- performs filtering and transport. Queue SendAsync and return immediately.
	task.spawn(function()
		local ok, result = pcall(function()
			if metadata ~= nil then return channel:SendAsync(text, metadata) end
			return channel:SendAsync(text)
		end)
		if not ok then
			warnChat("TextChannel:SendAsync failed: " .. tostring(result))
			fireSignal(moduleApiTable.OutgoingMessageFailed, result, text)
		elseif result and result.Status and result.Status ~= Enum.TextChatMessageStatus.Success
			and result.Status ~= Enum.TextChatMessageStatus.Sending then
			warnChat("TextChatService rejected the outgoing message: " .. tostring(result.Status))
			fireSignal(moduleApiTable.OutgoingMessageFailed, result.Status, text)
		end
	end)
	return true, "queued"
end

function moduleApiTable:DisplaySystemMessage(text, channelName, metadata)
	if type(text) ~= "string" or text == "" then
		return false, "System message must be a non-empty string."
	end
	local channel = findTextChannel(channelName)
	if not channel then return false, "No TextChannel is currently available." end

	local ok, result = pcall(function()
		return channel:DisplaySystemMessage(text, metadata)
	end)
	if not ok then
		warnChat("TextChannel:DisplaySystemMessage failed: " .. tostring(result))
		return false, tostring(result)
	end
	return true, result
end

function moduleApiTable:GetTextChannels()
	return getTextChannels()
end

function moduleApiTable:GetDefaultTextChannel()
	return findTextChannel()
end

-- ------------------------------------------------------------
-- SetCore/GetCore compatibility
-- ------------------------------------------------------------

function moduleApiTable:SetCore(setCoreName, data)
	if type(setCoreName) ~= "string" then
		return false, "SetCore name must be a string."
	end
	if setCoreName == "ChatActive" then
		return self:SetVisible(data)
	end
	if setCoreName == "ChatBarDisabled" then
		if type(data) ~= "boolean" then
			return false, "ChatBarDisabled expects a boolean."
		end
		ChatWindowState.ChatBarDisabled = data
	end

	local event = findInCollectionByKeyAndType(
		communicationsConnections.SetCore, setCoreName, "BindableEvent")
	if event then
		event:Fire(data)
		return true
	end

	local queue = SetCoreCache[setCoreName]
	if not queue then
		queue = {}
		SetCoreCache[setCoreName] = queue
	end
	if #queue &gt;= MAX_SETCORE_QUEUE then table.remove(queue, 1) end
	table.insert(queue, data)
	return false, "No connected UI handler; value was queued."
end

function moduleApiTable:GetCore(getCoreName, ...)
	if type(getCoreName) ~= "string" then return nil end
	if getCoreName == "ChatActive" then return self:GetVisibility() end
	if getCoreName == "UseNewLuaChat" then return hasModernTextChatBackend() end
	if getCoreName == "ChatBarDisabled" then
		local ok, result = attemptInvokeFunction(getCoreName, ...)
		if ok then return result end
		return ChatWindowState.ChatBarDisabled
	end

	local callback = findInCollectionByKeyAndType(
		communicationsConnections.GetCore, getCoreName, "BindableFunction")
	if not callback then return nil end
	local arguments = table.pack(...)
	local ok, result = pcall(function()
		return callback:Invoke(unpackValues(arguments, 1, arguments.n))
	end)
	if ok then return result end
	warnChat("GetCore handler " .. getCoreName .. " failed: " .. tostring(result))
	return nil
end

-- ------------------------------------------------------------
-- Chat.lua frontend registration
-- ------------------------------------------------------------

function moduleApiTable:RegisterCoreGuiConnections(containerTable)
	if type(containerTable) ~= "table" or type(containerTable.ChatWindow) ~= "table" then
		warnChat("RegisterCoreGuiConnections expects { ChatWindow = table, SetCore? = table, GetCore? = table }.")
		return false
	end
	if containerTable.SetCore ~= nil and type(containerTable.SetCore) ~= "table" then
		warnChat("SetCore must be a table when supplied.")
		return false
	end
	if containerTable.GetCore ~= nil and type(containerTable.GetCore) ~= "table" then
		warnChat("GetCore must be a table when supplied.")
		return false
	end

	disconnectAll(frontendConnections)
	communicationsConnections.ChatWindow = {}
	communicationsConnections.SetCore = {}
	communicationsConnections.GetCore = {}

	local chatWindow = containerTable.ChatWindow
	local setCore = containerTable.SetCore or {}
	local getCore = containerTable.GetCore or {}

	for _, name in ipairs({
		"ToggleVisibility", "SetVisible", "FocusChatBar", "TopbarEnabledChanged",
		"SpecialKeyPressed", "CoreGuiEnabled",
		}) do
		communicationsConnections.ChatWindow[name] =
			findInCollectionByKeyAndType(chatWindow, name, "BindableEvent")
	end
	for _, name in ipairs({ "GetVisibility", "GetMessageCount", "IsFocused" }) do
		communicationsConnections.ChatWindow[name] =
			findInCollectionByKeyAndType(chatWindow, name, "BindableFunction")
	end

	-- The registered Chat.lua renderer reports changes back through BindableEvents.
	for _, name in ipairs({ "ChatBarFocusChanged", "VisibilityStateChanged" }) do
		local bindable = findInCollectionByKeyAndType(chatWindow, name, "BindableEvent")
		if bindable then
			table.insert(frontendConnections, bindable.Event:Connect(function(...)
				if name == "VisibilityStateChanged" then
					local visible = ...
					if type(visible) == "boolean" then ChatWindowState.Visible = visible end
				end
				fireSignal(moduleApiTable[name], ...)
			end))
		end
	end

	local messagePosted = findInCollectionByKeyAndType(chatWindow, "MessagePosted", "BindableEvent")
	if messagePosted then
		table.insert(frontendConnections, messagePosted.Event:Connect(function(message)
			local text = type(message) == "table" and message.Text or message
			if type(text) ~= "string" then
				warnChat("Ignored MessagePosted payload that was not text.")
				return
			end
			task.spawn(function()
				local sent, err = moduleApiTable:SendMessage(text)
				if not sent then warnChat("Could not send posted message: " .. tostring(err)) end
			end)
		end))
	end

	setInitialChatTypes(chatWindow.ChatTypes or {})

	for _, name in ipairs({ "ChatMakeSystemMessage", "ChatWindowPosition", "ChatWindowSize", "ChatBarDisabled" }) do
		local event = findInCollectionByKeyAndType(setCore, name, "BindableEvent")
		if event then
			communicationsConnections.SetCore[name] = event
			local queue = SetCoreCache[name]
			if queue then
				for _, data in ipairs(queue) do
					local ok, err = pcall(function() event:Fire(data) end)
					if not ok then warnChat("Could not replay " .. name .. ": " .. tostring(err)) end
				end
				SetCoreCache[name] = nil
			end
		end
	end

	for _, name in ipairs({ "ChatWindowPosition", "ChatWindowSize", "ChatBarDisabled" }) do
		communicationsConnections.GetCore[name] =
			findInCollectionByKeyAndType(getCore, name, "BindableFunction")
	end

	-- Do not force CoreGui enablement. This only synchronizes current state with Chat.lua.
	dispatchEvent("SetVisible", ChatWindowState.Visible)
	dispatchEvent("TopbarEnabledChanged", ChatWindowState.TopbarEnabled)
	dispatchEvent("CoreGuiEnabled", ChatWindowState.CoreGuiEnabled)

	log("Chat.lua historical frontend registered.")
	return true
end
moduleApiTable.RegisterFrontend = moduleApiTable.RegisterCoreGuiConnections

-- ------------------------------------------------------------
-- Passive CoreGui state (read-only)
-- ------------------------------------------------------------

local function readChatCoreGuiEnabled(fallback)
	local ok, enabled = pcall(function()
		return StarterGui:GetCoreGuiEnabled(Enum.CoreGuiType.Chat)
	end)
	if ok and type(enabled) == "boolean" then return enabled end
	return fallback
end

ChatWindowState.CoreGuiEnabled = readChatCoreGuiEnabled(ChatWindowState.CoreGuiEnabled)

local coreGuiSignalOK, coreGuiChangedSignal = pcall(function()
	return StarterGui.CoreGuiChangedSignal
end)
if coreGuiSignalOK and coreGuiChangedSignal then
	local ok, connection = pcall(function()
		return coreGuiChangedSignal:Connect(function(coreGuiType, enabledNow)
			if coreGuiType == Enum.CoreGuiType.Chat then
				ChatWindowState.CoreGuiEnabled = enabledNow == true
			elseif coreGuiType == Enum.CoreGuiType.All then
				-- All=false can simply mean some OTHER CoreGui is disabled.
				-- Read Chat itself instead of incorrectly hiding this frontend.
				ChatWindowState.CoreGuiEnabled = readChatCoreGuiEnabled(ChatWindowState.CoreGuiEnabled)
			else
				return
			end
			dispatchEvent("CoreGuiEnabled", ChatWindowState.CoreGuiEnabled)
		end)
	end)
	if ok and connection then table.insert(runtimeConnections, connection) end
end

-- Physical '/' is owned exclusively by the matching Topbar ContextActionService binding.
-- This module does not observe/re-dispatch the key because InputBegan cannot Sink it.

-- ------------------------------------------------------------
-- TextChatService incoming/outgoing message events
-- ------------------------------------------------------------

do
	local ok, connection = pcall(function()
		return TextChatService.MessageReceived:Connect(function(message)
			if not message then return end
			-- Avoid adding messages Roblox explicitly marked as failed to the old renderer.
			local statusOK, status = pcall(function() return message.Status end)
			if statusOK and status and Enum.TextChatMessageStatus
				and status ~= Enum.TextChatMessageStatus.Success then
				return
			end
			ChatWindowState.MessageCount += 1
			fireSignal(moduleApiTable.IncomingMessage, message)
			fireSignal(moduleApiTable.MessagesChanged, message, ChatWindowState.MessageCount)
		end)
	end)
	if ok and connection then
		table.insert(runtimeConnections, connection)
	else
		warnChat("Could not connect TextChatService.MessageReceived.")
	end
end

do
	local ok, connection = pcall(function()
		return TextChatService.SendingMessage:Connect(function(message)
			fireSignal(moduleApiTable.OutgoingMessage, message)
		end)
	end)
	if ok and connection then table.insert(runtimeConnections, connection) end
end

-- ------------------------------------------------------------
-- TextChannel lifecycle (including folders created after this module starts)
-- ------------------------------------------------------------

local folderConnections = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
local function onChannelsChanged()
	fireSignal(moduleApiTable.TextChannelsChanged, getTextChannels())
end

local function connectTextChannelsFolder(folder)
	if not folder or not folder:IsA("Folder") or folderConnections[folder] then return end
	folderConnections[folder] = true
	table.insert(runtimeConnections, folder.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
		if child:IsA("TextChannel") then onChannelsChanged() end
	end))
	table.insert(runtimeConnections, folder.ChildRemoved:Connect(function(child)
		if child:IsA("TextChannel") then onChannelsChanged() end
	end))
end

connectTextChannelsFolder(getTextChannelsFolder())
table.insert(runtimeConnections, TextChatService.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
	if child:IsA("TextChannel") then
		onChannelsChanged()
	elseif child.Name == "TextChannels" and child:IsA("Folder") then
		connectTextChannelsFolder(child)
		onChannelsChanged()
	end
end))
table.insert(runtimeConnections, TextChatService.ChildRemoved:Connect(function(child)
	if child:IsA("TextChannel") or child.Name == "TextChannels" then onChannelsChanged() end
end))

-- ------------------------------------------------------------
-- Cleanup
-- ------------------------------------------------------------

function moduleApiTable:Disconnect()
	disconnectAll(frontendConnections)
	disconnectAll(runtimeConnections)
	communicationsConnections.ChatWindow = {}
	communicationsConnections.SetCore = {}
	communicationsConnections.GetCore = {}
	log("Disconnected bridge connections.")
end

end)

	warnChat("No TextChannel is currently available. This backend requires TextChatService channels and does not fall back to Players:Chat().")
end

log("TextChatService backend initialized; waiting for the Chat.lua frontend registration.")
return moduleApiTable

local function LoadLibrary(name)
	local root = CoreGui:FindFirstChild("NewRobloxGui")
	local folder = root and root:FindFirstChild("LoadLibrary")
	local moduleScript = folder and folder:FindFirstChild(name)

	if not moduleScript or not moduleScript:IsA("ModuleScript") then
		return nil
	end

	local ok, result = pcall(require, moduleScript)
	if ok then
		return result
	end

	warn("[PlayerlistModule] Could not load " .. tostring(name) .. ": " .. tostring(result))
	return nil
end

	task.wait(0.1)
end
assert(Players.LocalPlayer, "PlayerlistModule: LocalPlayer was not available within 20 seconds")

local function waitForChildBounded(parent, childName, timeout)
	local found = parent and parent:FindFirstChild(childName)
	if found then return found end
	if not parent then return nil end
	local ok, result = pcall(function() return parent:WaitForChild(childName, timeout or 10) end)
	if ok then return result end
	return nil
end

-- Follower cache
local FollowRelationships = {}
local FollowRequestInProgress = false
local LastFollowRequestTime = 0
local FOLLOW_REFRESH_INTERVAL = 15
local FOLLOWING_EXISTS_URL = "https://friends.roblox.com/v1/user/following-exists"

-- Friendship cache.
-- Friendship is queried through the built-in player API. No executor HTTP
-- bridge is used to determine whether players are friends or have requests.
local FriendStatusCache = {}
local FriendStatusRefreshInProgress = false
local LastFriendStatusRefresh = 0
local FRIEND_STATUS_REFRESH_INTERVAL = 30
local onFriendshipChanged

-- Helper functions
local function clamp(value, minimum, maximum)
	return math.max(minimum, math.min(maximum, value))
end

local function getFriendStatus(selectedPlayer)
	if not selectedPlayer or selectedPlayer == Player then
		return Enum.FriendStatus.NotFriend
	end

	local cachedStatus = FriendStatusCache[tostring(selectedPlayer.UserId)]
	if cachedStatus ~= nil then
		return cachedStatus
	end

	return Enum.FriendStatus.Unknown
end

-- The single source of friendship status: Roblox's built-in Player API.
local function readBuiltInFriendStatus(otherPlayer)
	if not otherPlayer or otherPlayer == Player then
		return Enum.FriendStatus.NotFriend
	end

	local success, status = pcall(function()
		return Player:GetFriendStatus(otherPlayer)
	end)

	if not success then
		return nil
	end

	if status == Enum.FriendStatus.Unknown
		or status == Enum.FriendStatus.NotFriend
		or status == Enum.FriendStatus.Friend
		or status == Enum.FriendStatus.FriendRequestSent
		or status == Enum.FriendStatus.FriendRequestReceived then
		return status
	end

	return nil
end

local function cacheFriendStatus(otherPlayer, status)
	if not otherPlayer or otherPlayer == Player or status == nil then
		return
	end

	local key = tostring(otherPlayer.UserId)
	local previousStatus = FriendStatusCache[key]

	FriendStatusCache[key] = status

	if previousStatus ~= status and onFriendshipChanged then
		onFriendshipChanged(otherPlayer, status)
	end
end

local function getFollowerStatus(selectedPlayer)
	if not IsFollowersEnabled or not selectedPlayer or selectedPlayer == Player then
		return nil
	end

	if selectedPlayer.UserId &lt;= 0 or Player.UserId &lt;= 0 then
		return nil
	end

	local relationship = FollowRelationships[tostring(selectedPlayer.UserId)]
	if not relationship then
		return nil
	end

	if relationship.IsFollowing and relationship.IsFollowed then
		return FOLLOWER_STATUS.MUTUAL
	elseif relationship.IsFollowed then
		return FOLLOWER_STATUS.FOLLOWER
	elseif relationship.IsFollowing then
		return FOLLOWER_STATUS.FOLLOWING
	end

	return nil
end

local function getFollowerStatusIcon(status)
	if status == FOLLOWER_STATUS.MUTUAL then
		return MUTUAL_FOLLOWING_ICON
	elseif status == FOLLOWER_STATUS.FOLLOWING then
		return FOLLOWING_ICON
	elseif status == FOLLOWER_STATUS.FOLLOWER then
		return FOLLOWER_ICON
	end

	return nil
end

local function getFriendStatusIcon(status)
	if status == Enum.FriendStatus.Unknown or status == Enum.FriendStatus.NotFriend then
		return nil
	elseif status == Enum.FriendStatus.Friend then
		return FRIEND_ICON
	elseif status == Enum.FriendStatus.FriendRequestSent then
		return FRIEND_REQUEST_ICON
	elseif status == Enum.FriendStatus.FriendRequestReceived then
		return FRIEND_RECEIVED_ICON
	end

	return nil
end

local function getAdminIcon(player)
	local userId = tostring(player.UserId)

	if ADMINS[userId] then
		return nil
	end

	local success, result = pcall(function()
		return player:IsInGroup(1200769)
	end)

	if not success then
		warn("[PlayerlistModule] getAdminIcon failed: " .. tostring(result))
		return nil
	end

	if result then
		return ADMIN_ICON
	end

	return nil
end

local function setAvatarIconAsync(player, iconImage)
	local useSubdomains = false

	pcall(function()
		useSubdomains = settings():GetFFlag("UseNewSubdomainsInCoreScripts")
	end)

	local thumbsUrl = BaseUrl
	if useSubdomains and AssetGameUrl then
		thumbsUrl = AssetGameUrl
	end

	local thumbnailLoader = nil

	pcall(function()
		thumbnailLoader = require(NewRobloxGui.Modules.ThumbnailLoader)
	end)

	local loaded = false

	if thumbnailLoader then
		pcall(function()
			local loader = thumbnailLoader:Create(
				iconImage,
				player.UserId,
				thumbnailLoader.Sizes.Small,
				thumbnailLoader.AssetType.Avatar,
				true
			)
			loaded = loader:LoadAsync(false, true, nil)
		end)
	end

	if not loaded then
		iconImage.Image = "rbxasset://textures/ui/Shell/Icons/DefaultProfileIcon.png"
	end
end

local function getMembershipIcon(player)
	if isTenFootInterface then
		return ""
	end

	local isBlocked = false
	if blockingUtility then
		pcall(function() isBlocked = blockingUtility:IsPlayerBlockedByUserId(player.UserId) == true end)
	end
	if isBlocked then
		return BLOCKED_ICON
	end

	local userId = tostring(player.UserId)
	local membershipType = player.MembershipType

	if ADMINS[userId] then
		return ADMINS[userId]
	elseif player.UserId == game.CreatorId and game.CreatorType == Enum.CreatorType.User then
		return PLACE_OWNER_ICON
	elseif membershipType == Enum.MembershipType.None then
		return ""
	elseif membershipType == Enum.MembershipType.BuildersClub then
		return BC_ICON
	elseif membershipType == Enum.MembershipType.TurboBuildersClub then
		return TBC_ICON
	elseif membershipType == Enum.MembershipType.OutrageousBuildersClub then
		return OBC_ICON
	end

	return ""
end

local function isValidStat(object)
	return object:IsA("StringValue")
		or object:IsA("IntValue")
		or object:IsA("BoolValue")
		or object:IsA("NumberValue")
		or object:IsA("DoubleConstrainedValue")
		or object:IsA("IntConstrainedValue")
end

local function sortPlayerEntries(a, b)
	if a.PrimaryStat == b.PrimaryStat then
		return a.Player.Name:upper() &lt; b.Player.Name:upper()
	end

	if not a.PrimaryStat then
		return false
	end

	if not b.PrimaryStat then
		return true
	end

	return a.PrimaryStat &gt; b.PrimaryStat
end

local function sortLeaderStats(a, b)
	if a.IsPrimary ~= b.IsPrimary then
		return a.IsPrimary
	end

	if a.Priority == b.Priority then
		return a.AddId &lt; b.AddId
	end

	if typeof(a.Priority) == "Instance" then
		return a.Priority.Value &lt; b.Priority.Value
	end

	return a.Priority &lt; b.Priority
end

local function sortTeams(a, b)
	if a.TeamScore == b.TeamScore then
		return a.Id &lt; b.Id
	end

	if not a.TeamScore then
		return false
	end

	if not b.TeamScore then
		return true
	end

	return a.TeamScore &lt; b.TeamScore
end
