                                <BinaryString name="SerializedOverrides" />
                                <ProtectedString name="Source">--[[
    Filename: PlayerPermissionsModule.lua
    Yeku compatibility patch: safe group/rank lookups and per-user caches.
    This module does not depend on CoreGui.RobloxGui.
]]

local PlayerPermissionsModule = {}

local function makePlayerCacheLookup(callback)
	local cache = {}
	return function(player)
		if not player or typeof(player) ~= "Instance" or not player:IsA("Player") then
			return false
		end
		local userId = player.UserId
		if type(userId) ~= "number" or userId &lt;= 0 then
			return false
		end
		local cached = cache[userId]
		if cached ~= nil then
			return cached
		end
		local ok, value = pcall(callback, player)
		value = ok and value == true or false
		cache[userId] = value
		return value
	end
end

local function HasRankInGroupFunctionFactory(groupId, requiredRank)
	assert(type(groupId) == "number", "groupId must be a number")
	assert(type(requiredRank) == "number", "requiredRank must be a number")
	return makePlayerCacheLookup(function(player)
		return player:GetRankInGroup(groupId) &gt;= requiredRank
	end)
end

local function IsInGroupFunctionFactory(groupId)
	assert(type(groupId) == "number", "groupId must be a number")
	return makePlayerCacheLookup(function(player)
		return player:IsInGroup(groupId)
	end)
end

PlayerPermissionsModule.IsPlayerAdminAsync = IsInGroupFunctionFactory(1200769)
PlayerPermissionsModule.IsPlayerInternAsync = HasRankInGroupFunctionFactory(2868472, 100)
PlayerPermissionsModule.IsPlayerStarAsync = IsInGroupFunctionFactory(4199740)

return PlayerPermissionsModule

                                <ProtectedString name="Source">--[[
    Filename: PlayerDropDown.lua
    Yeku compatibility implementation for the legacy PlayerDropDown public API - No View button.

    Complete replacement module without a View popup action.
    - Follow/unfollow is dispatched through ReplicatedStorage.DoSomething.
      The executor/backend can answer GetFollowStatus and Follow/Unfollow requests later.
    - Block/unblock uses the authenticated user-blocking API.
    - Friend actions use the same Player API as Players.lua:
      LocalPlayer:GetFriendStatus and LocalPlayer.FriendStatusChanged.
    - Dropdown Hide ignores click-away requests when the pointer is inside the popup,
      so the PlayerList click-away handler cannot destroy buttons before activation.
    - A single Activated connection prevents double-running each action.
    - Button captions are rendered directly by each full-width TextButton and centered.
    - Popup width matches PlayerlistModule's 150px PopupClipFrame to prevent off-center clipping.
    - DoSomething follow requests use scalar positional arguments for the executor bridge.
    - Block-list and block/unblock HTTP detect common executor request APIs.
    - Friend-count limit checks retain the original HttpRbxApiService request path.
]]

local function resolveRobloxGui()
	local root
	pcall(function()
		root = script:FindFirstAncestor("NewRobloxGui")
	end)
	if root and root:IsA("ScreenGui") then
		return root
	end

	root = CoreGui:FindFirstChild("NewRobloxGui")
	if root and root:IsA("ScreenGui") then
		return root
	end

	root = CoreGui:FindFirstChild("RobloxGui")
	if root then
		return root
	end

	return CoreGui:WaitForChild("NewRobloxGui", 15)
		or CoreGui:WaitForChild("RobloxGui", 5)
end

local function createSignal()
	local event = Instance.new("BindableEvent")
	local signal = {}

	function signal:fire(...)
		event:Fire(...)
	end
	function signal:Fire(...)
		event:Fire(...)
	end
	function signal:connect(callback)
		return event.Event:Connect(callback)
	end
	function signal:Connect(callback)
		return event.Event:Connect(callback)
	end
	function signal:wait()
		return event.Event:Wait()
	end
	function signal:Wait()
		return event.Event:Wait()
	end
	function signal:Destroy()
		event:Destroy()
	end

	signal.Event = event.Event
	return signal
end

moduleApiTable.FollowerStatusChanged = createSignal()

-- DoSomething is the executor-facing follow bridge. A BindableEvent is created
-- when no endpoint exists. Existing BindableFunction/RemoteEvent/RemoteFunction
-- instances are also supported, so the executor can own the endpoint type.
--
-- Requests sent by this module:
--   { Source="YekuPlayerDropDown", Channel="YekuFollow",
--     Action="GetFollowStatus" | "Follow" | "Unfollow",
--     UserId=number, UserName=string, RequestId=string }
--
-- Expected asynchronous BindableEvent/RemoteEvent response:
--   { Source="YekuFollowBackend", Channel="YekuFollow",
--     Action="FollowStatusResult", UserId=number, IsFollowing=boolean }
-- or:
--   { Source="YekuFollowBackend", Channel="YekuFollow",
--     Action="FollowResult", UserId=number, Success=boolean,
--     IsFollowing=boolean, Error=string? }
--
-- The executor-side backend is intentionally separate. This module does not
-- query the Friends web API to determine follow state and does not pretend a
-- queued executor request has succeeded on the backend.
local DoSomething = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("DoSomething")
if not DoSomething then
	DoSomething = Instance.new("BindableEvent")
	DoSomething.Name = "DoSomething"
	DoSomething.Parent = ReplicatedStorage
end

local function setFollowButtonCaption(userId, isFollowing)
	local button = liveFollowButtons[tostring(userId)]
	if not button or not button.Parent then
		return
	end

	-- Use the TextButton's own text, not an overlay label. The button occupies
	-- the whole visible 150px row, so Roblox has no wider clipped label to center.
	button.Text = isFollowing and "Unfollow Player" or "Follow Player"
	button.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
	button.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center
	button.TextTransparency = 0
	button.TextScaled = false
	button.TextWrapped = false
end

local function findPlayerByUserId(userId)
	userId = tonumber(userId)
	if not userId then
		return nil
	end
	for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
		if player.UserId == userId then
			return player
		end
	end
	return nil
end

local function handleDoSomethingReply(source, channel, action, userId, requestId, success, isFollowing, errorMessage)
	local message
	if type(source) == "table" then
		-- Backward compatibility with the old table-payload bridge.
		message = source
	else
		message = {
			Source = source,
			Channel = channel,
			Action = action,
			UserId = userId,
			RequestId = requestId,
			Success = success,
			IsFollowing = isFollowing,
			Error = errorMessage,
		}
	end

	if message.Source ~= "YekuFollowBackend" or message.Channel ~= "YekuFollow" then
		return
	end

	local userIdNumber = tonumber(message.UserId)
	if not userIdNumber or userIdNumber &lt;= 0 then
		return
	end

	local key = tostring(userIdNumber)

	if message.Action == "FollowStatusResult" then
		-- Do not turn a failed Supabase status lookup into a false "not following" state.
		if message.Success == false then
			pendingFollowRequests[key] = nil
			warn("PlayerDropDown: follow status lookup failed for UserId " .. key
				.. ": " .. tostring(message.Error or "unknown backend error"))
			return
		end
		if type(message.IsFollowing) ~= "boolean" then
			return
		end
		recentFollowing[key] = message.IsFollowing
		pendingFollowRequests[key] = nil
		setFollowButtonCaption(userIdNumber, message.IsFollowing)

		local player = findPlayerByUserId(userIdNumber)
		if player then
			moduleApiTable.FollowerStatusChanged:fire(player, message.IsFollowing)
		end
		return
	end

	if message.Action == "FollowResult" then
		local pending = pendingFollowRequests[key]

		if message.Success == false then
			if pending and pending.PreviousState ~= nil then
				recentFollowing[key] = pending.PreviousState
				setFollowButtonCaption(userIdNumber, pending.PreviousState)
			elseif pending then
				recentFollowing[key] = nil
				setFollowButtonCaption(userIdNumber, false)
			end
			pendingFollowRequests[key] = nil

			warn("PlayerDropDown: executor follow request failed for UserId " .. key
				.. ": " .. tostring(message.Error or "no error supplied"))
			notify("Follow action failed", tostring(message.Error or "The executor backend rejected the request."), "", 5)
			return
		end

		local followingState = message.IsFollowing
		if type(followingState) ~= "boolean" and pending then
			followingState = pending.DesiredState
		end
		if type(followingState) == "boolean" then
			recentFollowing[key] = followingState
			setFollowButtonCaption(userIdNumber, followingState)

			local player = findPlayerByUserId(userIdNumber)
			if player then
				moduleApiTable.FollowerStatusChanged:fire(player, followingState)
			end
		end
		pendingFollowRequests[key] = nil
	end
end

local function connectDoSomethingReplies()
	if DoSomething:IsA("BindableEvent") then
		DoSomething.Event:Connect(handleDoSomethingReply)
	elseif DoSomething:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
		DoSomething.OnClientEvent:Connect(handleDoSomethingReply)
	end
end
connectDoSomethingReplies()

local function dispatchDoSomething(action, target, desiredState)
	if not target or not tonumber(target.UserId) or target.UserId &lt;= 0 then
		return false, "invalid target"
	end

	if not DoSomething or not DoSomething.Parent then
		return false, "ReplicatedStorage.DoSomething is missing"
	end

	local requestId = HttpService:GenerateGUID(false)
	local source = "YekuPlayerDropDown"
	local channel = "YekuFollow"
	local userId = target.UserId
	local userName = target.Name

	-- IMPORTANT: the executor bridge accepts scalar positional arguments.
	-- Do not pass a table payload to DoSomething.
	local function invokeBridge()
		if desiredState ~= nil then
			if DoSomething:IsA("BindableEvent") then
				DoSomething:Fire(source, channel, action, userId, userName, requestId, desiredState)
			elseif DoSomething:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
				DoSomething:FireServer(source, channel, action, userId, userName, requestId, desiredState)
			elseif DoSomething:IsA("BindableFunction") then
				return DoSomething:Invoke(source, channel, action, userId, userName, requestId, desiredState)
			elseif DoSomething:IsA("RemoteFunction") then
				return DoSomething:InvokeServer(source, channel, action, userId, userName, requestId, desiredState)
			else
				error("unsupported DoSomething class: " .. DoSomething.ClassName)
			end
		else
			if DoSomething:IsA("BindableEvent") then
				DoSomething:Fire(source, channel, action, userId, userName, requestId)
			elseif DoSomething:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
				DoSomething:FireServer(source, channel, action, userId, userName, requestId)
			elseif DoSomething:IsA("BindableFunction") then
				return DoSomething:Invoke(source, channel, action, userId, userName, requestId)
			elseif DoSomething:IsA("RemoteFunction") then
				return DoSomething:InvokeServer(source, channel, action, userId, userName, requestId)
			else
				error("unsupported DoSomething class: " .. DoSomething.ClassName)
			end
		end
		return nil
	end

	if DoSomething:IsA("BindableFunction") or DoSomething:IsA("RemoteFunction") then
		task.spawn(function()
			local results = table.pack(pcall(invokeBridge))
			if not results[1] then
				warn("PlayerDropDown: DoSomething function failed: " .. tostring(results[2]))
				return
			end
			if results.n &gt;= 2 and results[2] ~= nil then
				handleDoSomethingReply(table.unpack(results, 2, results.n))
			end
		end)
		return true, requestId
	end

	local ok, err = pcall(invokeBridge)
	if not ok then
		return false, tostring(err)
	end
	return true, requestId
end

local function getExecutorEnvironments()
	local environments = {}
	local seen = {}
	local function add(environment)
		if type(environment) == "table" and not seen[environment] then
			seen[environment] = true
			table.insert(environments, environment)
		end
	end

	if type(getgenv) == "function" then
		local ok, environment = pcall(getgenv)
		if ok then add(environment) end
	end
	if type(getfenv) == "function" then
		local ok, environment = pcall(getfenv, 0)
		if ok then add(environment) end
	end
	add(_G)
	return environments
end

local ExecutorEnvironments = getExecutorEnvironments()
local ExecutorEnvironment = ExecutorEnvironments[1] or _G

local function findExecutorRequest()
	local paths = {
		{ "request", function(environment) return environment.request end },
		{ "http_request", function(environment) return environment.http_request end },
		{ "httprequest", function(environment) return environment.httprequest end },
		{ "http.request", function(environment) return type(environment.http) == "table" and environment.http.request end },
		{ "syn.request", function(environment) return type(environment.syn) == "table" and environment.syn.request end },
		{ "fluxus.request", function(environment) return type(environment.fluxus) == "table" and environment.fluxus.request end },
		{ "krnl.request", function(environment) return type(environment.krnl) == "table" and environment.krnl.request end },
	}
	for _, environment in ipairs(ExecutorEnvironments) do
		for _, entry in ipairs(paths) do
			local ok, callback = pcall(entry[2], environment)
			if ok and type(callback) == "function" then
				return callback, entry[1]
			end
		end
	end
	return nil, nil
end

local ExecutorRequest, ExecutorRequestName = findExecutorRequest()
local executorCsrfToken = nil

local function headerValue(headers, wantedName)
	if type(headers) ~= "table" then
		return nil
	end
	local wanted = string.lower(wantedName)
	for name, value in pairs(headers) do
		if string.lower(tostring(name)) == wanted then
			return tostring(value)
		end
	end
	return nil
end

local function decodeApiResponse(raw)
	if type(raw) == "table" then
		return raw
	end
	if type(raw) ~= "string" or raw == "" then
		return { success = true }
	end

	local ok, decoded = pcall(function()
		return HttpService:JSONDecode(raw)
	end)
	if ok and type(decoded) == "table" then
		return decoded
	end
	return { success = true, _raw = raw }
end

local function getExecutorCookieHeader()
	local value = nil
	for _, environment in ipairs(ExecutorEnvironments) do
		value = environment.YekuRobloxCookie or environment.ROBLOSECURITY or environment.RobloxCookie
		if type(value) == "string" and value ~= "" then
			break
		end
		value = nil
	end
	if type(value) ~= "string" or value == "" then
		return nil
	end
	value = value:gsub("^%s+", ""):gsub("%s+$", "")
	value = value:gsub("^Cookie:%s*", "")
	if value:match("^%.ROBLOSECURITY=") then
		return value
	end
	return ".ROBLOSECURITY=" .. value
end

local function normalizeExecutorResponse(response)
	if type(response) == "string" then
		return { StatusCode = 200, Body = response, Success = true, Headers = {} }
	end
	if type(response) ~= "table" then
		error("PlayerDropDown: " .. tostring(ExecutorRequestName or "executor request")
			.. " returned " .. typeof(response) .. " instead of a response")
	end
	return {
		StatusCode = tonumber(response.StatusCode or response.statusCode or response.status),
		Body = response.Body or response.body or "",
		Headers = response.Headers or response.headers or {},
		Success = response.Success ~= nil and response.Success or response.success,
	}
end

local function executeExecutorRequest(options)
	if type(ExecutorRequest) ~= "function" then
		error("PlayerDropDown: no compatible executor HTTP API was found (request, http_request, http.request, syn.request, fluxus.request, or krnl.request)")
	end
	local ok, response = pcall(ExecutorRequest, options)
	if not ok then
		error("PlayerDropDown: " .. tostring(ExecutorRequestName or "executor HTTP request")
			.. " failed: " .. tostring(response))
	end
	return normalizeExecutorResponse(response)
end

local function requestRobloxApi(method, url, body)
	local headers = {
		["Accept"] = "application/json",
		["Content-Type"] = "application/json",
	}
	local cookie = getExecutorCookieHeader()
	if cookie then
		headers["Cookie"] = cookie
	end
	if executorCsrfToken then
		headers["X-CSRF-TOKEN"] = executorCsrfToken
	end

	local options = {
		Url = url,
		Method = method,
		Headers = headers,
		Timeout = 20,
	}
	if method ~= "GET" and method ~= "HEAD" then
		options.Body = body or ""
	end

	local response = executeExecutorRequest(options)
	local token = headerValue(response.Headers, "x-csrf-token")
	if token and token ~= "" then
		executorCsrfToken = token
	end

	local status = tonumber(response.StatusCode)
	if status == 403 and executorCsrfToken and method ~= "GET" and method ~= "HEAD" then
		local retryHeaders = {}
		for key, value in pairs(headers) do
			retryHeaders[key] = value
		end
		retryHeaders["X-CSRF-TOKEN"] = executorCsrfToken
		local retryOptions = {
			Url = url,
			Method = method,
			Headers = retryHeaders,
			Timeout = 20,
			Body = body or "",
		}
		response = executeExecutorRequest(retryOptions)
		token = headerValue(response.Headers, "x-csrf-token")
		if token and token ~= "" then
			executorCsrfToken = token
		end
		status = tonumber(response.StatusCode)
	end

	local decoded = decodeApiResponse(response.Body)
	if not status or status &lt; 200 or status &gt;= 300 or response.Success == false then
		local detail = ""
		if type(decoded) == "table" then
			detail = tostring(decoded.message or decoded.error or decoded.errors or decoded._raw or "")
		end
		error("HTTP " .. tostring(status or "unknown")
			.. (detail ~= "" and (": " .. detail) or ""))
	end

	return decoded
end

local function apiRead(url)
	return requestRobloxApi("GET", url)
end

local function apiWrite(url, body)
	return requestRobloxApi("POST", url, body)
end

local function apiResponseSucceeded(response)
	if type(response) ~= "table" then
		return response == true
	end
	if response.success == false or response.Success == false then
		return false
	end
	if type(response.errors) == "table" and #response.errors &gt; 0 then
		return false
	end
	if type(response.error) == "string" and response.error ~= "" then
		return false
	end
	return true
end

notify = function(title, message, image, duration)
	local event = RobloxGui:FindFirstChild("SendNotificationInfo")
	if event and event:IsA("BindableEvent") then
		pcall(function()
			event:Fire({
				Title = title,
				Text = message,
				Image = image or "",
				Duration = duration or 5,
			})
		end)
	end
end

local function loadBlockedList()
	local fresh = {}
	if LocalPlayer.UserId &lt;= 0 then
		BLOCKED = fresh
		blockedListLoaded = true
		return BLOCKED
	end

	local cursor = ""
	local pageCount = 0
	local succeededAtLeastOnce = false

	while pageCount &lt; 5 do
		local url = "https://apis.roblox.com/user-blocking-api/v1/users/get-blocked-users?cursor="
			.. HttpService:UrlEncode(cursor) .. "&amp;count=50"
		local ok, result = pcall(apiRead, url)

		if not ok or type(result) ~= "table" or not apiResponseSucceeded(result) then
			if not succeededAtLeastOnce then
				warn("PlayerDropDown: blocked-user list request failed: " .. tostring(result))
			end
			break
		end
		succeededAtLeastOnce = true

		local rows = result.blockedUsers or result.blockedUserIds or result.userList or result.data
		if type(rows) == "table" then
			for _, item in pairs(rows) do
				local userId
				if type(item) == "table" then
					userId = tonumber(item.userId or item.id or item.blockeeId or item.UserId)
				else
					userId = tonumber(item)
				end
				if userId and userId &gt; 0 then
					fresh[userId] = true
				end
			end
		end

		pageCount += 1
		local nextCursor = result.nextPageCursor or result.nextCursor
		if type(nextCursor) ~= "string" or nextCursor == "" then
			break
		end
		cursor = nextCursor
	end

	if succeededAtLeastOnce then
		BLOCKED = fresh
		blockedListLoaded = true
	else
		-- A request failure is not an empty block list. Leave the loaded flag
		-- false so the next lookup can retry and never cache a false negative.
		BLOCKED = BLOCKED or {}
	end
	return BLOCKED
end

local function initializeBlockList()
	if blockListInitializationStarted then
		return
	end
	blockListInitializationStarted = true

	task.spawn(function()
		loadBlockedList()
		if FFlagHandlePlayerBlockListsInternalPermissive then
			local setEvent = RobloxReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("SetPlayerBlockList")
				or RobloxReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("SetPlayerBlockList", 15)
			if setEvent and setEvent:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
				local ids = {}
				for userId in pairs(BLOCKED) do
					table.insert(ids, userId)
				end
				pcall(function()
					setEvent:FireServer(ids)
				end)
			end
		end
	end)
end

initializeBlockList()

local function getBlockedUserIds()
	if not blockedListLoaded then
		local deadline = os.clock() + BLOCKED_TIMEOUT
		while not blockedListLoaded and os.clock() &lt; deadline do
			task.wait(0.1)
		end
	end

	local ids = {}
	for userId in pairs(BLOCKED) do
		table.insert(ids, userId)
	end
	table.sort(ids)
	return ids
end

local function isBlocked(userId)
	-- Popup construction is a UI path: never wait for HTTP/block-list loading here.
	-- initializeBlockList() populates this cache asynchronously in the background.
	userId = tonumber(userId)
	if not userId or userId &lt;= 0 then return false end
	return BLOCKED[userId] == true
end
local function isMuted(userId)
	return MUTED[tonumber(userId)] == true
end

local function BlockPlayerAsync(target)
	if not target or target == LocalPlayer or target.UserId &lt;= 0 then
		return false
	end

	local userId = target.UserId
	if BLOCKED[userId] == true then
		return true
	end

	local ok, result = pcall(
		apiWrite,
		"https://apis.roblox.com/user-blocking-api/v1/users/" .. tostring(userId) .. "/block-user",
		""
	)
	if ok and apiResponseSucceeded(result) then
		BLOCKED[userId] = true
		blockedListLoaded = true
		BlockChangedEvent:Fire(userId, true)
		if FFlagHandlePlayerBlockListsInternalPermissive and UpdateBlockListEvent then
			pcall(function()
				UpdateBlockListEvent:FireServer(userId, true)
			end)
		end
		print("PlayerDropDown: blocked UserId " .. tostring(userId))
		return true
	end

	warn("PlayerDropDown: block failed for " .. tostring(userId) .. ": " .. tostring(result))
	notify("Couldn't block player", "Roblox did not accept the block request. Try again.", "", 5)
	return false
end

local function UnblockPlayerAsync(target)
	if not target or target.UserId &lt;= 0 then
		return false
	end

	local userId = target.UserId
	if BLOCKED[userId] ~= true and blockedListLoaded then
		return true
	end

	local ok, result = pcall(
		apiWrite,
		"https://apis.roblox.com/user-blocking-api/v1/users/" .. tostring(userId) .. "/unblock-user",
		""
	)
	if ok and apiResponseSucceeded(result) then
		BLOCKED[userId] = nil
		blockedListLoaded = true
		BlockChangedEvent:Fire(userId, false)
		if FFlagHandlePlayerBlockListsInternalPermissive and UpdateBlockListEvent then
			pcall(function()
				UpdateBlockListEvent:FireServer(userId, false)
			end)
		end
		print("PlayerDropDown: unblocked UserId " .. tostring(userId))
		return true
	end

	warn("PlayerDropDown: unblock failed for " .. tostring(userId) .. ": " .. tostring(result))
	notify("Couldn't unblock player", "Roblox did not accept the unblock request. Try again.", "", 5)
	return false
end

local function MutePlayer(target)
	if target and target ~= LocalPlayer and target.UserId &gt; 0 and not isMuted(target.UserId) then
		MUTED[target.UserId] = true
		MuteChangedEvent:Fire(target.UserId, true)
	end
end

local function UnmutePlayer(target)
	if target then
		MUTED[target.UserId] = nil
		MuteChangedEvent:Fire(target.UserId, false)
	end
end

local function getFriendStatus(player)
	if not player or player == LocalPlayer then
		return Enum.FriendStatus.NotFriend
	end

	-- Match the source Players.lua CoreScript API; do not replace pending
	-- friendship states with a web request or the boolean IsFriendsWithAsync API.
	local ok, status = pcall(function()
		return LocalPlayer:GetFriendStatus(player)
	end)

	if ok and status ~= nil then
		return status
	end

	return Enum.FriendStatus.NotFriend
end

local function getFriendCountAsync(playerOrUserId)
	local userId = typeof(playerOrUserId) == "Instance"
		and playerOrUserId.UserId
		or tonumber(playerOrUserId)
	if not userId or userId &lt;= 0 then
		return nil
	end

	-- Preserve PlayerDropDown's original friend-count request path. This is
	-- only used to check the 200-friend limit before requesting friendship;
	-- actual relationship/request state still comes from GetFriendStatus.
	local ok, result = pcall(function()
		local raw = HttpRbxApiService:GetAsyncFullUrl(
			"https://friends.roblox.com/v1/users/" .. tostring(userId) .. "/friends/count",
			Enum.ThrottlingPriority.Default,
			Enum.HttpRequestType.Players
		)
		return decodeApiResponse(raw)
	end)
	if ok and type(result) == "table" and tonumber(result.count) then
		return tonumber(result.count)
	end
	return nil
end

local function canSendFriendRequestAsync(target)
	if not target or target == LocalPlayer or target.UserId &lt;= 0 then
		return false
	end

	local myCount = getFriendCountAsync(LocalPlayer.UserId)
	local theirCount = getFriendCountAsync(target.UserId)
	if myCount and myCount &gt;= MAX_FRIEND_COUNT then
		notify("Cannot send friend request", "You are at the maximum friends limit.", "", 5)
		return false
	end
	if theirCount and theirCount &gt;= MAX_FRIEND_COUNT then
		notify("Cannot send friend request", target.Name .. " is at the maximum friends limit.", "", 5)
		return false
	end
	if not myCount or not theirCount then
		-- RequestFriendship is authoritative, so an unavailable count lookup is not fatal.
		warn("PlayerDropDown: friend count unavailable; letting Roblox validate the request")
	end
	return true
end

-- Follow state belongs to the executor bridge, not the Roblox friendship API.
-- If no answer is cached, request one from DoSomething and return the current
-- local value (false until the executor backend reports the actual state).
local function isFollowing(targetUserId, followerUserId, target)
	targetUserId = tonumber(targetUserId)
	followerUserId = tonumber(followerUserId)

	if not targetUserId or targetUserId &lt;= 0 or followerUserId ~= LocalPlayer.UserId then
		return false
	end

	local key = tostring(targetUserId)
	if recentFollowing[key] ~= nil then
		return recentFollowing[key]
	end

	local now = os.clock()
	local lastRequest = followStatusRequestTimes[key] or 0
	if target and now - lastRequest &gt;= 2 then
		followStatusRequestTimes[key] = now
		local requestOk, requestError = dispatchDoSomething("GetFollowStatus", target)
		if not requestOk then
			warn("PlayerDropDown: could not dispatch GetFollowStatus for UserId "
				.. key .. ": " .. tostring(requestError))
		else
			print("PlayerDropDown: requested follow state through DoSomething for UserId " .. key)
		end
	end

	return false
end

local function setFollowing(target, shouldFollow)
	if not target or target == LocalPlayer or target.UserId &lt;= 0 then
		return false
	end

	local userId = target.UserId
	local key = tostring(userId)
	local previousState = recentFollowing[key]
	local action = shouldFollow and "Follow" or "Unfollow"

	pendingFollowRequests[key] = {
		PreviousState = previousState,
		DesiredState = shouldFollow,
		Action = action,
	}

	-- Optimistic UI state keeps the dropdown usable before the backend is
	-- implemented. A FollowResult failure from the executor rolls it back.
	recentFollowing[key] = shouldFollow
	setFollowButtonCaption(userId, shouldFollow)

	local ok, result = dispatchDoSomething(action, target, shouldFollow)
	if not ok then
		pendingFollowRequests[key] = nil
		recentFollowing[key] = previousState
		setFollowButtonCaption(userId, previousState == true)
		if previousState == nil then
			recentFollowing[key] = nil
		end
		warn("PlayerDropDown: could not dispatch " .. action .. " through DoSomething for UserId "
			.. tostring(userId) .. ": " .. tostring(result))
		notify("Follow action unavailable", "ReplicatedStorage.DoSomething could not receive the request.", "", 5)
		return false
	end

	-- A synchronous response may already have resolved the pending request.
	-- Only fire the optimistic status event while the request is still pending.
	if pendingFollowRequests[key] then
		moduleApiTable.FollowerStatusChanged:fire(target, shouldFollow)
	end

	print("PlayerDropDown: sent " .. action .. " request through DoSomething for UserId "
		.. tostring(userId) .. " (RequestId=" .. tostring(result) .. ")")

	notify(
		shouldFollow and "Follow request queued" or "Unfollow request queued",
		"Sent " .. action .. " request to the executor for " .. target.Name .. ".",
		"",
		4
	)

	return true
end

local function fireAnalytics(counter, action)
	pcall(function()
		AnalyticsService:ReportCounter(counter)
	end)
	pcall(function()
		AnalyticsService:TrackEvent("Game", action, "PlayerDropDown")
	end)
end

local function reportPlayer(target)
	if not target or target.Parent ~= Players then
		return false
	end

	local modules = RobloxGui:FindFirstChild("Modules")
	local settingsFolder = modules and modules:FindFirstChild("Settings")
	local pages = settingsFolder and settingsFolder:FindFirstChild("Pages")
	local page = pages and pages:FindFirstChild("ReportAbuseMenu")

	if not page or not page:IsA("ModuleScript") then
		warn("PlayerDropDown: ReportAbuseMenu ModuleScript is missing")
		notify("Report unavailable", "The legacy Report Abuse page could not be loaded.", "", 5)
		return false
	end

	local requireOk, reportModule = pcall(require, page)
	if not requireOk or type(reportModule) ~= "table" or type(reportModule.ReportPlayer) ~= "function" then
		warn("PlayerDropDown: ReportAbuseMenu failed to load: " .. tostring(reportModule))
		notify("Report unavailable", "The legacy Report Abuse page could not be loaded.", "", 5)
		return false
	end

	local reportOk, reportError = pcall(function()
		reportModule:ReportPlayer(target)
	end)
	if not reportOk then
		warn("PlayerDropDown: ReportPlayer failed: " .. tostring(reportError))
		return false
	end
	return true
end

-- Remember the pointer that caused InputBegan. PlayerlistModule calls :Hide() on
-- global mouse/touch input; when that same pointer is inside this popup, ignore
-- that click-away request. Button actions call forceHide() after their callbacks.
local lastPointerPosition = nil
local lastPointerAt = 0
local lastPointerWasTouch = false
UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
	local inputType = input.UserInputType
	if inputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
		lastPointerPosition = Vector2.new(input.Position.X, input.Position.Y)
		lastPointerAt = os.clock()
		lastPointerWasTouch = true
	elseif inputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
		local position = UserInputService:GetMouseLocation()
		lastPointerPosition = Vector2.new(position.X, position.Y)
		lastPointerAt = os.clock()
		lastPointerWasTouch = false
	end
end)

local function pointInsideFrame(point, frame)
	if not point or not frame or not frame.Parent then
		return false
	end
	local pos = frame.AbsolutePosition
	local size = frame.AbsoluteSize
	return point.X &gt;= pos.X and point.X &lt;= pos.X + size.X
		and point.Y &gt;= pos.Y and point.Y &lt;= pos.Y + size.Y
end

local function pointerIsInsidePopup(frame)
	if not frame or not frame.Parent then
		return false
	end

	local point = lastPointerPosition
	if lastPointerWasTouch and point and os.clock() - lastPointerAt &lt;= 0.75 then
		-- Touch input has its own screen position; don't compare it to the mouse.
		return pointInsideFrame(point, frame)
	end

	-- For mouse clicks, check the current cursor first. The cached mouse point
	-- is deliberately not used as a fallback because it may be from an older click.
	local mousePosition = UserInputService:GetMouseLocation()
	local mousePoint = Vector2.new(mousePosition.X, mousePosition.Y)
	if pointInsideFrame(mousePoint, frame) then
		return true
	end

	-- GuiService's hit-test also covers nested descendants and GuiInset differences.
	local ok, hitObjects = pcall(function()
		return GuiService:GetGuiObjectsAtPosition(mousePosition.X, mousePosition.Y)
	end)
	if ok and type(hitObjects) == "table" then
		for _, guiObject in ipairs(hitObjects) do
			if guiObject == frame or guiObject:IsDescendantOf(frame) then
				return true
			end
		end
	end

	return false
end

local function createPlayerDropDown()
	local dropdown = {
		Player = nil,
		PopupFrame = nil,
		HidePopupImmediately = false,
		PopupFrameOffScreenPosition = nil,
		HiddenSignal = createSignal(),
	}

	local function forceHide()
		if dropdown.Player then
			local oldKey = tostring(dropdown.Player.UserId)
			local oldButton = liveFollowButtons[oldKey]
			if oldButton and dropdown.PopupFrame and oldButton:IsDescendantOf(dropdown.PopupFrame) then
				liveFollowButtons[oldKey] = nil
			end
		end
		if dropdown.PopupFrame then
			dropdown.PopupFrame:Destroy()
			dropdown.PopupFrame = nil
		end
		dropdown.Player = nil
		dropdown.HiddenSignal:fire()
	end

	local function hideUnlessClickIsInsidePopup()
		if pointerIsInsidePopup(dropdown.PopupFrame) then
			return
		end
		forceHide()
	end

	function dropdown:Hide()
		hideUnlessClickIsInsidePopup()
	end

	function dropdown:ForceHide()
		forceHide()
	end

	local function addButton(list, name, caption, callback)
		table.insert(list, {
			Name = name,
			Text = caption,
			OnPress = callback,
		})
	end

	function dropdown:CreatePopup(player)
		if not player or player == LocalPlayer or player.Parent ~= Players then
			warn("PlayerDropDown: CreatePopup received an invalid player")
			return nil
		end

		if self.PopupFrame then
			forceHide()
		end
		self.Player = player

		local items = {}
		local status = getFriendStatus(player)
		local friendCaption = "Send Friend Request"
		if status == Enum.FriendStatus.Friend then
			friendCaption = "Unfriend Player"
		elseif status == Enum.FriendStatus.FriendRequestSent then
			friendCaption = "Revoke Friend Request"
		elseif status == Enum.FriendStatus.FriendRequestReceived then
			friendCaption = "Accept Friend Request"
		end

		local blocked = isBlocked(player.UserId)
		local targetUserId = player.UserId
		local targetPlayer = player


		if not blocked then
			addButton(items, "FriendButton", friendCaption, function()
				local target = targetPlayer
				if not target or target.Parent ~= Players then
					forceHide()
					return
				end

				local targetStatus = getFriendStatus(target)
				local actionOk, actionError
				if targetStatus == Enum.FriendStatus.Friend
					or targetStatus == Enum.FriendStatus.FriendRequestSent then
					actionOk, actionError = pcall(function()
						LocalPlayer:RevokeFriendship(target)
					end)
					if actionOk then
						fireAnalytics("PlayerDropDown-RevokeFriendship", "RevokeFriendship")
					end
				elseif targetStatus == Enum.FriendStatus.FriendRequestReceived then
					actionOk, actionError = pcall(function()
						LocalPlayer:RequestFriendship(target)
					end)
					if actionOk then
						fireAnalytics("PlayerDropDown-RequestFriendship", "RequestFriendship")
					end
				else
					forceHide()
					task.spawn(function()
						if canSendFriendRequestAsync(target) and target.Parent == Players then
							local requestOk, requestError = pcall(function()
								LocalPlayer:RequestFriendship(target)
							end)
							if requestOk then
								fireAnalytics("PlayerDropDown-RequestFriendship", "RequestFriendship")
								print("PlayerDropDown: friend request sent to UserId " .. tostring(target.UserId))
							else
								warn("PlayerDropDown: RequestFriendship failed: " .. tostring(requestError))
								notify("Friend request failed", "Roblox did not accept the request. Try again.", "", 5)
							end
						end
					end)
					return
				end

				if not actionOk then
					warn("PlayerDropDown: friend action failed: " .. tostring(actionError))
					notify("Friend action failed", "Roblox did not accept the friend action. Try again.", "", 5)
				end
				forceHide()
			end)
		end

		if status == Enum.FriendStatus.FriendRequestReceived and not blocked then
			addButton(items, "DeclineFriendButton", "Decline Friend Request", function()
				local target = targetPlayer
				if not target or target.Parent ~= Players then
					forceHide()
					return
				end

				local ok, err = pcall(function()
					LocalPlayer:RevokeFriendship(target)
				end)
				if not ok then
					warn("PlayerDropDown: declining friend request failed: " .. tostring(err))
					notify("Couldn't decline request", "Roblox did not accept the action. Try again.", "", 5)
				end
				forceHide()
			end)
		end

		if not blocked then
			local following = isFollowing(targetUserId, LocalPlayer.UserId, targetPlayer)
			addButton(items, "FollowerButton", following and "Unfollow Player" or "Follow Player", function()
				local target = targetPlayer
				if not target or target.Parent ~= Players then
					forceHide()
					return
				end

				local current = recentFollowing[tostring(target.UserId)]
				if current == nil then
					current = following
				end
				local shouldFollow = not current
				forceHide()
				task.spawn(function()
					setFollowing(target, shouldFollow)
				end)
			end)
		end

		addButton(items, "BlockButton", blocked and "Unblock Player" or "Block Player", function()
			local target = targetPlayer
			if not target or target.UserId &lt;= 0 then
				forceHide()
				return
			end
			forceHide()
			task.spawn(function()
				if blocked then
					UnblockPlayerAsync(target)
				else
					BlockPlayerAsync(target)
				end
			end)
		end)

		addButton(items, "ReportButton", "Report Abuse", function()
			local target = targetPlayer
			if not target or target.Parent ~= Players then
				forceHide()
				return
			end
			forceHide()
			task.defer(function()
				reportPlayer(target)
			end)
		end)

		local frame = Instance.new("Frame")
		frame.Name = "PopupFrame"
		frame.Size = UDim2.new(0, DROPDOWN_WIDTH, 0, #items * (ENTRY_HEIGHT + ENTRY_PADDING))
		frame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		frame.BorderSizePixel = 0
		frame.Active = false
		frame.ClipsDescendants = false

		for index, item in ipairs(items) do
			local button = Instance.new("TextButton")
			button.Name = item.Name
			button.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0)
			button.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, (index - 1) * (ENTRY_HEIGHT + ENTRY_PADDING))
			button.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, ENTRY_HEIGHT)
			button.BackgroundColor3 = BG_COLOR
			button.BackgroundTransparency = 0.5
			button.BorderSizePixel = 0
			button.AutoButtonColor = true
			button.Active = true
			button.Selectable = true
			button.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSans
			button.TextSize = 14
			button.TextColor3 = TEXT_COLOR
			button.TextTransparency = 0
			button.Text = tostring(item.Text or "")
			button.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
			button.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center
			button.TextWrapped = false
			button.TextScaled = false
			button.RichText = false
			button.ClipsDescendants = true
			button.ZIndex = 2
			button.Parent = frame

			-- Keep the entire row clickable; no Caption/TextLabel overlay sits
			-- above this button, so Activated reliably receives the click.
			if item.Name == "FollowerButton" then
				liveFollowButtons[tostring(targetUserId)] = button
				local knownState = recentFollowing[tostring(targetUserId)]
				if type(knownState) == "boolean" then
					setFollowButtonCaption(targetUserId, knownState)
				end
			end

			local lastActivation = -math.huge
			button.Activated:Connect(function()
				local now = os.clock()
				if now - lastActivation &lt; 0.25 then
					return
				end
				lastActivation = now

				if type(item.OnPress) ~= "function" then
					warn("PlayerDropDown: no callback for " .. tostring(item.Name))
					return
				end

				print("PlayerDropDown: activated " .. tostring(item.Name)
					.. " for UserId " .. tostring(targetUserId))
				local callbackOk, callbackError = pcall(item.OnPress)
				if not callbackOk then
					warn("PlayerDropDown: " .. tostring(item.Name)
						.. " callback failed: " .. tostring(callbackError))
					notify("Action failed", "The action failed. Check the developer console for details.", "", 5)
				end
			end)
		end

		self.PopupFrame = frame
		return frame
	end

	Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(playerLeaving)
		if dropdown.Player == playerLeaving then
			forceHide()
		end
	end)

	return dropdown
end

local function registerEventCore(name, instance)
	pcall(function()
		StarterGui:RegisterGetCore(name, function()
			return instance
		end)
	end)
end

local function bindStatusEvent(event, isBlock)
	event.Event:Connect(function(userId, value)
		local player = Players:GetPlayerByUserId(tonumber(userId) or -1)
		if not player then
			return
		end

		local key
		if isBlock then
			key = value and "PlayerBlockedEvent" or "PlayerUnblockedEvent"
		else
			key = value and "PlayerMutedEvent" or "PlayerUnmutedEvent"
		end
		local targetEvent = moduleApiTable[key]
		if targetEvent then
			targetEvent:Fire(player)
		end
	end)
end

moduleApiTable.PlayerBlockedEvent = Instance.new("BindableEvent")
moduleApiTable.PlayerUnblockedEvent = Instance.new("BindableEvent")
moduleApiTable.PlayerMutedEvent = Instance.new("BindableEvent")
moduleApiTable.PlayerUnmutedEvent = Instance.new("BindableEvent")
moduleApiTable.PlayerFriendedEvent = Instance.new("BindableEvent")
moduleApiTable.PlayerUnfriendedEvent = Instance.new("BindableEvent")

registerEventCore("PlayerBlockedEvent", moduleApiTable.PlayerBlockedEvent)
registerEventCore("PlayerUnblockedEvent", moduleApiTable.PlayerUnblockedEvent)
registerEventCore("PlayerMutedEvent", moduleApiTable.PlayerMutedEvent)
registerEventCore("PlayerUnmutedEvent", moduleApiTable.PlayerUnmutedEvent)
registerEventCore("PlayerFriendedEvent", moduleApiTable.PlayerFriendedEvent)
registerEventCore("PlayerUnfriendedEvent", moduleApiTable.PlayerUnfriendedEvent)

bindStatusEvent(BlockChangedEvent, true)
bindStatusEvent(MuteChangedEvent, false)

LocalPlayer.FriendStatusChanged:Connect(function(player, status)
	if status == Enum.FriendStatus.Friend then
		moduleApiTable.PlayerFriendedEvent:Fire(player)
	elseif status == Enum.FriendStatus.NotFriend then
		moduleApiTable.PlayerUnfriendedEvent:Fire(player)
	end
end)

task.spawn(function()
	if FFlagHandlePlayerBlockListsInternalPermissive then
		UpdateBlockListEvent = RobloxReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("UpdatePlayerBlockList")
			or RobloxReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("UpdatePlayerBlockList", 60)
	end
end)

function moduleApiTable:CreatePlayerDropDown()
	return createPlayerDropDown()
end

function moduleApiTable:GetFriendCountAsync(player)
	return getFriendCountAsync(player and player.UserId)
end

function moduleApiTable:InitBlockListAsync()
	initializeBlockList()
end

function moduleApiTable:MaxFriendCount()
	return MAX_FRIEND_COUNT
end

function moduleApiTable:GetFriendStatus(player)
	return getFriendStatus(player)
end

function moduleApiTable:CreateBlockingUtility()
	return {
		BlockPlayerAsync = function(_, player)
			return BlockPlayerAsync(player)
		end,
		UnblockPlayerAsync = function(_, player)
			return UnblockPlayerAsync(player)
		end,
		MutePlayer = function(_, player)
			return MutePlayer(player)
		end,
		UnmutePlayer = function(_, player)
			return UnmutePlayer(player)
		end,
		IsPlayerBlockedByUserId = function(_, userId)
			return isBlocked(userId)
		end,
		GetBlockedStatusChangedEvent = function()
			return BlockChangedEvent.Event
		end,
		GetMutedStatusChangedEvent = function()
			return MuteChangedEvent.Event
		end,
		IsPlayerMutedByUserId = function(_, userId)
			return isMuted(userId)
		end,
		GetBlockedUserIdsAsync = function()
			return getBlockedUserIds()
		end,
	}
end

return moduleApiTable

                                <BinaryString name="SerializedOverrides" />
                                <ProtectedString name="Source">--[[
    Filename: GameTranslator.lua
    Yeku CoreScript localization bridge.
    Keeps player/game translation behavior and bootstraps CoreScript AutoLocalize.
]]

local LocalizationService = game:GetService("LocalizationService")
local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local CoreGui = game:GetService("CoreGui")

local function getFlag(name, fallback)
    local ok, value = pcall(function() return settings():GetFFlag(name) end)
    if ok then return value end
    return fallback
end

-- When this FFlag is absent on older clients, keep translation enabled and let
-- the translator safely fall back to the original English text.
-- 2015-2017 are intentionally English-only. Do not translate via Player.LocaleId.
local CoreScriptTranslateGameText = false

local function getRobloxTranslator()
    local modules = script.Parent
    local module = modules and modules:FindFirstChild("RobloxTranslator")
    if not module then
        local root = CoreGui:FindFirstChild("NewRobloxGui")
        modules = root and root:FindFirstChild("Modules")
        module = modules and modules:FindFirstChild("RobloxTranslator")
    end
    if module and module:IsA("ModuleScript") then
        local ok, result = pcall(require, module)
        if ok and type(result) == "table" then
            return result
        end
    end
    return nil
end

local CoreScriptTranslator = getRobloxTranslator()
if CoreScriptTranslator and type(CoreScriptTranslator.AttachCoreGui) == "function" then
    pcall(function() CoreScriptTranslator:AttachCoreGui() end)
end

local playerTranslator = nil
local player = nil
local localeId = nil
local localeIdConnection = nil
local localeChangedEvent = Instance.new("BindableEvent")
local registryInfoMap = {}

local function handlePlayerOrLocaleChanged()
    if player then
        local currentLocale
        pcall(function() currentLocale = player.LocaleId end)
        if currentLocale and currentLocale ~= localeId then
            localeId = currentLocale
            localeChangedEvent:Fire(localeId)
        end
    end
end

local function reset()
    playerTranslator = nil
    player = nil
    localeId = nil
    if localeIdConnection then
        localeIdConnection:Disconnect()
        localeIdConnection = nil
    end
end

local function getTranslator()
    if not playerTranslator then
        player = Players.LocalPlayer
        if player then
            local ok, translator = pcall(function()
                return LocalizationService:GetTranslatorForPlayer(player)
            end)
            if ok then playerTranslator = translator end
            handlePlayerOrLocaleChanged()
            local okSignal, signal = pcall(function()
                return player:GetPropertyChangedSignal("LocaleId")
            end)
            if okSignal and signal then
                localeIdConnection = signal:Connect(handlePlayerOrLocaleChanged)
            end
        end
    end
    return playerTranslator
end

local function isInsideGame(element)
    local ok, result = pcall(function() return game:IsAncestorOf(element) end)
    return ok and result or false
end

local GameTranslator = {}
GameTranslator.LocaleChanged = localeChangedEvent.Event

function GameTranslator:TranslateGameText(context, text)
    if type(text) ~= "string" or not CoreScriptTranslateGameText then
        return text
    end
    local translator = getTranslator()
    if translator then
        local ok, result = pcall(function()
            return translator:Translate(context, text)
        end)
        if ok and type(result) == "string" and result ~= "" then
            return result
        end
        -- Keep compatibility with CoreScript builds that expose the internal alias.
        ok, result = pcall(function()
            return translator:RobloxOnlyTranslate(context, text)
        end)
        if ok and type(result) == "string" and result ~= "" then
            return result
        end
    end
    return text
end

local function unregisterGui(element)
    local info = registryInfoMap[element]
    if info then
        if info.connection then info.connection:Disconnect() end
        registryInfoMap[element] = nil
    end
end

local function registerGui(element, context, text)
    local info = registryInfoMap[element]
    if info then
        info.context, info.text = context, text
        return
    end
    info = {context = context, text = text, hasBeenAdded = isInsideGame(element)}
    info.connection = element.AncestryChanged:Connect(function()
        if not isInsideGame(element) then
            if info.hasBeenAdded then unregisterGui(element) end
        else
            info.hasBeenAdded = true
        end
    end)
    registryInfoMap[element] = info
end

function GameTranslator:TranslateAndRegister(element, context, text)
    if not element then return text end
    if CoreScriptTranslateGameText then
        element.Text = self:TranslateGameText(context, text)
        registerGui(element, context, text)
    else
        element.Text = text
    end
    return text
end

local function retranslateAll()
    for element, info in pairs(registryInfoMap) do
        if element and element.Parent then
            element.Text = GameTranslator:TranslateGameText(info.context, info.text)
        else
            unregisterGui(element)
        end
    end
end

if CoreScriptTranslateGameText then
    pcall(function() LocalizationService.AutoTranslateWillRun:Connect(retranslateAll) end)
    Players:GetPropertyChangedSignal("LocalPlayer"):Connect(function()
        reset()
        getTranslator()
        retranslateAll()
    end)
end

return GameTranslator

                                <ProtectedString name="Source">--[[
    Filename: TenFootInterface.lua
    Yeku compatibility patch for CoreGui.NewRobloxGui.
    Preserves the public API used by legacy CoreScripts.

local HEALTH_GREEN_COLOR = Color3.new(27/255, 252/255, 107/255)
local DISPLAY_POS_INIT_INSET = 0
local DISPLAY_ITEM_OFFSET = 4
local FORCE_TEN_FOOT_INTERFACE = false

local UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")
local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local CoreGui = game:GetService("CoreGui")

local function platformIs(name)
	local ok, value = pcall(function() return Enum.Platform[name] end)
	return ok and value ~= nil and UserInputService:GetPlatform() == value
end

local tenFootInterfaceEnabled = platformIs("XBoxOne") or platformIs("WiiU") or
	platformIs("PS4") or platformIs("AndroidTV") or platformIs("XBox360") or
	platformIs("PS3") or platformIs("Ouya") or platformIs("SteamOS")
if FORCE_TEN_FOOT_INTERFACE then tenFootInterfaceEnabled = true end

local function resolveRobloxGui()
	local root
	pcall(function() root = script:FindFirstAncestor("NewRobloxGui") end)
	if root and root:IsA("ScreenGui") then return root end
	root = CoreGui:FindFirstChild("NewRobloxGui")
	if root and root:IsA("ScreenGui") then return root end
	root = CoreGui:FindFirstChild("RobloxGui")
	if root then return root end
	return CoreGui:WaitForChild("NewRobloxGui", 15) or CoreGui:WaitForChild("RobloxGui", 5)
end

local RobloxGui = resolveRobloxGui()
assert(RobloxGui, "TenFootInterface: CoreGui.NewRobloxGui is missing")

local function create(className, properties)
	local object = Instance.new(className)
	for key, value in pairs(properties or {}) do
		pcall(function() object[key] = value end)
	end
	return object
end

local function CreateModule()
	local api = {}
	local displayStack = {}
	local stackChanged = Instance.new("BindableEvent")
	local healthChanged = Instance.new("BindableEvent")

	local function createContainer()
		if not api.Container then
			api.Container = create("ImageButton", {
				Name = "TopRightContainer",
				Size = UDim2.new(0, 350, 0, 100),
				Position = UDim2.new(1, -415, 0, 10),
				AutoButtonColor = false,
				Image = "",
				Active = false,
				BackgroundTransparency = 1,
				Parent = RobloxGui,
			})
		end
	end

	local function repositionStack()
		local y = DISPLAY_POS_INIT_INSET
		for _, item in ipairs(displayStack) do
			if item and item.Parent then
				item.Position = UDim2.new(item.Position.X.Scale, item.Position.X.Offset, 0, y)
				if item.Visible then y = y + item.AbsoluteSize.Y + DISPLAY_ITEM_OFFSET end
			end
		end
		stackChanged:Fire()
	end

	local function addToDisplayStack(item)
		for i = #displayStack, 1, -1 do
			if displayStack[i] == item then table.remove(displayStack, i) end
		end
		table.insert(displayStack, item)
		createContainer()
		item.Parent = api.Container
		item:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Visible"):Connect(repositionStack)
		item:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteSize"):Connect(repositionStack)
		repositionStack()
	end

	function api:CreateHealthBar()
		self.HealthContainer = create("Frame", {
			Name = "HealthContainer", Size = UDim2.new(1, -86, 0, 50),
			Position = UDim2.new(0, 92, 0, 0), BorderSizePixel = 0,
			BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0), BackgroundTransparency = 0.5,
		})
		local holder = create("Frame", {
			Name = "HealthFillHolder", Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 1, -10),
			Position = UDim2.new(0, 5, 0, 5), BorderSizePixel = 0,
			BackgroundTransparency = 1, Parent = self.HealthContainer,
		})
		local fill = create("Frame", {
			Name = "HealthFill", Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0),
			BackgroundColor3 = HEALTH_GREEN_COLOR, BorderSizePixel = 0, Parent = holder,
		})
		local label = create("TextLabel", {
			Name = "HealthText", Size = UDim2.new(0, 98, 0, 50),
			Position = UDim2.new(0, -100, 0, 0), BackgroundTransparency = 0.5,
			BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0), Font = Enum.Font.SourceSans,
			TextSize = 36, Text = "Health", TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1),
			BorderSizePixel = 0, Parent = self.HealthContainer,
		})
		local username = create("TextLabel", {Visible = false})
		local accountType = create("TextLabel", {Visible = false})
		addToDisplayStack(self.HealthContainer)
		createContainer()
		self.HealthContainer.Changed:Connect(function() healthChanged:Fire() end)
		return self.Container, username, self.HealthContainer, fill, accountType
	end

	function api:CreateAccountType(accountTypeTextShort)
		self.AccountTypeContainer = create("Frame", {
			Name = "AccountTypeContainer", Size = UDim2.new(0, 50, 0, 50),
			Position = UDim2.new(1, -55, 0, 10), BorderSizePixel = 0,
			BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0), BackgroundTransparency = 0.5,
			Parent = RobloxGui,
		})
		create("TextLabel", {
			Name = "AccountTypeText", Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0),
			BackgroundTransparency = 1, Font = Enum.Font.SourceSans, TextSize = 36,
			Text = tostring(accountTypeTextShort or ""), TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1),
			BorderSizePixel = 0, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center,
			TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center, Parent = self.AccountTypeContainer,
		})
		return self.AccountTypeContainer
	end

	function api:SetupTopStat()
		local topStatEnabled = true
		local displayedStat = nil
		local changedConnection = nil
		local parentConnection = nil
		local childConnection = nil
		local statFrame = nil

		local function makeStatFrame()
			if statFrame then return end
			statFrame = create("Frame", {
				Name = "OneStatFrame", Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 36),
				BackgroundTransparency = 1,
			})
			create("TextLabel", {
				Name = "StatName", Size = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, 36),
				BackgroundTransparency = 1, Font = Enum.Font.SourceSans, TextSize = 36,
				TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1), Text = "", TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
				Parent = statFrame,
			})
			create("TextLabel", {
				Name = "StatValue", Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, 0),
				Size = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, 36), BackgroundTransparency = 1,
				Font = Enum.Font.SourceSans, TextSize = 36, TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1),
				Text = "", TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right, Parent = statFrame,
			})
			addToDisplayStack(statFrame)
		end

		local function isValidStat(obj)
			return obj and (obj:IsA("StringValue") or obj:IsA("IntValue") or obj:IsA("BoolValue") or
				obj:IsA("NumberValue") or obj:IsA("DoubleConstrainedValue") or obj:IsA("IntConstrainedValue"))
		end

		local function refreshStat()
			if not statFrame then return end
			local nameLabel = statFrame:FindFirstChild("StatName")
			local valueLabel = statFrame:FindFirstChild("StatValue")
			if displayedStat and displayedStat.Parent and topStatEnabled then
				nameLabel.Text = " " .. displayedStat.Name .. ":"
				valueLabel.Text = tostring(displayedStat.Value)
			else
				nameLabel.Text, valueLabel.Text = "", ""
			end
		end

		local function setDisplayedStat(stat)
			if changedConnection then changedConnection:Disconnect(); changedConnection = nil end
			if parentConnection then parentConnection:Disconnect(); parentConnection = nil end
			displayedStat = stat
			if displayedStat then
				makeStatFrame()
				changedConnection = displayedStat.Changed:Connect(refreshStat)
				parentConnection = displayedStat.AncestryChanged:Connect(function()
					if not displayedStat.Parent then setDisplayedStat(nil) else refreshStat() end
				end)
			end
			refreshStat()
		end

		local function scanLeaderstats()
			local localPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
			local leaderstats = localPlayer and localPlayer:FindFirstChild("leaderstats")
			if not leaderstats then return end
			if childConnection then childConnection:Disconnect() end
			for _, child in ipairs(leaderstats:GetChildren()) do
				if not displayedStat and isValidStat(child) then setDisplayedStat(child) end
			end
			childConnection = leaderstats.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
				if not displayedStat and isValidStat(child) then setDisplayedStat(child) end
			end)
		end

		local localPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
		if localPlayer then
			localPlayer.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
				if child.Name == "leaderstats" then scanLeaderstats() end
			end)
			scanLeaderstats()
		end

		local result = {}
		function result:SetTopStatEnabled(value)
			topStatEnabled = value == true
			refreshStat()
		end
		return result
	end
	return api
end

local TenFootInterfaceModule = CreateModule()
local moduleApiTable = {}
function moduleApiTable:IsEnabled() return tenFootInterfaceEnabled end
function moduleApiTable:CreateHealthBar() return TenFootInterfaceModule:CreateHealthBar() end
function moduleApiTable:CreateAccountType(text) return TenFootInterfaceModule:CreateAccountType(text) end
function moduleApiTable:SetupTopStat() return TenFootInterfaceModule:SetupTopStat() end
return moduleApiTable

local CoreGui = game:GetService("CoreGui")

local function resolveRobloxGui()
	local root
	pcall(function() root = script:FindFirstAncestor("NewRobloxGui") end)
	if root and root:IsA("ScreenGui") then return root end
	root = CoreGui:FindFirstChild("NewRobloxGui")
	if root and root:IsA("ScreenGui") then return root end
	root = CoreGui:FindFirstChild("RobloxGui")
	if root then return root end
	return CoreGui:WaitForChild("NewRobloxGui", 15) or CoreGui:WaitForChild("RobloxGui", 5)
end

                                <BinaryString name="SerializedOverrides" />
                                <ProtectedString name="Source">--[[
    Filename: ChatUtil.lua
    Yeku legacy CoreGui compatibility patch.

    Provides the historical Signal API used by legacy chat modules:
        signal:fire(...)
        signal:connect(callback)
        signal:wait()

    Also exposes the modern spellings:
        signal:Fire(...)
        signal:Connect(callback)
        signal:Wait()
        signal:Destroy()

    The BindableEvent receives the fired arguments directly. This avoids
    the original implementation's shared mArgData/mArgDataCount state,
    which could be overwritten when multiple fires occurred close together.
]]

local Util = {}

function Util.Signal()
	local bindable = Instance.new("BindableEvent")
	bindable.Name = "ChatUtilSignal"

	local signal = {
		_bindable = bindable,
		_destroyed = false,
	}

	local function assertAlive()
		if signal._destroyed then
			error("attempt to use a destroyed ChatUtil signal", 3)
		end
	end

	-- Historical method name used by older CoreScripts.
	function signal:fire(...)
		assertAlive()
		bindable:Fire(...)
	end

	-- Historical method name used by older CoreScripts.
	function signal:connect(callback)
		assertAlive()
		if type(callback) ~= "function" then
			error("connect(callback) expects a function", 2)
		end
		return bindable.Event:Connect(callback)
	end

	-- Yields the current thread until the next fire and returns every argument.
	function signal:wait()
		assertAlive()
		return bindable.Event:Wait()
	end

	-- Modern spellings share the same implementation and calling convention.
	signal.Fire = signal.fire
	signal.Connect = signal.connect
	signal.Wait = signal.wait

	function signal:Destroy()
		if self._destroyed then
			return
		end
		self._destroyed = true
		bindable:Destroy()
		self._bindable = nil
	end

	signal.destroy = signal.Destroy

	return signal
end

return Util

    FileName: Chat.lua
    Version: Yeku Legacy Chat 2016-style / TextChatService bridge v3.1

    Recreates the classic SolarCrane-era chat window and chat bar, while using
    NewChat as the only text-message transport. Includes direct-focus handling

    Expected hierarchy:
        CoreGui.NewRobloxGui.Modules.Chat

        CoreGui.NewRobloxGui.Modules.LegacyBubbleChat (optional)

    The module exposes the historical topbar API plus accessors used by the
    Yeku topbar's direct live-ChatBar focus route.
]]

local MESSAGES_FADE_OUT_TIME = 30
local MAX_UDIM_SIZE = 2 ^ 15 - 1
local PHONE_SCREEN_WIDTH = 640
local TABLET_SCREEN_WIDTH = 1024
local FLOOD_CHECK_MESSAGE_COUNT = 7
local FLOOD_CHECK_MESSAGE_INTERVAL = 15
local SCROLLBAR_THICKNESS = 7
local CHAT_WINDOW_Y_OFFSET = 2 -- the global Topbar GUI inset supplies the 36px header
local CHAT_BAR_HEIGHT = 32
local CHAT_BAR_FOCUSED_HEIGHT = 40
local CHAT_WINDOW_BACKGROUND = Color3.fromRGB(31, 31, 31)
local CHAT_BAR_INNER_BACKGROUND = Color3.fromRGB(209, 216, 221)
local CHAT_BAR_FOCUSED_BACKGROUND = Color3.fromRGB(235, 238, 240)
local TEXT_COLOR = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 243)
local ADMIN_TEXT_COLOR = Color3.fromRGB(255, 215, 0)
local INTERN_TEXT_COLOR = Color3.fromRGB(175, 221, 255)
local CHAT_COLORS = {
	Color3.fromRGB(253, 41, 67),
	Color3.fromRGB(1, 162, 255),
	Color3.fromRGB(2, 184, 87),
	BrickColor.new("Bright violet").Color,

local function log(message)
	print("[Chat] " .. tostring(message))
end

local function warnChat(message)
	warn("[Chat] " .. tostring(message))
end

local function tryRequire(moduleScript, label)
	if not moduleScript or not moduleScript:IsA("ModuleScript") then
		return nil
	end
	local ok, result = pcall(require, moduleScript)
	if not ok then
		warnChat("Could not load " .. tostring(label) .. ": " .. tostring(result))
		return nil
	end
	return result
end

local function connectSignal(signal, callback)
	if not signal then return nil end
	if typeof(signal) == "Instance" and signal:IsA("BindableEvent") then
		signal = signal.Event
	end
	local ok, connection = pcall(function()
		if signal.Connect then return signal:Connect(callback) end
		if signal.connect then return signal:connect(callback) end
		error("Unsupported signal")
	end)
	return ok and connection or nil
end

local function disconnect(connection)
	if connection then pcall(function() connection:Disconnect() end) end
end

local ChatBackend = tryRequire(Modules:FindFirstChild("NewChat"), "NewChat")
-- Use the dedicated 2015/2016 renderer at Modules.LegacyBubbleChat.
-- The nested Server.ClientChat.BubbleChat module is the 2017-style renderer.
-- ChatMain initializes this same cached module with the NewChat transport.
local LegacyBubbleChat = nil

local function startLegacyBubbleChat()
	if not LegacyBubbleChat or type(LegacyBubbleChat.Initialize) ~= "function" then return end
	task.spawn(function()
		-- PlayerGui may appear late in injected/CoreGui startup. Keep retrying
		-- instead of ending the dedicated 2015/2016 bubble renderer after 20s.
		for attempt = 1, 120 do
			if not script.Parent then return end
			local bubbleOK, bubbleResult = pcall(LegacyBubbleChat.Initialize, ChatBackend)
			if bubbleOK and bubbleResult == true then
				log("Initialized the 2015/2016 large-text bubble renderer from Chat.lua.")
				return
			end
			if not bubbleOK then
				warnChat("2015/2016 LegacyBubbleChat attempt " .. tostring(attempt) .. " failed: " .. tostring(bubbleResult))
			end
			task.wait(0.5)
		end
		warnChat("2015/2016 LegacyBubbleChat did not become ready after 120 attempts; chat history remains available.")
	end)
end

local bubbleModule = Modules:FindFirstChild("LegacyBubbleChat")
if bubbleModule and bubbleModule:IsA("ModuleScript") then
	LegacyBubbleChat = tryRequire(bubbleModule, "2015/2016 large-text LegacyBubbleChat")
end
if LegacyBubbleChat then
	startLegacyBubbleChat()
else
	-- Module insertion can lag behind Chat.lua during the initial tree clone.
	task.spawn(function()
		local deadline = os.clock() + 20
		while not LegacyBubbleChat and os.clock() &lt; deadline and script.Parent do
			local candidate = Modules:FindFirstChild("LegacyBubbleChat")
			if candidate and candidate:IsA("ModuleScript") then
				LegacyBubbleChat = tryRequire(candidate, "2015/2016 large-text LegacyBubbleChat")
				if LegacyBubbleChat then break end
			end
			task.wait(0.25)
		end
		if LegacyBubbleChat then
			startLegacyBubbleChat()
		else
			warnChat("The dedicated Modules.LegacyBubbleChat renderer did not become available.")
		end
	end)
end

local playerDropDownModule = nil
local playerDropDown = nil
local blockingUtility = nil
local playerDropDownLoading = false
local playerDropDownLoadFailed = false
local playerDropDownCallbacks = {}

local function ensurePlayerDropDown(callback)
	if callback then
		if playerDropDownModule and (playerDropDown or blockingUtility) then
			task.defer(callback)
			return true
		end
		table.insert(playerDropDownCallbacks, callback)
	end
	if playerDropDownModule and (playerDropDown or blockingUtility) then return true end
	if playerDropDownLoading or playerDropDownLoadFailed then return false end
	playerDropDownLoading = true
	task.spawn(function()
		local dropdownScript = Modules:FindFirstChild("PlayerDropDown")
		if not dropdownScript then dropdownScript = Modules:WaitForChild("PlayerDropDown", 15) end
		local loadedModule
		local loadOK = false
		if dropdownScript and dropdownScript:IsA("ModuleScript") then
			loadOK, loadedModule = pcall(require, dropdownScript)
		end
		if not loadOK or type(loadedModule) ~= "table" then
			playerDropDownLoading = false
			playerDropDownLoadFailed = true
			warnChat("Deferred PlayerDropDown require failed: " .. tostring(loadedModule or "module not found"))
			playerDropDownCallbacks = {}
			return
		end
		playerDropDownModule = loadedModule
		if type(loadedModule.CreatePlayerDropDown) == "function" then
			local ok, result = pcall(loadedModule.CreatePlayerDropDown, loadedModule)
			if ok then playerDropDown = result else warnChat("Player dropdown creation failed: " .. tostring(result)) end
		end
		if type(loadedModule.CreateBlockingUtility) == "function" then
			local ok, result = pcall(loadedModule.CreateBlockingUtility, loadedModule)
			if ok then blockingUtility = result else warnChat("Player blocking utility creation failed: " .. tostring(result)) end
		end
		playerDropDownLoading = false
		local callbacks = playerDropDownCallbacks
		playerDropDownCallbacks = {}
		for _, pending in ipairs(callbacks) do
			task.defer(function()
				local ok, err = pcall(pending)
				if not ok then warnChat("Deferred player dropdown action failed: " .. tostring(err)) end
			end)
		end
	end)
	return false
end
local function backendCall(methodName, ...)
	if not ChatBackend or type(ChatBackend[methodName]) ~= "function" then
		return false, "NewChat method " .. tostring(methodName) .. " is unavailable."
	end
	local args = table.pack(...)
	local ok, a, b, c = pcall(function()
		return ChatBackend[methodName](ChatBackend, table.unpack(args, 1, args.n))
	end)
	if not ok then return false, tostring(a) end
	return true, a, b, c
end

local function textChannels()
	local ok, channels = backendCall("GetTextChannels")
	if ok and type(channels) == "table" then return channels end
	local result = {}
	local folder = TextChatService:FindFirstChild("TextChannels")
	if folder then
		for _, child in ipairs(folder:GetChildren()) do
			if child:IsA("TextChannel") then table.insert(result, child) end
		end
	end
	for _, child in ipairs(TextChatService:GetChildren()) do
		if child:IsA("TextChannel") then table.insert(result, child) end
	end
	return result
end

local function findChannel(mode, targetPlayer)
	local all = textChannels()
	if mode == "All" then
		for _, channel in ipairs(all) do
			if channel.Name == "RBXGeneral" then return channel.Name end
		end
		return nil
	elseif mode == "Team" then
		for _, channel in ipairs(all) do
			if string.find(string.lower(channel.Name), "team", 1, true) then
				return channel.Name
			end
		end
	elseif mode == "Whisper" and targetPlayer then
		local targetId = tostring(targetPlayer.UserId)
		local targetName = string.lower(targetPlayer.Name)
		for _, channel in ipairs(all) do
			local channelName = string.lower(channel.Name)
			if string.find(channelName, "whisper", 1, true)
				and (string.find(channelName, targetId, 1, true)
					or string.find(channelName, targetName, 1, true)) then
				return channel.Name
			end
		end
	end
	return nil
end

local function computeChatColor(playerName)
	local sum = 0
	for index = 1, #playerName do
		local value = string.byte(string.sub(playerName, index, index))
		local reverseIndex = #playerName - index + 1
		if #playerName % 2 == 1 then reverseIndex -= 1 end
		if reverseIndex % 4 &gt;= 2 then value = -value end
		sum += value
	end
	return CHAT_COLORS[(sum % #CHAT_COLORS) + 1]
end

local function isBlocked(player)
	if not player or not blockingUtility then return false end
	local ok, result = pcall(function()
		return blockingUtility:IsPlayerBlockedByUserId(player.UserId)
	end)
	return ok and result == true
end

local function isMuted(player)
	if not player or not blockingUtility then return false end
	local ok, result = pcall(function()
		return blockingUtility:IsPlayerMutedByUserId(player.UserId)
	end)
	return ok and result == true
end

-- The legacy root name plus these compatibility marker frames lets the Yeku

local IDLE_CHAT_PROMPT = 'To chat click here or press "/" key'
local MOBILE_CHAT_BAR_PROMPT = 'Tap here to chat'
local mobileChatBarFocused = false
local function setIdleChatPromptVisible(visible)
	-- The idle prompt is a separate button behind the TextBox. System welcome
	-- messages are inserted only into ChatWindow history by PrintWelcome().
	ChatBar.PlaceholderText = ""
	if UserInputService.TouchEnabled then
		ChatBar.PlaceholderText = MOBILE_CHAT_BAR_PROMPT
		ClickToChatButton.Text = ""
		ClickToChatButton.Visible = false
	else
		ClickToChatButton.Text = IDLE_CHAT_PROMPT
		ClickToChatButton.Visible = visible == true
	end
end

local function clearChatBarWelcomePrompt()
	-- The mobile textbox prompt is separate from ChatWindow history. Hide it
	-- only while the TextBox is focused; restore it when the bar is idle.
	pcall(function()
		ChatBar.PlaceholderText = (UserInputService.TouchEnabled and not mobileChatBarFocused)
			and MOBILE_CHAT_BAR_PROMPT or ""
	end)
end
clearChatBarWelcomePrompt()
setIdleChatPromptVisible(false)

-- ============================================================
-- SIGNALS / STATE
-- ============================================================

local function createSignal()
	local event = Instance.new("BindableEvent")
	local signal = {}
	function signal:fire(...) event:Fire(...) end
	function signal:Fire(...) event:Fire(...) end
	function signal:connect(callback) return event.Event:Connect(callback) end
	function signal:Connect(callback) return event.Event:Connect(callback) end
	function signal:wait() return event.Event:Wait() end
	function signal:Wait() return event.Event:Wait() end
	signal.Event = event.Event
	signal._event = event
	return signal
end

local topbarEnabled = true
-- The custom legacy frontend must obey the same Chat CoreGui permission as
-- Roblox's built-in chat. A hidden native chat flag is not a license to show
-- or focus the replacement UI.
local chatCoreGuiEnabled = true
local allCoreGuiDisabled = false
pcall(function()
	local chatAllowed = StarterGui:GetCoreGuiEnabled(Enum.CoreGuiType.Chat)
	if type(chatAllowed) == "boolean" then chatCoreGuiEnabled = chatAllowed end
end)
local function isYekuCustomChatEnabled()
    local root = CoreGui:FindFirstChild("NewRobloxGui")
    if not root then return true end
    return root:GetAttribute("YekuCustomChatEnabled") ~= false
        and root:GetAttribute("YekuCoreGuiChatEnabled") ~= false
        and root:GetAttribute("YekuCoreGuiAllEnabled") ~= false

end
