-- ============================================================
-- LAYOUT: classic 2016 phone/tablet/desktop proportions
-- ============================================================

local function currentViewportSize()
	local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
	if camera then return camera.ViewportSize end
	return GuiRoot.AbsoluteSize
end

local function isSmallScreen()
	local viewport = currentViewportSize()
	return viewport.X &lt;= PHONE_SCREEN_WIDTH
end

local function bubbleChatEnabled()
	-- The 2015/2016 chat window and its historical bubble renderer coexist.
	-- A backend BubbleChatOnly flag must never make the classic message window
	-- impossible to open or close.
	return false
end

-- In bar-only mode, hide the conversation-history panel but retain the
-- standalone ChatBar. This is driven by the same authoritative mode signals
-- as the bundled legacy ChatWindow: Players.ClassicChat=false, or a disabled
-- TextChatService ChatWindowConfiguration with the input bar still enabled.
local function chatHistoryDisabled()
	local classicOK, classicEnabled = pcall(function()
		return Players.ClassicChat
	end)
	if classicOK and type(classicEnabled) == "boolean" and classicEnabled == false then
		return true
	end

	local windowConfig = TextChatService:FindFirstChild("ChatWindowConfiguration")
	local inputConfig = TextChatService:FindFirstChild("ChatInputBarConfiguration")
	if windowConfig and inputConfig then
		local windowOK, windowEnabled = pcall(function() return windowConfig.Enabled end)
		local inputOK, inputEnabled = pcall(function() return inputConfig.Enabled end)
		if windowOK and inputOK
			and type(windowEnabled) == "boolean"
			and type(inputEnabled) == "boolean"
			and windowEnabled == false and inputEnabled == true then
			return true
		end
	end
	return false
end

local function chatHistoryVisible()
	return not chatHistoryDisabled()
end

local function setChatBarParentHeight(height)
	local size = ChatBarParentFrame.Size
	ChatBarParentFrame.Size = UDim2.new(size.X.Scale, size.X.Offset, 0, height)
	-- The positionable outer frame owns the height; its inner frame always fills it.
	ChatBarContainer.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
end

local function keepMobileChatBarOffscreen()
	-- 2015/2016 touch UI focused the hidden edit box from the topbar, not
	-- from a visible ChatBar overlay. Keep the TextBox alive for CaptureFocus.
	if UserInputService.TouchEnabled and not UserInputService.VREnabled then
		ChatBarParentFrame.Position = UDim2.new(0, -10000, 0, 0)
	end
end

local function updateChatLayout()
	local viewport = currentViewportSize()
	local width = viewport.X

	-- SetCore ChatWindowPosition/ChatWindowSize opt out of automatic geometry.
	-- The original CoreScripts did the same, so viewport changes must not undo it.
	if not chatRepositioned then
		local windowSize
		local barHeight = chatBarFocused and CHAT_BAR_FOCUSED_HEIGHT or CHAT_BAR_HEIGHT

		if UserInputService.VREnabled then
			windowSize = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
			ChatWindow.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
			ChatBarParentFrame.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 20)
			ChatBarParentFrame.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, barHeight)
		elseif width &lt;= PHONE_SCREEN_WIDTH then
			windowSize = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, -CHAT_WINDOW_Y_OFFSET)
			ChatWindow.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, CHAT_WINDOW_Y_OFFSET)
			ChatBarParentFrame.Size = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, barHeight)
			ChatBarParentFrame.Position = bubbleChatEnabled()
				and UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 6)
				or UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.5, 2)
		elseif width &lt;= TABLET_SCREEN_WIDTH then
			windowSize = UDim2.new(0.4, 0, 0.3, -CHAT_WINDOW_Y_OFFSET)
			ChatWindow.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, CHAT_WINDOW_Y_OFFSET)
			ChatBarParentFrame.Size = UDim2.new(0.4, 0, 0, barHeight)
			ChatBarParentFrame.Position = bubbleChatEnabled()
				and UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 6)
				or UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.3, 2)
		else
			windowSize = UDim2.new(0.3, 0, 0.25, -CHAT_WINDOW_Y_OFFSET)
			ChatWindow.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, CHAT_WINDOW_Y_OFFSET)
			ChatBarParentFrame.Size = UDim2.new(0.3, 0, 0, barHeight)
			ChatBarParentFrame.Position = bubbleChatEnabled()
				and UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 6)
				or UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.25, 2)
		end

		ChatWindow.Size = windowSize
	end

	if chatHistoryDisabled() then
		ChatBarParentFrame.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, CHAT_WINDOW_Y_OFFSET)
	elseif chatRepositioned then
		ChatBarParentFrame.Position = ChatWindow.Position
			+ UDim2.new(0, 0, ChatWindow.Size.Y.Scale, ChatWindow.Size.Y.Offset + 2)
	end

	scrollingFrame.Size = UDim2.new(1, -14, 1, -20)
	ChatBarContainer.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
	keepMobileChatBarOffscreen()

	-- Reflow rows after Roblox has applied the new absolute sizes. rebuildMessages
	-- already destroys and recreates its own rows; do not temporarily empty history.
	task.defer(function()
		if ChatInstance and ChatWindow.Parent and ChatInstance._rebuildMessages then
			ChatInstance:_rebuildMessages()
		end
	end)
end

-- ============================================================
-- HISTORICAL MESSAGE ROWS
-- ============================================================

local function setGuiTextTransparency(gui, value)
	if not gui then return end
	for _, descendant in ipairs(gui:GetDescendants()) do
		if descendant:IsA("TextLabel") or descendant:IsA("TextButton") then
			descendant.TextTransparency = value
			descendant.TextStrokeTransparency = value &gt;= 1 and 1 or ChatInstance.Settings.TextStrokeTransparency
		end
	end
end

local function measureText(text, font, size, maxWidth)
	local safeWidth = math.max(30, maxWidth)
	-- RichText markup contributes no visible glyphs; exclude tags from layout.
	local plainText = string.gsub(tostring(text or ""), "&lt;[^&gt;]-&gt;", "")
	local ok, bounds = pcall(function()
		return TextService:GetTextSize(plainText, size, font, Vector2.new(safeWidth, 1000))
	end)
	if ok and bounds then return bounds end
	return Vector2.new(safeWidth, size + 3)
end

local function messageRowWidth()
	return math.max(80, scrollingFrame.AbsoluteSize.X - SCROLLBAR_THICKNESS - 2)
end

local function destroyRows()
	for _, child in ipairs(MessageContainer:GetChildren()) do
		child:Destroy()
	end
end

local function rebuildMessages()
	if isRebuildingLayout then return end
	isRebuildingLayout = true
	destroyRows()
	local y = 0
	local maxWidth = messageRowWidth()
	local fontSize = isSmallScreen() and ChatInstance.Settings.SmallScreenFontSize or ChatInstance.Settings.FontSize

	for _, record in ipairs(ChatInstance.Messages) do
		local row = Instance.new("Frame")
		row.Name = "Message_" .. tostring(record.Id)
		row.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		row.BorderSizePixel = 0
		row.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, y)
		row.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 18)
		row.ZIndex = 2
		row.Parent = MessageContainer
		record.Gui = row

		if record.Kind == "system" then
			local label = Instance.new("TextLabel")
			label.Name = "SystemChatMessage"
			label.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
			label.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 18)
			label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
			label.BorderSizePixel = 0
			label.Text = record.Text
			label.RichText = record.RichText == true
			label.TextColor3 = record.Color or ChatInstance.Settings.DefaultMessageTextColor
			label.TextStrokeColor3 = ChatInstance.Settings.TextStrokeColor
			label.TextStrokeTransparency = record.TextTransparency or ChatInstance.Settings.TextStrokeTransparency
			label.Font = record.Font or ChatInstance.Settings.Font
			label.TextSize = record.TextSize or fontSize
			label.TextWrapped = true
			label.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
			label.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top
			label.ZIndex = 2
			label.Parent = row
			local bounds = measureText(label.Text, label.Font, label.TextSize, maxWidth)
			local height = math.max(fontSize + 2, bounds.Y + 1)
			row.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, height)
			label.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, height)
			record.Height = height
		else
			local speaker = record.Speaker or "Unknown"
			local prefix = "[" .. speaker .. "]:"
			local nameColor = record.NameColor or ChatInstance.Settings.DefaultMessageTextColor
			local nameBounds = measureText(prefix, ChatInstance.Settings.Font, fontSize, maxWidth)
			local nameWidth = math.min(maxWidth * 0.65, math.ceil(nameBounds.X + 1))
			local availableMessageWidth = math.max(25, maxWidth - nameWidth - 4)
			local bodyBounds = measureText(record.Text or "", ChatInstance.Settings.Font, fontSize, availableMessageWidth)
			local height = math.max(fontSize + 2, bodyBounds.Y + 2, nameBounds.Y + 2)

			local nameLabel = (record.Sender and not UserInputService.TouchEnabled)
				and Instance.new("TextButton") or Instance.new("TextLabel")
			nameLabel.Name = "PlayerName"
			nameLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
			nameLabel.Size = UDim2.new(0, nameWidth, 0, math.max(fontSize + 2, nameBounds.Y + 1))
			nameLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
			nameLabel.BorderSizePixel = 0
			if nameLabel:IsA("TextButton") then nameLabel.AutoButtonColor = false end
			nameLabel.Text = prefix
			nameLabel.TextColor3 = nameColor
			nameLabel.TextTransparency = record.Pending and 0.35 or 0
			nameLabel.TextStrokeColor3 = ChatInstance.Settings.TextStrokeColor
			nameLabel.TextStrokeTransparency = record.TextTransparency or ChatInstance.Settings.TextStrokeTransparency
			nameLabel.Font = ChatInstance.Settings.Font
			nameLabel.TextSize = fontSize
			nameLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
			nameLabel.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top
			nameLabel.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
			nameLabel.ZIndex = 3
			nameLabel.Parent = row
			if record.Sender and nameLabel:IsA("TextButton") then
				local sender = record.Sender
				nameLabel.Activated:Connect(function()
					if sender.Parent == Players and sender ~= LocalPlayer and setMessageMode and focusChatBar then
						setMessageMode("Whisper", sender)
						focusChatBar()
					end
				end)
				nameLabel.MouseButton2Click:Connect(function()
					if sender.Parent ~= Players or sender == LocalPlayer then return end
					ensurePlayerDropDown(function()
						if not playerDropDown or sender.Parent ~= Players then return end
						local ok, popup = pcall(function()
							return playerDropDown:CreatePopup(sender)
						end)
						if ok and typeof(popup) == "Instance" then
							popup.Position = UDim2.new(0, nameLabel.AbsolutePosition.X + nameLabel.AbsoluteSize.X + 2,
								0, nameLabel.AbsolutePosition.Y)
							popup.ZIndex = 20
							popup.Parent = GuiRoot
						end
					end)
				end)
			end

			local body = Instance.new("TextLabel")
			body.Name = "ChatMessage"
			body.Position = UDim2.new(0, nameWidth + 4, 0, 0)
			body.Size = UDim2.new(1, -(nameWidth + 4), 0, height)
			body.BackgroundTransparency = 1
			body.BorderSizePixel = 0
			body.Text = record.Text or ""
			body.RichText = record.RichText == true
			body.TextColor3 = record.Color or ChatInstance.Settings.DefaultMessageTextColor
			body.TextTransparency = record.Pending and 0.35 or 0
			body.TextStrokeColor3 = ChatInstance.Settings.TextStrokeColor
			body.TextStrokeTransparency = record.TextTransparency or ChatInstance.Settings.TextStrokeTransparency
			body.Font = record.Font or ChatInstance.Settings.Font
			body.TextSize = record.TextSize or fontSize
			body.TextWrapped = true
			body.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
			body.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top
			body.ZIndex = 2
			body.Parent = row
			row.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, height)
			record.Height = height
		end

		if record.Faded then
			setGuiTextTransparency(row, 1)
		end
		y += record.Height or (fontSize + 2)
	end

	MessageContainer.Size = UDim2.new(1, -SCROLLBAR_THICKNESS - 1, 0, y)
	MessageContainer.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, -y)
	scrollingFrame.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(1, -14, 0, y)
	if scrollingFrame.AbsoluteWindowSize.Y &gt; 0 then
		scrollingFrame.CanvasPosition = Vector2.new(0, math.max(0, y - scrollingFrame.AbsoluteWindowSize.Y))
	end
	isRebuildingLayout = false
end
ChatInstance._rebuildMessages = rebuildMessages

local function getYekuChatLocalization()
	local modulesRoot = script:FindFirstAncestor("Modules")
	local server = modulesRoot and modulesRoot:FindFirstChild("Server")
	local clientChat = server and server:FindFirstChild("ClientChat")
	local defaults = clientChat and clientChat:FindFirstChild("DefaultClientChatModules")
	local moduleScript = defaults and defaults:FindFirstChild("ChatLocalization")
	if not moduleScript or not moduleScript:IsA("ModuleScript") then return nil end
	local ok, result = pcall(require, moduleScript)
	if ok and type(result) == "table" and type(result.Get) == "function" then return result end
	return nil
end

local function resolveLocalizationMarkup(value)
	return tostring(value or "")
end


local function addMessage(kind, text, sender, options, silently)
	text = resolveLocalizationMarkup(text)
	if type(text) ~= "string" or text == "" then return end
	options = options or {}
	messageId += 1
	local record = {
		Id = messageId,
		Kind = kind,
		Text = string.sub(text, 1, 2048),
		Speaker = sender and sender.Name or options.Speaker,
		Sender = sender,
		NameColor = sender and (not sender.Neutral and sender.TeamColor.Color or computeChatColor(sender.Name)) or options.NameColor,
		Color = options.Color,
		Font = options.Font,
		TextSize = options.TextSize,
		RichText = options.RichText == true,
		TextTransparency = nil,
		CreatedAt = os.clock(),
		Faded = silently == true,
	}
	table.insert(ChatInstance.Messages, record)
	while #ChatInstance.Messages &gt; ChatInstance.Settings.MaxWindowChatMessages do
		table.remove(ChatInstance.Messages, 1)
	end
	if not silently then
		ChatInstance.MessageCount += 1
		ChatInstance.MessagesChanged:fire(ChatInstance.MessageCount)
		lastChatActivity = os.clock()
		for _, oldRecord in ipairs(ChatInstance.Messages) do
			oldRecord.Faded = false
		end
	end
	rebuildMessages()
	if ChatWindow.Visible and not silently then
		for _, oldRecord in ipairs(ChatInstance.Messages) do
			if oldRecord.Gui then setGuiTextTransparency(oldRecord.Gui, 0) end
		end
	end
	task.delay(MESSAGES_FADE_OUT_TIME, function()
		if not ChatGui.Parent or not record.Gui then return end
		if os.clock() - lastChatActivity &gt;= MESSAGES_FADE_OUT_TIME then
			for _, current in ipairs(ChatInstance.Messages) do
				current.Faded = true
				if current.Gui then setGuiTextTransparency(current.Gui, 1) end
			end
		end
	end)
	return record
end

function ChatInstance:AddSystemChatMessage(text, silently, options)
	local normalized = string.lower(tostring(text or "")):gsub("%s+", " ")
	if normalized == "tap here to chat"
		or normalized == "to chat click here or press '/' key"
		or normalized == 'to chat click here or press "/" key' then
		return false
	end
	return addMessage("system", tostring(text), nil, options, silently == true)
end

function ChatInstance:AddDeveloperSystemChatMessage(info)
	if type(info) ~= "table" or type(info.Text) ~= "string" then return end
	local options = {}
	if typeof(info.Color) == "Color3" then options.Color = info.Color end
	if typeof(info.Font) == "EnumItem" and info.Font.EnumType == Enum.Font then options.Font = info.Font end
	options.RichText = info.RichText == true
	local requestedTextSize = info.TextSize
	if requestedTextSize == nil then
		requestedTextSize = info.FontSize
	end
	local resolvedTextSize
	if type(requestedTextSize) == "number" then
		resolvedTextSize = requestedTextSize
	elseif type(requestedTextSize) == "string" then
		resolvedTextSize = tonumber(requestedTextSize)
			or tonumber(string.match(requestedTextSize, "(%d+)"))
	elseif typeof(requestedTextSize) == "EnumItem" and requestedTextSize.EnumType == Enum.FontSize then
		resolvedTextSize = tonumber(string.match(requestedTextSize.Name, "(%d+)"))
	end
	if resolvedTextSize then
		options.TextSize = math.clamp(resolvedTextSize, 8, 48)
	end
	self:AddSystemChatMessage(info.Text, false, options)
end

local function isSlashCommand(text)
	return string.sub(text, 1, 1) == "/"
end

local function shouldShowPlayerMessage(player, text)
	if not player or type(text) ~= "string" or text == "" or isSlashCommand(text) then return false end
	if isBlocked(player) or isMuted(player) then return false end
	return true
end

local PlayerPermissionsModule = tryRequire(Modules:FindFirstChild("PlayerPermissionsModule"), "PlayerPermissionsModule")

function ChatInstance:OnPlayerChatted(chatType, sender, text, receivingPlayer)
	if not shouldShowPlayerMessage(sender, text) then return end
	local renderedText = text
	if chatType == Enum.PlayerChatType.Whisper and sender == LocalPlayer and receivingPlayer then
		renderedText = "To " .. receivingPlayer.Name .. ": " .. text
	end
	local options = {}
	if PlayerPermissionsModule and sender then
		local isAdmin = false
		local isIntern = false
		pcall(function()
			if type(PlayerPermissionsModule.IsPlayerAdminAsync) == "function" then
				isAdmin = PlayerPermissionsModule.IsPlayerAdminAsync(sender) == true
			end
			if type(PlayerPermissionsModule.IsPlayerInternAsync) == "function" then
				isIntern = PlayerPermissionsModule.IsPlayerInternAsync(sender) == true
			end
		end)
		if isAdmin then options.Color = ChatInstance.Settings.AdminTextColor
		elseif isIntern then options.Color = ChatInstance.Settings.InternTextColor end
	end
	addMessage("player", renderedText, sender, options)
end

-- ============================================================
-- LAYOUT / SHOW-HIDE / INPUT FOCUS
-- ============================================================

local function playTransparencyTween(previous, object, property, value, duration)
	if previous then pcall(function() previous:Cancel() end) end
	local ok, tween = pcall(function()
		return TweenService:Create(object,
			TweenInfo.new(duration or 0.2, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear, Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
			{[property] = value})
	end)
	if ok and tween then tween:Play(); return tween end
	return nil
end

local function playTransparencyTween(previous, object, property, value, duration)
	if previous then pcall(function() previous:Cancel() end) end
	local ok, tween = pcall(function()
		return TweenService:Create(object,
			TweenInfo.new(duration or 0.2, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear, Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
			{[property] = value})
	end)
	if ok and tween then tween:Play(); return tween end
	return nil
end

local function setBackgroundTransparency(value, duration)
	local fadeDuration = duration or 0.2
	transparencyTween = playTransparencyTween(transparencyTween, ChatWindow, "BackgroundTransparency", value, fadeDuration)
	-- The detached ChatBar overlay must fade on the exact same clock as the
	-- history panel; previously only ChatWindow was tweened.
	local barBackground = UserInputService.TouchEnabled and 1 or value
	local innerBackground = UserInputService.TouchEnabled and 1 or value
	barContainerTransparencyTween = playTransparencyTween(barContainerTransparencyTween, ChatBarContainer, "BackgroundTransparency", barBackground, fadeDuration)
	barInnerTransparencyTween = playTransparencyTween(barInnerTransparencyTween, ChatBarInnerBackground, "BackgroundTransparency", innerBackground, fadeDuration)
	local textTransparency = value &gt;= 0.99 and 1 or 0
	barTextTransparencyTween = playTransparencyTween(barTextTransparencyTween, ClickToChatButton, "TextTransparency", textTransparency, fadeDuration)
	barInputTextTransparencyTween = playTransparencyTween(barInputTextTransparencyTween, ChatBar, "TextTransparency", textTransparency, fadeDuration)
	barModeTextTransparencyTween = playTransparencyTween(barModeTextTransparencyTween, ChatModeText, "TextTransparency", textTransparency, fadeDuration)
end

local function updateChatBarModeText()
	if ChatInstance.MessageMode == "Team" then
		ChatModeText.Text = "[Team]"
		ChatModeText.TextColor3 = ChatInstance.Settings.TeamTextColor
	elseif ChatInstance.MessageMode == "Whisper" then
		ChatModeText.Text = "[" .. (ChatInstance.TargetWhisperPlayer and ChatInstance.TargetWhisperPlayer.Name or "?") .. "]"
		ChatModeText.TextColor3 = ChatInstance.Settings.WhisperTextColor
	else
		ChatModeText.Text = ""
	end
	local prefixWidth = 0
	if ChatModeText.Text ~= "" then
		prefixWidth = measureText(ChatModeText.Text, ChatModeText.Font, ChatModeText.TextSize, 250).X + 6
	end
	ChatModeText.Size = UDim2.new(0, prefixWidth, 1, 0)
	ChatBar.Position = UDim2.new(0, 7 + prefixWidth, 0, 0)
	ChatBar.Size = UDim2.new(1, -14 - prefixWidth, 1, 0)
end

local function setVisible(visible)
	local requestedVisible = visible == true
	visible = requestedVisible and coreGuiEnabled
	ChatInstance.Visible = visible
	local canRender = visible and (topbarEnabled or UserInputService.VREnabled)
	visibilityFadeToken += 1
	local thisFade = visibilityFadeToken

	if canRender then
		ChatGui.Enabled = true
		ChatWindow.Visible = not bubbleChatEnabled() and chatHistoryVisible()
		ChatBarParentFrame.Visible = not chatBarDisabled
		if UserInputService.TouchEnabled then
			ChatBar.Visible = not chatBarDisabled
			ChatBar.Active = not chatBarDisabled
			ChatBar.TextTransparency = 1
			setIdleChatPromptVisible(false)
		elseif not chatBarFocused and not chatBarDisabled then
			ChatBar.Visible = false
			ChatBar.Active = false
			ChatBar.TextTransparency = 1
			setIdleChatPromptVisible(true)
		else
			setIdleChatPromptVisible(false)
		end
		keepMobileChatBarOffscreen()
		mouseLeaveFadeToken += 1
		lastChatActivity = os.clock()
		setBackgroundTransparency(0.5, 0.15)
	else
		-- Close synchronously; never leave a stale visible ChatWindow after the
		-- logical state has toggled off.
		ChatWindow.Visible = false
		ChatBarParentFrame.Visible = false
		setIdleChatPromptVisible(false)
		ChatBar.Visible = false
		ChatBar.Active = false
		ChatBar.TextTransparency = 1
		if UserInputService:GetFocusedTextBox() == ChatBar then
			pcall(function() ChatBar:ReleaseFocus(false) end)
		end
		clearChatBarWelcomePrompt()
		setBackgroundTransparency(1, 0.15)
	end
	ChatInstance.VisibilityStateChanged:fire(canRender)
	return ChatInstance.Visible == requestedVisible
end

local function applyFocusedChatBarVisuals()
	local wasFocused = chatBarFocused
	chatBarFocused = true
	mobileChatBarFocused = true
	ChatBarParentFrame.Visible = ChatInstance.Visible and coreGuiEnabled
		and (topbarEnabled or UserInputService.VREnabled) and not chatBarDisabled
	keepMobileChatBarOffscreen()
	ChatBar.Visible = true
	ChatBar.Active = true
	setIdleChatPromptVisible(false)
	clearChatBarWelcomePrompt()
	ChatModeText.Visible = not UserInputService.TouchEnabled and ChatInstance.MessageMode ~= "All"
	if UserInputService.TouchEnabled then
		-- Mobile uses the legacy topbar's single/two-dot chat control. Keep the
		-- editable TextBox active for the keyboard, but do not draw a desktop bar.
		ChatBar.TextTransparency = 1
		ChatBar.PlaceholderText = ""
		ChatBarInnerBackground.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		ChatBarContainer.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		setChatBarParentHeight(CHAT_BAR_HEIGHT)
		keepMobileChatBarOffscreen()
	else
		ChatBar.TextTransparency = 0
		ChatBar.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(64, 64, 64)
		ChatBarInnerBackground.BackgroundColor3 = CHAT_BAR_FOCUSED_BACKGROUND
		ChatBarInnerBackground.BackgroundTransparency = 0
		ChatBarContainer.BackgroundTransparency = 0.5
		setChatBarParentHeight(CHAT_BAR_FOCUSED_HEIGHT)
	end
	updateChatBarModeText()
	lastChatActivity = os.clock()
	if not wasFocused then ChatInstance.ChatBarFocusChanged:fire(true) end
end

-- ChatbarFocusVisual is the explicit visual + keyboard-focus operation exposed
-- to Topbar. It assumes the chat window has already been opened. Keeping this
-- separate prevents the '/' shortcut from focusing an invisible ChatBar while
-- leaving the message window closed.
ChatbarFocusVisual = function()
	if chatBarDisabled or not coreGuiEnabled
		or (not topbarEnabled and not UserInputService.VREnabled) then
		return false
	end
	-- Re-apply visibility unconditionally. The logical Visible flag can be
	-- true while an earlier transition left the actual GUI disabled/hidden.
	local visibilityRestored = setVisible(true)
	if visibilityRestored ~= true or not ChatInstance.Visible
		or not ChatGui.Parent or not ChatBarParentFrame.Parent or not ChatBar.Parent then
		return false
	end

	ChatGui.Enabled = true
	ChatWindow.Visible = ChatInstance.Visible and coreGuiEnabled
		and (topbarEnabled or UserInputService.VREnabled) and not bubbleChatEnabled() and chatHistoryVisible()
	ChatBarParentFrame.Visible = true
	ChatBar.Visible = true
	ChatBar.Active = true
	ChatBar.TextEditable = true
	ChatBar.ClearTextOnFocus = false
	setIdleChatPromptVisible(false)
	keepMobileChatBarOffscreen()

	-- Match the working 2017 ChatBar: call CaptureFocus on the real TextBox,
	-- then update mode prefix/background/focus signals after focus is verified.
	for attempt = 1, 10 do
		if UserInputService:GetFocusedTextBox() == ChatBar then
			applyFocusedChatBarVisuals()
			lastChatActivity = os.clock()
			return true
		end
		local focusOK, focusError = pcall(function() ChatBar:CaptureFocus() end)
		if not focusOK then
			warnChat("ChatbarFocusVisual CaptureFocus attempt " .. tostring(attempt) .. " failed: " .. tostring(focusError))
		end
		RunService.RenderStepped:Wait()
	end

	if UserInputService:GetFocusedTextBox() == ChatBar then
		applyFocusedChatBarVisuals()
		return true
	end
	chatBarFocused = false
	ChatModeText.Visible = false
	ChatBar.TextTransparency = 1
	setIdleChatPromptVisible(not UserInputService.TouchEnabled and ChatInstance.Visible and not chatBarDisabled)
	setChatBarParentHeight(CHAT_BAR_HEIGHT)
	ChatBarInnerBackground.BackgroundTransparency = 0.5
	warnChat("CaptureFocus failed; ChatBar did not become the active TextBox.")
	return false
end

-- Opening and focusing are intentionally two steps: Topbar first calls
-- SetVisible(true), then ChatbarFocusVisual. Chat's own click target uses both.
focusChatBar = function()
    if chatBarDisabled or not coreGuiEnabled
        or (not topbarEnabled and not UserInputService.VREnabled) then
        return false
    end

    setVisible(true)
    if not ChatInstance.Visible then
        return false
    end

    for _ = 1, 4 do
        ChatGui.Enabled = true
        ChatWindow.Visible = ChatInstance.Visible and coreGuiEnabled
            and (topbarEnabled or UserInputService.VREnabled) and not bubbleChatEnabled() and chatHistoryVisible()
        ChatBarParentFrame.Visible = true
        if ChatWindow.Visible or bubbleChatEnabled() or chatHistoryDisabled() then break end
        RunService.RenderStepped:Wait()
    end

    return ChatbarFocusVisual() == true
end

local function focusChatBarVisible()
    return focusChatBar()
end

local function unfocusChatBar(restorePlaceholder)
	local wasFocused = chatBarFocused
	chatBarFocused = false
	mobileChatBarFocused = false
	chatBarUnfocusedAt = os.clock()
	local idleVisible = ChatInstance.Visible and coreGuiEnabled
		and (topbarEnabled or UserInputService.VREnabled) and not chatBarDisabled
	ChatBar.Visible = UserInputService.TouchEnabled and idleVisible
	ChatBar.Active = UserInputService.TouchEnabled and idleVisible
	ChatBar.TextTransparency = 1
	ChatModeText.Visible = false
	setIdleChatPromptVisible(idleVisible and not UserInputService.TouchEnabled and restorePlaceholder ~= false)
	if UserInputService.TouchEnabled then
		ChatBarParentFrame.Visible = ChatInstance.Visible and coreGuiEnabled
			and (topbarEnabled or UserInputService.VREnabled) and not chatBarDisabled
	end
	clearChatBarWelcomePrompt()
	setChatBarParentHeight(CHAT_BAR_HEIGHT)
	if UserInputService.TouchEnabled then
		ChatBarInnerBackground.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		ChatBarContainer.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		ChatBar.TextTransparency = 1
		keepMobileChatBarOffscreen()
	else
		ChatBar.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(112, 110, 106)
		ChatBarInnerBackground.BackgroundColor3 = CHAT_BAR_INNER_BACKGROUND
		ChatBarInnerBackground.BackgroundTransparency = 0.5
		ChatBarContainer.BackgroundTransparency = 0.5
	end
	if wasFocused then
		ChatInstance.ChatBarFocusChanged:fire(false)
	end
end

clickToChatConnection = ClickToChatButton.Activated:Connect(focusChatBar)

-- Topbar intentionally captures this exact TextBox directly. Observe Focused so
-- the placeholder, height, mode prefix and focus signal stay correct even when
-- focus did not enter through this module's FocusChatBar() method.
chatBarFocusedConnection = ChatBar.Focused:Connect(function()
	clearChatBarWelcomePrompt()
	if not coreGuiEnabled or chatBarDisabled
		or (not topbarEnabled and not UserInputService.VREnabled) then
		task.defer(function()
			if UserInputService:GetFocusedTextBox() == ChatBar then
				pcall(function() ChatBar:ReleaseFocus(false) end)
			end
		end)
		return
	end
	if not ChatInstance.Visible then setVisible(true) end
	applyFocusedChatBarVisuals()
end)

chatBarFocusLostConnection = ChatBar.FocusLost:Connect(function(enterPressed)
	clearChatBarWelcomePrompt()
	local input = ChatBar.Text
	if enterPressed then
		ChatBar.Text = ""
		unfocusChatBar(true)
		processInput(input)
	else
		unfocusChatBar(true)
	end
end)

chatBarTextConnection = ChatBar:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()
	if #ChatBar.Text &gt; ChatInstance.Settings.MaxCharactersInMessage then
		ChatBar.Text = string.sub(ChatBar.Text, 1, ChatInstance.Settings.MaxCharactersInMessage)
	end
	if chatBarFocused or UserInputService:GetFocusedTextBox() == ChatBar then
		setIdleChatPromptVisible(false)
	end
end)

-- Match the historical behavior: Escape abandons the draft and releases focus.
chatBarEscapeConnection = UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
	if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Escape
		and (chatBarFocused or UserInputService:GetFocusedTextBox() == ChatBar) then
		ChatBar.Text = ""
		pcall(function() ChatBar:ReleaseFocus(false) end)
	end
end)

local function pointInside(gui, point)
	if not gui or not gui.Visible then return false end
	local position = gui.AbsolutePosition
	local size = gui.AbsoluteSize
	return point.X &gt;= position.X and point.X &lt;= position.X + size.X
		and point.Y &gt;= position.Y and point.Y &lt;= position.Y + size.Y
end

chatWindowMouseEnterConnection = ChatWindow.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
	mouseLeaveFadeToken += 1
	if ChatInstance.Visible then
		lastChatActivity = os.clock()
		setBackgroundTransparency(0.5, 0.15)
		for _, record in ipairs(ChatInstance.Messages) do
			record.Faded = false
			if record.Gui then setGuiTextTransparency(record.Gui, 0) end
		end
	end
end)

chatWindowMouseLeaveConnection = ChatWindow.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
	if not ChatInstance.Visible then return end
	mouseLeaveFadeToken += 1
	local myToken = mouseLeaveFadeToken
	-- Give the pointer time to move onto the separate ChatBarOverlay. A new
	-- MouseEnter cancels this pending fade; the fade itself is gradual.
	task.delay(1.25, function()
		if myToken ~= mouseLeaveFadeToken or not ChatInstance.Visible then return end
		local mousePosition = UserInputService:GetMouseLocation()
		if pointInside(ChatWindow, mousePosition) or pointInside(ChatBarParentFrame, mousePosition) then
			return
		end
		setBackgroundTransparency(1, 0.6)
	end)
end)

local function onViewportChanged()
	updateChatLayout()
end
rootSizeConnection = GuiRoot:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteSize"):Connect(onViewportChanged)
workspaceCameraChangeConnection = workspace:GetPropertyChangedSignal("CurrentCamera"):Connect(function()
	disconnect(currentCameraConnection)
	local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
	if camera then
		currentCameraConnection = camera:GetPropertyChangedSignal("ViewportSize"):Connect(onViewportChanged)
	end
	updateChatLayout()
end)
if workspace.CurrentCamera then
	currentCameraConnection = workspace.CurrentCamera:GetPropertyChangedSignal("ViewportSize"):Connect(onViewportChanged)
end
updateChatLayout()

local function refreshChatHistoryMode()
	updateChatLayout()
	local canRender = ChatInstance.Visible and coreGuiEnabled
		and (topbarEnabled or UserInputService.VREnabled)
	ChatWindow.Visible = canRender and not chatHistoryDisabled() and not bubbleChatEnabled()
	ChatBarParentFrame.Visible = canRender and not chatBarDisabled
	keepMobileChatBarOffscreen()
end

pcall(function()
	Players:GetPropertyChangedSignal("ClassicChat"):Connect(refreshChatHistoryMode)
end)

local observedHistoryConfigs = {}
local function bindHistoryConfig(configName, config)
	if not config or config.Name ~= configName or observedHistoryConfigs[configName] == config then
		return
	end
	observedHistoryConfigs[configName] = config
	pcall(function()
		config:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Enabled"):Connect(refreshChatHistoryMode)
	end)
	refreshChatHistoryMode()
end

for _, configName in ipairs({"ChatWindowConfiguration", "ChatInputBarConfiguration"}) do
	bindHistoryConfig(configName, TextChatService:FindFirstChild(configName))
end
TextChatService.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
	if child.Name == "ChatWindowConfiguration" or child.Name == "ChatInputBarConfiguration" then
		bindHistoryConfig(child.Name, child)
	end
end)
TextChatService.ChildRemoved:Connect(function(child)
	for configName, observed in pairs(observedHistoryConfigs) do
		if observed == child then observedHistoryConfigs[configName] = nil end
	end
	refreshChatHistoryMode()
end)

-- ============================================================
-- MESSAGE COMMANDS AND TEXTCHAT TRANSPORT
-- ============================================================

local function findPlayerByName(name)
	if type(name) ~= "string" then return nil end
	local lower = string.lower(name)
	for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
		if string.lower(player.Name) == lower then return player end
	end
	return nil
end

setMessageMode = function(mode, targetPlayer)
	ChatInstance.MessageMode = mode
	if targetPlayer then ChatInstance.TargetWhisperPlayer = targetPlayer end
	if mode ~= "Whisper" then ChatInstance.TargetWhisperPlayer = nil end
	updateChatBarModeText()
end

local function sendMessage(text)
	if not ChatBackend then
		ChatInstance:AddSystemChatMessage("Chat backend is unavailable.")
		return false
	end
	if not coreGuiEnabled then return false end
	text = tostring(text or ""):gsub("^%s+", ""):gsub("%s+$", "")
	if text == "" then return false end

	local now = os.clock()
	while floodTimes[1] and now - floodTimes[1] &gt; FLOOD_CHECK_MESSAGE_INTERVAL do
		table.remove(floodTimes, 1)
	end
	if #floodTimes &gt;= FLOOD_CHECK_MESSAGE_COUNT then
		ChatInstance:AddSystemChatMessage("Wait before sending another message.")
		return false
	end

	local mode = ChatInstance.MessageMode
	local target = ChatInstance.TargetWhisperPlayer
	local payload = text
	local channelName = findChannel(mode, target)

	if mode == "Team" then
		if LocalPlayer.Neutral then
			ChatInstance:AddSystemChatMessage("You're not on a team.")
			return false
		end
		if not channelName then payload = "/team " .. payload end
	elseif mode == "Whisper" then
		if not target then
			ChatInstance:AddSystemChatMessage("Choose a player to whisper to with /w PlayerName.")
			return false
		end
		if target == LocalPlayer then
			ChatInstance:AddSystemChatMessage("You cannot send a whisper to yourself.")
			return false
		end
		if not channelName then payload = "/w " .. target.Name .. " " .. payload end
	end

	local ok, sent, result = backendCall("SendMessage", payload, channelName)
	if not ok or sent ~= true then
		ChatInstance:AddSystemChatMessage("Message could not be sent: " .. tostring(result or sent))
		return false
	end
	table.insert(floodTimes, now)
	lastChatActivity = now
	if UserInputService.TouchEnabled then setMessageMode("All") end
	return true
end

local function blockOrMuteCommand(action, targetName)
	local target = findPlayerByName(targetName)
	if not target then
		ChatInstance:AddSystemChatMessage("Cannot find player: " .. tostring(targetName))
		return
	end
	if not blockingUtility then
		ensurePlayerDropDown(function()
			blockOrMuteCommand(action, targetName)
		end)
		return
	end
	local methodName = ({ block = "BlockPlayerAsync", unblock = "UnblockPlayerAsync", mute = "MutePlayer", unmute = "UnmutePlayer" })[action]
	local method = methodName and blockingUtility[methodName]
	if type(method) ~= "function" then
		ChatInstance:AddSystemChatMessage("The player utility does not support " .. action .. ".")
		return
	end
	local ok, err = pcall(method, blockingUtility, target)
	if ok then
		ChatInstance:AddSystemChatMessage(target.Name .. " processed for " .. action .. ".")
	else
		ChatInstance:AddSystemChatMessage("Could not " .. action .. " " .. target.Name .. ": " .. tostring(err))
	end
end

local function printHelp()
	ChatInstance:AddSystemChatMessage("Help Menu")
	ChatInstance:AddSystemChatMessage("Chat commands:")
	ChatInstance:AddSystemChatMessage("/w [PlayerName] or /whisper [PlayerName] - Whisper chat")
	ChatInstance:AddSystemChatMessage("/t or /team - Team chat; /a or /all - All chat")
	ChatInstance:AddSystemChatMessage("/block, /unblock, /mute, /unmute [PlayerName]")
end

processInput = function(input)
	input = tostring(input or ""):gsub("^%s+", ""):gsub("%s+$", "")
	if input == "" then return end
	local lower = string.lower(input)

	if lower == "/?" or lower == "/help" then
		printHelp()
		return
	end

	local action, name, rest = lower:match("^/(block)%s+([%w_]+)%s*(.*)$")
	if not action then action, name, rest = lower:match("^/(unblock)%s+([%w_]+)%s*(.*)$") end
	if not action then action, name, rest = lower:match("^/(mute)%s+([%w_]+)%s*(.*)$") end
	if not action then action, name, rest = lower:match("^/(unmute)%s+([%w_]+)%s*(.*)$") end
	if action then
		blockOrMuteCommand(action, name)
		return
	end

	local whisperName, whisperText = input:match("^/[wW]%s+([%w_]+)%s*(.*)$")
	if not whisperName then whisperName, whisperText = lower:match("^/whisper%s+([%w_]+)%s*(.*)$") end
	if whisperName then
		local target = findPlayerByName(whisperName)
		if not target then
			ChatInstance:AddSystemChatMessage("Unable to find player: " .. whisperName)
			return
		end
		setMessageMode("Whisper", target)
		if whisperText and whisperText ~= "" then
			sendMessage(whisperText)
			if not UserInputService.TouchEnabled then setMessageMode("All") end
		else
			focusChatBar()
		end
		return
	end

	local teamText
	if lower == "/t" or lower == "/team" then
		teamText = ""
	else
		teamText = input:match("^/[tT][eE][aA][mM]%s+(.+)$")
			or input:match("^/[tT]%s+(.+)$")
	end
	if teamText ~= nil then
		setMessageMode("Team")
		if teamText ~= "" then sendMessage(teamText) else focusChatBar() end
		return
	end

	local allText
	if lower == "/a" or lower == "/all" or lower == "/s" or lower == "/say" then
		allText = ""
	else
		allText = input:match("^/[aA][lL][lL]%s+(.+)$")
			or input:match("^/[aA]%s+(.+)$")
			or input:match("^/[sS][aA][yY]%s+(.+)$")
			or input:match("^/[sS]%s+(.+)$")
	end
	if allText ~= nil then
		setMessageMode("All")
		if allText ~= "" then sendMessage(allText) else focusChatBar() end
		return
	end

	if string.sub(input, 1, 1) == "%" or string.match(lower, "^%(team%)") then
		local body = input:gsub("^%%+%s*", ""):gsub("^%([Tt][Ee][Aa][Mm]%)%s*", "")
		setMessageMode("Team")
		if body ~= "" then sendMessage(body) else focusChatBar() end
		return
	end

	if lower == "/console" or lower == "/newconsole" then
		local moduleName = lower == "/newconsole" and "DevConsoleMaster" or "DeveloperConsoleModule"
		local moduleScript = RobloxGui:FindFirstChild(moduleName, true)
		if moduleScript and moduleScript:IsA("ModuleScript") then
			local module = tryRequire(moduleScript, moduleName)
			if module then
				pcall(function()
					if type(module.ToggleVisibility) == "function" then
						module.ToggleVisibility()
					elseif type(module.GetVisibility) == "function" and type(module.SetVisibility) == "function" then
						module:SetVisibility(not module:GetVisibility())
					end
				end)
			end
		else
			ChatInstance:AddSystemChatMessage("Developer console module is unavailable.")
		end
		return
	end

	sendMessage(input)
end


local function addModernMessage(message)
	if not message then return end
	local statusOK, status = pcall(function() return message.Status end)
	if statusOK and status and Enum.TextChatMessageStatus and status ~= Enum.TextChatMessageStatus.Success then
		return
	end
	local text, source, channel
	pcall(function()
		text = message.Text
		source = message.TextSource
		channel = message.TextChannel
	end)
	if type(text) ~= "string" or text == "" then return end
	if source then
		local sender
		pcall(function() sender = Players:GetPlayerByUserId(source.UserId) end)
		if not sender or not shouldShowPlayerMessage(sender, text) then return end
		if reconcilePendingMessage(message, text, sender) then return end
		local channelName = string.lower(channel and channel.Name or "")
		local chatType = Enum.PlayerChatType.All
		if string.find(channelName, "team", 1, true) then
			chatType = Enum.PlayerChatType.Team
		elseif string.find(channelName, "whisper", 1, true) then
			chatType = Enum.PlayerChatType.Whisper
		end
		ChatInstance:OnPlayerChatted(chatType, sender, text, nil)
	else
		-- NPC/dialogue messages may have no TextSource. They belong in bubbles,
		-- never in the 2015/2016 player-chat history. Admit explicit system rows only.
		local messageType
		pcall(function() messageType = message.MessageType end)
		if not Enum.TextChatMessageType or messageType ~= Enum.TextChatMessageType.System then
			return
		end
		ChatInstance:AddSystemChatMessage(text)
	end
end

local pendingOutgoingById = {}
local pendingOutgoingConnections = {}
local function removePendingRecord(record)
	if not record then return end
	for id, candidate in pairs(pendingOutgoingById) do
		if candidate == record then pendingOutgoingById[id] = nil end
	end
	for i = #ChatInstance.Messages, 1, -1 do
		if ChatInstance.Messages[i] == record then
			table.remove(ChatInstance.Messages, i)
			break
		end
	end
	rebuildMessages()
end
local function reconcilePendingMessage(message, text, sender)
	local messageId
	pcall(function() messageId = message.MessageId end)
	local record = messageId and pendingOutgoingById[messageId]
	if not record and sender == LocalPlayer then
		-- MessageId is normally stable across SendingMessage/MessageReceived;
		-- text matching is a fallback for clients that do not expose it.
		for _, candidate in pairs(pendingOutgoingById) do
			if candidate.Text == text then record = candidate break end
		end
	end
	if record then
		if messageId then pendingOutgoingById[messageId] = nil end
		record.Text = text
		record.Pending = false
		record.TextTransparency = nil
		rebuildMessages()
		return true
	end
	return false
end
if ChatBackend and ChatBackend.IncomingMessage then
	backendIncomingConnection = connectSignal(ChatBackend.IncomingMessage, addModernMessage)
else
	warnChat("NewChat.IncomingMessage is unavailable; incoming chat will not render.")
end
if ChatBackend and ChatBackend.OutgoingMessage then
	table.insert(pendingOutgoingConnections, connectSignal(ChatBackend.OutgoingMessage, function(message)
		local messageId, text, source
		pcall(function() messageId = message.MessageId; text = message.Text; source = message.TextSource end)
		local userId
		pcall(function() userId = source and source.UserId end)
		if userId ~= LocalPlayer.UserId or type(text) ~= "string" or text == "" then return end
		local record = addMessage("player", text, LocalPlayer, { Pending = true }, false)
		if record then
			record.Pending = true
			record.PendingText = text
			if messageId then pendingOutgoingById[messageId] = record end
			rebuildMessages()
		end
	end))
end
if ChatBackend and ChatBackend.OutgoingMessageFailed then
	table.insert(pendingOutgoingConnections, connectSignal(ChatBackend.OutgoingMessageFailed, function(reason, text)
		for _, record in pairs(pendingOutgoingById) do
			if not text or record.PendingText == text then removePendingRecord(record); break end
		end
		if reason then warnChat("Outgoing message failed: " .. tostring(reason)) end
	end))
end

-- NPC-origin Chat.Chatted events belong exclusively to LegacyBubbleChat.
-- Only events whose origin resolves to an actual Roblox Player may enter history.
local function playerFromChatOrigin(origin)
	if typeof(origin) ~= "Instance" then return nil end
	if origin:IsA("Player") then return origin end
	local character = origin:IsA("Model") and origin or origin:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")
	if not character then return nil end
	local ok, player = pcall(function() return Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(character) end)
	return ok and player or nil
end
local okGameChat, gameChatSignal = pcall(function() return ChatService.Chatted end)
if okGameChat and gameChatSignal then
	blockingConnection = connectSignal(gameChatSignal, function(origin, message, _color)
		if typeof(message) ~= "string" or message == "" then return end
		local sender = playerFromChatOrigin(origin)
		if not sender then return end -- NPCs never become ChatWindow rows.
		ChatInstance:OnPlayerChatted(Enum.PlayerChatType.All, sender, message, nil)
	end)
end

-- ============================================================
-- COREGUI STATE AND HISTORICAL API
-- ============================================================

function ChatInstance:ToggleVisibility()
	if not coreGuiEnabled then return false end
	return setVisible(not self.Visible)
end

function ChatInstance:SetVisible(visible)
	visible = visible == true
	if visible and not coreGuiEnabled then return false end
	return setVisible(visible)
end

function ChatInstance:FocusChatBar()
	return focusChatBar()
end

function ChatInstance:GetVisibility()
	return self.Visible
end

function ChatInstance:GetCurrentWindowMessageCount()
	return self.MessageCount
end

function ChatInstance:IsFocused(useWasFocused)
	if UserInputService:GetFocusedTextBox() == ChatBar then return true end
	local focusedOK, focusedValue = pcall(function() return ChatBar:IsFocused() end)
	if focusedOK and focusedValue == true then return true end
	if chatBarFocused then return true end
	return useWasFocused == true and (os.clock() - chatBarUnfocusedAt) &lt; 0.25
end

function ChatInstance:TopbarEnabledChanged(enabled)
	self.TopbarEnabled = enabled == true
	topbarEnabled = self.TopbarEnabled
	local canRender = self.Visible and coreGuiEnabled
		and (topbarEnabled or UserInputService.VREnabled)
	ChatWindow.Visible = canRender and not bubbleChatEnabled() and chatHistoryVisible()
	ChatBarParentFrame.Visible = canRender and not chatBarDisabled
	keepMobileChatBarOffscreen()
	if not canRender and UserInputService:GetFocusedTextBox() == ChatBar then
		pcall(function() ChatBar:ReleaseFocus(false) end)
	elseif canRender and chatBarFocused then
		applyFocusedChatBarVisuals()
	end
end

function ChatInstance:CoreGuiChanged(coreGuiType, enabled, isCustomAttribute)
	if coreGuiType == Enum.CoreGuiType.All then
		allCoreGuiDisabled = enabled ~= true
	elseif coreGuiType == Enum.CoreGuiType.Chat then
		if not isCustomAttribute then chatCoreGuiEnabled = enabled == true end
	else
		return
	end

	coreGuiEnabled = isYekuCustomChatEnabled() and chatCoreGuiEnabled and not allCoreGuiDisabled
	self.CoreGuiEnabled = coreGuiEnabled
	if not coreGuiEnabled then
		-- Close the logical state as well as the rendered widgets. Otherwise a
		-- later slash/topbar call can reopen the custom chat while CoreGui.Chat is off.
		setVisible(false)
		if UserInputService:GetFocusedTextBox() == ChatBar then
			pcall(function() ChatBar:ReleaseFocus(false) end)
		end
		ChatBar.Active = false
		ChatBar.Visible = false
		ChatWindow.Visible = false
		ChatBarParentFrame.Visible = false
		setIdleChatPromptVisible(false)
		return
	end

	local canRender = self.Visible and (topbarEnabled or UserInputService.VREnabled)
	ChatGui.Enabled = true
	ChatWindow.Visible = canRender and not bubbleChatEnabled() and chatHistoryVisible()
	ChatBarParentFrame.Visible = canRender and not chatBarDisabled
	if not canRender and UserInputService:GetFocusedTextBox() == ChatBar then
		pcall(function() ChatBar:ReleaseFocus(false) end)
	end
end

local moduleApiTable = {
	ModuleName = "Chat",
	KeepVRTopbarOpen = true,
	VRIsExclusive = false,
	VRClosesNonExclusive = false,
}

function moduleApiTable:ToggleVisibility() return ChatInstance:ToggleVisibility() end
function moduleApiTable:SetVisible(value) return ChatInstance:SetVisible(value) end
function moduleApiTable:FocusChatBar() return ChatInstance:FocusChatBar() end
function moduleApiTable:FocusChatBarVisible() return focusChatBarVisible() end
function moduleApiTable:ChatbarFocusVisual() return ChatbarFocusVisual() == true end
function moduleApiTable:GetVisibility() return ChatInstance:GetVisibility() end
function moduleApiTable:GetMessageCount() return ChatInstance:GetCurrentWindowMessageCount() end
function moduleApiTable:TopbarEnabledChanged(value) return ChatInstance:TopbarEnabledChanged(value) end
function moduleApiTable:IsFocused(useWasFocused) return ChatInstance:IsFocused(useWasFocused) end
function moduleApiTable:GetChatGui() return ChatGui end
function moduleApiTable:GetChatWindowGui() return ChatWindow end
function moduleApiTable:GetChatBarTextBox() return ChatBar end
function moduleApiTable:IsBubbleChatOnly()
	-- The 2015/2016 legacy chat window always coexists with legacy bubbles.
	-- Never let a modern backend BubbleChatOnly flag hide the classic window.
	return false
end
function moduleApiTable:IsChatHistoryVisible()
	return chatHistoryVisible()
end
function moduleApiTable:ClassicChatEnabled()
	local ok, result = backendCall("ClassicChatEnabled")
	return ok and result == true
end
function moduleApiTable:IsDisabled()
	-- A missing transport/backend must not remove the local legacy chat UI.
	-- Message delivery remains governed by the actual backend/server permission.
	return not coreGuiEnabled
end
function moduleApiTable:SetCore(name, value)
	if name == "ChatMakeSystemMessage" then
		ChatInstance:AddDeveloperSystemChatMessage(value)
		return true
	elseif name == "ChatBarDisabled" then
		if type(value) ~= "boolean" then return false end
		chatBarDisabled = value
		ChatInstance.ChatBarDisabled = value
		ChatBarParentFrame.Visible = ChatInstance.Visible and coreGuiEnabled and not value
	keepMobileChatBarOffscreen()
		if value and UserInputService:GetFocusedTextBox() == ChatBar then
			pcall(function() ChatBar:ReleaseFocus(false) end)
		end
		return true
	elseif name == "ChatActive" then
		ChatInstance:SetVisible(value == true)
		return true
	end
	local ok, result = backendCall("SetCore", name, value)
	return ok and result == true
end
function moduleApiTable:GetCore(name, ...)
	if name == "ChatActive" then return ChatInstance.Visible end
	if name == "ChatBarDisabled" then return chatBarDisabled end
	local ok, result = backendCall("GetCore", name, ...)
	if ok then return result end
	return nil
end
function moduleApiTable:IsPlayerBlocked(player) return isBlocked(player) end
function moduleApiTable:IsPlayerMuted(player) return isMuted(player) end
function moduleApiTable:BlockPlayerAsync(player) return blockOrMuteCommand("block", player and player.Name) end
function moduleApiTable:UnblockPlayerAsync(player) return blockOrMuteCommand("unblock", player and player.Name) end
function moduleApiTable:MutePlayer(player) return blockOrMuteCommand("mute", player and player.Name) end
function moduleApiTable:UnmutePlayer(player) return blockOrMuteCommand("unmute", player and player.Name) end

moduleApiTable.ChatBarFocusChanged = ChatInstance.ChatBarFocusChanged
moduleApiTable.VisibilityStateChanged = ChatInstance.VisibilityStateChanged
moduleApiTable.MessagesChanged = ChatInstance.MessagesChanged
moduleApiTable.SpecialKeyPressed = createSignal()
moduleApiTable.ChatDisabled = createSignal()

-- The NewChat backend uses these local Bindables to preserve the historical
-- RegisterCoreGuiConnections API for system messages and topbar state changes.
local function createBindable(parent, className, name)
	local instance = Instance.new(className)
	instance.Name = name
	instance.Parent = parent
	return instance
end

frontendFolder = RobloxGui:FindFirstChild("LegacyChatFrontendConnections")
if not frontendFolder then
	frontendFolder = Instance.new("Folder")
	frontendFolder.Name = "LegacyChatFrontendConnections"
	frontendFolder.Parent = RobloxGui
else
	for _, child in ipairs(frontendFolder:GetChildren()) do child:Destroy() end
end

local function bindEvent(name, callback)
	local event = createBindable(frontendFolder, "BindableEvent", name)
	if callback then event.Event:Connect(callback) end
	return event
end
local function bindFunction(name, callback)
	local func = createBindable(frontendFolder, "BindableFunction", name)
	if callback then func.OnInvoke = callback end
	return func
end

local ChatWindowConnections = {
	ToggleVisibility = bindEvent("ToggleVisibility", function() ChatInstance:ToggleVisibility() end),
	SetVisible = bindEvent("SetVisible", function(value) ChatInstance:SetVisible(value == true) end),
	FocusChatBar = bindEvent("FocusChatBar", function() ChatInstance:FocusChatBar() end),
	FocusChatBarVisible = bindFunction("FocusChatBarVisible", function()
		return focusChatBarVisible() == true
	end),
	ChatbarFocusVisual = bindFunction("ChatbarFocusVisual", function()
		return ChatbarFocusVisual() == true
	end),
	TopbarEnabledChanged = bindEvent("TopbarEnabledChanged", function(value) ChatInstance:TopbarEnabledChanged(value) end),
	SpecialKeyPressed = bindEvent("SpecialKeyPressed", function(_keyCode)
		-- Slash-key ownership belongs exclusively to Topbar.lua. The chat
		-- frontend only exposes ChatbarFocusVisual for the topbar to invoke.
	end),
	CoreGuiEnabled = bindEvent("CoreGuiEnabled", function(value) ChatInstance:CoreGuiChanged(Enum.CoreGuiType.Chat, value == true, true) end),
	ChatBarFocusChanged = bindEvent("ChatBarFocusChanged"),
	VisibilityStateChanged = bindEvent("VisibilityStateChanged"),
	GetVisibility = bindFunction("GetVisibility", function() return ChatInstance.Visible end),
	GetMessageCount = bindFunction("GetMessageCount", function() return ChatInstance.MessageCount end),
	IsFocused = bindFunction("IsFocused", function() return ChatInstance:IsFocused() end),
}

ChatInstance.ChatBarFocusChanged:connect(function(value) ChatWindowConnections.ChatBarFocusChanged:Fire(value == true) end)
ChatInstance.VisibilityStateChanged:connect(function(value) ChatWindowConnections.VisibilityStateChanged:Fire(value == true) end)

local SetCoreConnections = {
	ChatMakeSystemMessage = bindEvent("ChatMakeSystemMessage", function(value) ChatInstance:AddDeveloperSystemChatMessage(value) end),
	ChatWindowPosition = bindEvent("ChatWindowPosition", function(value)
		if typeof(value) == "UDim2" then
			chatRepositioned = true
			ChatWindow.Position = value
			ChatBarParentFrame.Position = value + UDim2.new(0, 0, ChatWindow.Size.Y.Scale, ChatWindow.Size.Y.Offset + 2)
			keepMobileChatBarOffscreen()
		end
	end),
	ChatWindowSize = bindEvent("ChatWindowSize", function(value)
		if typeof(value) == "UDim2" then
			chatRepositioned = true
			ChatWindow.Size = value
			ChatBarParentFrame.Size = UDim2.new(value.X.Scale, value.X.Offset, 0, CHAT_BAR_HEIGHT)
			ChatBarParentFrame.Position = ChatWindow.Position + UDim2.new(0, 0, value.Y.Scale, value.Y.Offset + 2)
			keepMobileChatBarOffscreen()
		end
	end),
	ChatBarDisabled = bindEvent("ChatBarDisabled", function(value)
		if type(value) == "boolean" then
			chatBarDisabled = value
			ChatInstance.ChatBarDisabled = value
			ChatBarParentFrame.Visible = ChatInstance.Visible and coreGuiEnabled and not value
	keepMobileChatBarOffscreen()
		end
	end),
}

local GetCoreConnections = {
	ChatWindowPosition = bindFunction("GetChatWindowPosition", function() return ChatWindow.Position end),
	ChatWindowSize = bindFunction("GetChatWindowSize", function() return ChatWindow.Size end),
	ChatBarDisabled = bindFunction("GetChatBarDisabled", function() return chatBarDisabled end),
}

if ChatBackend and type(ChatBackend.SetVisible) == "function" then
	-- NewChat defaults to Visible=true for compatibility; this historical CoreGui
	-- frontend starts hidden until the topbar requests visibility.
	pcall(function() ChatBackend:SetVisible(false) end)
end

if ChatBackend and type(ChatBackend.RegisterCoreGuiConnections) == "function" then
	local ok, result = pcall(function()
		return ChatBackend:RegisterCoreGuiConnections({
			ChatWindow = ChatWindowConnections,
			SetCore = SetCoreConnections,
			GetCore = GetCoreConnections,
		})
	end)
	if not ok or result ~= true then
		warnChat("NewChat frontend registration failed: " .. tostring(result))
	else
		log("Registered the 2016-style chat window with NewChat.")
	end
else
	warnChat("NewChat backend is unavailable; the window can display local system messages but cannot send/receive player chat.")
end

-- Observe CoreGui flags only; do not force-enable or force-disable native CoreGui.
-- The Yeku custom chat is independent of StarterGui:GetCoreGuiEnabled(Chat),
-- because the loader intentionally suppresses Roblox's native chat UI.
coreGuiEnabled = isYekuCustomChatEnabled() and chatCoreGuiEnabled and not allCoreGuiDisabled
ChatInstance.CoreGuiEnabled = coreGuiEnabled
local customRoot = CoreGui:FindFirstChild("NewRobloxGui")
if customRoot then
    local function refreshCustomChatGate()
        local allowed = customRoot:GetAttribute("YekuCustomChatEnabled") ~= false
            and customRoot:GetAttribute("YekuCoreGuiChatEnabled") ~= false
        ChatInstance:CoreGuiChanged(Enum.CoreGuiType.Chat, allowed, true)
    end
    customChatAttributeConnection = customRoot:GetAttributeChangedSignal("YekuCustomChatEnabled"):Connect(refreshCustomChatGate)
    customRoot:GetAttributeChangedSignal("YekuCoreGuiChatEnabled"):Connect(refreshCustomChatGate)
    customRoot:GetAttributeChangedSignal("YekuCoreGuiAllEnabled"):Connect(function()
        ChatInstance:CoreGuiChanged(Enum.CoreGuiType.All,
            customRoot:GetAttribute("YekuCoreGuiAllEnabled") ~= false)
    end)
end
local coreSignalOK, coreSignal = pcall(function() return StarterGui.CoreGuiChangedSignal end)
if coreSignalOK and coreSignal then
    coreGuiConnection = connectSignal(coreSignal, function(coreGuiType, enabled)
        if coreGuiType == Enum.CoreGuiType.All or coreGuiType == Enum.CoreGuiType.Chat then
            ChatInstance:CoreGuiChanged(coreGuiType, enabled == true, false)
        end
    end)
end

-- Reconcile StarterGui's real Chat flag as a fallback for missed or delayed
-- CoreGuiChangedSignal callbacks. This never calls SetCoreGuiEnabled.
task.spawn(function()
	while true do
		local readOK, enabled = pcall(function()
			return StarterGui:GetCoreGuiEnabled(Enum.CoreGuiType.Chat)
		end)
		if readOK and type(enabled) == "boolean" and enabled ~= chatCoreGuiEnabled then
			ChatInstance:CoreGuiChanged(Enum.CoreGuiType.Chat, enabled, false)
		end
		task.wait(0.2)
	end
end)

-- Keep the rendered widgets consistent with the authoritative visibility state.
-- NewChat is set to hidden before registration above; the topbar opens it on demand.
local initialCanRender = ChatInstance.Visible and coreGuiEnabled and (topbarEnabled or UserInputService.VREnabled)
ChatWindow.Visible = initialCanRender and not bubbleChatEnabled() and chatHistoryVisible()
ChatBarParentFrame.Visible = initialCanRender and not chatBarDisabled
keepMobileChatBarOffscreen()
-- Compatibility method names used by older modules.
local function localizedWelcomeText(key, fallback)
	local translatorModule = Modules:FindFirstChild("RobloxTranslator")
	if translatorModule and translatorModule:IsA("ModuleScript") then
		local ok, translator = pcall(require, translatorModule)
		if ok and type(translator) == "table" and type(translator.FormatByKey) == "function" then
			local translatedOK, translated = pcall(function()
				return translator:FormatByKey(key, nil, fallback)
			end)
			if translatedOK and type(translated) == "string" and translated ~= "" then
				return translated
			end
		end
	end
	local localization = getYekuChatLocalization()
	if localization and type(localization.Get) == "function" then
		local ok, translated = pcall(function() return localization:Get(key, fallback) end)
		if ok and type(translated) == "string" and translated ~= "" then return translated end
	end
	return fallback
end

local welcomePrinted = false
function moduleApiTable:PrintWelcome()
	if welcomePrinted or not ChatWindow or not ChatWindow.Parent then return end
	welcomePrinted = true
	local pcWelcome = "Please chat '/?' for a list of commands."
	if UserInputService.TouchEnabled then
		-- The desktop /? history row is also shown on mobile, followed by the
		-- separate ellipsis instruction. The TextBox prompt remains separate.
		ChatInstance:AddSystemChatMessage(pcWelcome, false)
		ChatInstance:AddSystemChatMessage("Click the '...' icon to chat.", false)
	else
		ChatInstance:AddSystemChatMessage(pcWelcome, false)
	end
end
function moduleApiTable:PrintHelp() printHelp() end
function moduleApiTable:OnPlayerChatted(chatType, sender, text, receivingPlayer)
	return ChatInstance:OnPlayerChatted(chatType, sender, text, receivingPlayer)
end
function moduleApiTable:AddSystemChatMessage(text, silently, options)
	return ChatInstance:AddSystemChatMessage(text, silently, options)
end
function moduleApiTable:Disconnect()
	disconnect(backendIncomingConnection)
	for _, connection in ipairs(pendingOutgoingConnections) do disconnect(connection) end
	table.clear(pendingOutgoingConnections)
	table.clear(pendingOutgoingById)
	disconnect(coreGuiConnection)
	disconnect(customChatAttributeConnection)
	disconnect(blockingConnection)
	disconnect(rootSizeConnection)
	disconnect(currentCameraConnection)
	disconnect(workspaceCameraChangeConnection)
	disconnect(chatGuiEnabledConnection)
	disconnect(chatBarEscapeConnection)
	disconnect(chatBarFocusedConnection)
	disconnect(chatBarFocusLostConnection)
	disconnect(chatBarTextConnection)
	disconnect(clickToChatConnection)
	disconnect(chatWindowMouseEnterConnection)
	disconnect(chatWindowMouseLeaveConnection)
	disconnect(slashConnection)
	for _, tween in ipairs({transparencyTween, barContainerTransparencyTween, barInnerTransparencyTween, barTextTransparencyTween, barInputTextTransparencyTween, barModeTextTransparencyTween}) do
		if tween then pcall(function() tween:Cancel() end) end
	end
	if ChatGui and ChatGui.Parent then ChatGui:Destroy() end
	if frontendFolder and frontendFolder.Parent then frontendFolder:Destroy() end
end

-- Slash-to-open/focus is owned by the 2016 Topbar. Do not install a second
-- Chat.lua InputEnded handler: it runs too late to open a closed window reliably
-- and can race the Topbar's capture-focus operation.
slashConnection = nil

log("2016-style chat window initialized; NewChat=" .. tostring(ChatBackend ~= nil)
	.. "; LegacyBubbleChat=" .. tostring(LegacyBubbleChat ~= nil))

-- Return the historical public API table expected by Yeku Topbar / ChatMain.
return moduleApiTable

                                <int64 name="SourceAssetId">-354</int64>
                                <BinaryString name="Tags" />
                            </Properties>

                                <BinaryString name="SerializedOverrides" />
                                <ProtectedString name="Source">--[[
    FileName: LegacyBubbleChat.lua
    Version: 2015/2016 LegacyBubbleChat frontend with 2026-compatible backend

    Install as CoreGui.NewRobloxGui.Modules.LegacyBubbleChat.

    The frontend follows the supplied historical BubbleChat.lua: ui/dialog_*
    nine-slice assets and shared ui/dialog_tail.png. The 2015/2016 renderer uses 30px SourceSans text, 36px line height,
    30px text padding, a narrow 22x10 tail, 400x250 billboard, and 65/100-stud LOD. Message routing
    uses TextChatService.MessageReceived, legacy chat remotes, Chat.Chatted, and
    TextChatService.BubbleDisplayed.
]]

local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
local CoreGui = game:GetService("CoreGui")
local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local StarterGui = game:GetService("StarterGui")
local ChatService = game:GetService("Chat")
local TextChatService = game:GetService("TextChatService")
local TextService = game:GetService("TextService")
local Workspace = game:GetService("Workspace")

if not RunService:IsClient() then
	return { Initialize = function() return false end }
end

-- Do not yield while this module is required by Chat.lua. Chat must be able to
-- finish starting even when PlayerGui has not replicated yet; Initialize() can
-- be retried by the frontend once the player UI exists.
-- Require is deliberately non-blocking: Chat.lua retries Initialize while the
-- player GUI is being replicated, so the chat window itself never waits here.
local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
local PlayerGui = nil
local BubbleChatScreenGui
local GuiRoot = script:FindFirstAncestor("NewRobloxGui") or CoreGui:FindFirstChild("NewRobloxGui")

----------------------------------------------------------------
-- CHAT SETTINGS / LEGACY MESSAGE METADATA (working backend)
----------------------------------------------------------------

local function findModule(folder, name)
	if not folder then return nil end
	local object = folder:FindFirstChild(name)
	if object and object:IsA("ModuleScript") then return object end
	return nil
end

local function findClientChatModules()
	-- Resolve only this year package. Never consult game.Chat.ClientChatModules.
	local modulesRoot = script.Parent
	if not modulesRoot or modulesRoot.Name ~= "Modules" then
		modulesRoot = script:FindFirstAncestor("Modules")
	end
	local server = modulesRoot and modulesRoot:FindFirstChild("Server")
	local clientChat = server and server:FindFirstChild("ClientChat")
	local defaults = clientChat and clientChat:FindFirstChild("DefaultClientChatModules")
	if defaults and defaults:IsA("Folder") then return defaults end
	return nil
end

local ChatSettings = {}
local ChatConstants = {
	MessageTypeDefault = "Message",
	MessageTypeWhisper = "Whisper",
}
local allowedMessageTypes = { "Message", "Whisper" }

local function loadBundledChatSettings()
	local defaults = findClientChatModules()
	if not defaults then
		warn("[LegacyBubbleChat] Bundled DefaultClientChatModules is not ready; using safe bubble defaults.")
		return false
	end
	local settingsModule = findModule(defaults, "ChatSettings")
	if settingsModule then
		local ok, result = pcall(require, settingsModule)
		if ok and type(result) == "table" then
			ChatSettings = result
		else
			warn("[LegacyBubbleChat] Bundled ChatSettings failed: " .. tostring(result))
		end
	end
	local constantsModule = findModule(defaults, "ChatConstants")
	if constantsModule then
		local ok, result = pcall(require, constantsModule)
		if ok and type(result) == "table" then
			ChatConstants = result
		else
			warn("[LegacyBubbleChat] Bundled ChatConstants failed: " .. tostring(result))
		end
	end
	ChatConstants.MessageTypeDefault = ChatConstants.MessageTypeDefault or "Message"
	ChatConstants.MessageTypeWhisper = ChatConstants.MessageTypeWhisper or "Whisper"
	local configuredTypes = ChatSettings.BubbleChatMessageTypes
	if type(configuredTypes) == "table" then
		allowedMessageTypes = configuredTypes
	else
		allowedMessageTypes = { ChatConstants.MessageTypeDefault, ChatConstants.MessageTypeWhisper }
	end
	return true
end

local function getSetting(name, fallback)
	local value = ChatSettings[name]
	if value == nil then return fallback end
	return value
end

local function showOwnFilteredMessage()
	return getSetting("ShowUserOwnFilteredMessage", true) == true
end

local function messageTypeAllowed(messageData)
	if not messageData or messageData.MessageType == nil then return true end
	for _, allowedType in ipairs(allowedMessageTypes) do
		if allowedType == messageData.MessageType then return true end
	end
	return false
end

local textChatServiceActive = false
pcall(function()
	textChatServiceActive = TextChatService.ChatVersion == Enum.ChatVersion.TextChatService
end)

-- Historical 2015/2016 BubbleChat dimensions: larger dark-grey text and a narrow tail.
local CHAT_BUBBLE_FONT = Enum.Font.SourceSans
local CHAT_BUBBLE_LAYOUT_FONT_SIZE_INT = 30 -- match the original rendered 2015/2016 text
local CHAT_BUBBLE_FONT_SIZE_INT = 30 -- original 2015/2016 SourceSans lettering
local CHAT_BUBBLE_LINE_HEIGHT = 36
local CHAT_BUBBLE_TAIL_WIDTH = 22
local CHAT_BUBBLE_TAIL_HEIGHT = 10
local CHAT_BUBBLE_WIDTH_PADDING = 30
local CHAT_BUBBLE_FADE_SPEED = 1.5
local BILLBOARD_MAX_WIDTH = 400
local BILLBOARD_MAX_HEIGHT = 250
local ELLIPSES = "..."
local MAX_CHAT_MESSAGE_LENGTH = 128
local MAX_CHAT_MESSAGE_LENGTH_EXCLUSIVE = MAX_CHAT_MESSAGE_LENGTH - #ELLIPSES - 1
local NEAR_BUBBLE_DISTANCE = 65
local MAX_BUBBLE_DISTANCE = 100

local okShouldClipInGameChat, valueShouldClipInGameChat = pcall(function()
	return UserSettings():IsUserFeatureEnabled("UserShouldClipInGameChat")
end)
local shouldClipInGameChat = okShouldClipInGameChat and valueShouldClipInGameChat

local ChatType = {
	PLAYER_CHAT = "pChat",
	PLAYER_TEAM_CHAT = "pChatTeam",
	PLAYER_WHISPER_CHAT = "pChatWhisper",
	PLAYER_GAME_CHAT = "pGame",
	BOT_CHAT = "bChat",
}

local BubbleColor = { WHITE = "dub", BLUE = "blu", GREEN = "gre", RED = "red" }

local module = {}
local initialized = false
local messageConnections = {}
local cameraConnection
local workspaceCameraConnection
local nativeBubbleConnection
local nativeChildAddedConnection
local nativeBubbleConfiguration
local billboardByOrigin = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
local bubbleTemplates = {}
local scalingTemplates = {}
local bubbleChatCoreGuiAllowed = true
local bubbleChatAllAllowed = true
local bubbleGuiAllowed = true

local function bubbleChatEnabled()
	local chatOK, chatEnabled = pcall(function() return StarterGui:GetCoreGuiEnabled(Enum.CoreGuiType.Chat) end)
	if chatOK then bubbleChatCoreGuiAllowed = chatEnabled ~= false end
	if not bubbleGuiAllowed or not bubbleChatCoreGuiAllowed or not bubbleChatAllAllowed then return false end
	if ChatSettings.BubbleChatEnabled ~= nil then
		return ChatSettings.BubbleChatEnabled == true
	end
	-- Default on when an older package has not replicated ChatSettings yet.
	-- Once bundled settings load, BubbleChatEnabled is respected normally.
	return true
end

local BubbleAssets = {
	[BubbleColor.WHITE] = {
		image = "ui/dialog_white",
		notify = "ui/chatBubble_white_notify_bkg",
		slice = Rect.new(5, 5, 15, 15),
		inset = false,
	},
	[BubbleColor.BLUE] = {
		image = "ui/dialog_blue",
		notify = "ui/chatBubble_blue_notify_bkg",
		slice = Rect.new(7, 7, 33, 33),
		inset = true,
	},
	[BubbleColor.RED] = {
		image = "ui/dialog_red",
		notify = "ui/chatBubble_red_notify_bkg",
		slice = Rect.new(7, 7, 33, 33),
		inset = true,
	},
	[BubbleColor.GREEN] = {
		image = "ui/dialog_green",
		notify = "ui/chatBubble_green_notify_bkg",
		slice = Rect.new(7, 7, 33, 33),
		inset = true,
	},
