local function getYekuChatLocalization()
	local modulesRoot = script:FindFirstAncestor("Modules")
	local server = modulesRoot and modulesRoot:FindFirstChild("Server")
	local clientChat = server and server:FindFirstChild("ClientChat")
	local defaults = clientChat and clientChat:FindFirstChild("DefaultClientChatModules")
	local moduleScript = defaults and defaults:FindFirstChild("ChatLocalization")
	if not moduleScript or not moduleScript:IsA("ModuleScript") then return nil end
	local ok, result = pcall(require, moduleScript)
	if ok and type(result) == "table" and type(result.Get) == "function" then return result end
	return nil
end

local function resolveLocalizationMarkup(value)
	local text = tostring(value or "")
	local marker = "{RBX_LOCALIZATION_KEY}"
	local first = string.find(text, marker, 1, true)
	if not first then return text end
	local second = string.find(text, marker, first + #marker, true)
	if not second then return string.gsub(text, marker, "") end
	local keyPart = string.sub(text, first + #marker, second - 1)
	local fallbackPart = string.sub(text, second + #marker)
	local key = string.match(keyPart, "^%s*%((.-)%)%s*$") or string.match(keyPart, "^%s*(.-)%s*$")
	local fallback = string.match(fallbackPart, "^%s*%((.*)%)%s*$") or fallbackPart
	key = string.gsub(tostring(key or ""), "^%s*(.-)%s*$", "%1")
	fallback = string.gsub(tostring(fallback or ""), "^%s*(.-)%s*$", "%1")
	if key == "" then return fallback end
	local localization = getYekuChatLocalization()
	if localization then
		local ok, translated = pcall(function() return localization:Get(key, fallback) end)
		if ok and type(translated) == "string" and translated ~= "" then return translated end
	end
	return fallback
end

local function sanitizeMessage(message)
	message = resolveLocalizationMarkup(message)
	if #message &gt; MAX_CHAT_MESSAGE_LENGTH_EXCLUSIVE then
		return string.sub(message, 1, MAX_CHAT_MESSAGE_LENGTH_EXCLUSIVE + #ELLIPSES)
	end
	return message
end

local function lerpLength(message, minimum, maximum)
	return minimum + (maximum - minimum) * math.min(#message / 75.0, 1.0)
end

local function ensureBubbleScreenGui()
	LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
	if not LocalPlayer then return false end
	PlayerGui = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui") or LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
	if not PlayerGui then return false end
	GuiRoot = GuiRoot or script:FindFirstAncestor("NewRobloxGui") or CoreGui:FindFirstChild("NewRobloxGui")
	if not GuiRoot then return false end
	-- This is the dedicated 2015/2016 legacy renderer. Its private root must
	-- not be mistaken for Roblox's native PlayerGui.BubbleChat by the loader.
	local root = PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("YekuLegacyBubbleChat")
	if root and (not root:IsA("ScreenGui") or root:GetAttribute("YekuLegacyBubbleRoot") ~= true) then
		pcall(function() root:Destroy() end)
		root = nil
	end
	if not root then
		root = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
		root.Name = "YekuLegacyBubbleChat"
		root.ResetOnSpawn = false
		root.Parent = PlayerGui
	end
	root.Name = "YekuLegacyBubbleChat"
	root:SetAttribute("YekuLegacyBubbleRoot", true)
	root:SetAttribute("YekuLegacyBubbleVersion", 12)
	root.ResetOnSpawn = false
	root.Enabled = true

	for _, child in ipairs(PlayerGui:GetChildren()) do
		if child ~= root and child.Name == "BubbleChat" and child:IsA("ScreenGui") then
			pcall(function() child.Enabled = false; child:Destroy() end)
		end
	end
	-- Remove leftover billboards from previous versions, including untagged ones.
	for _, child in ipairs(root:GetChildren()) do
		if child:IsA("BillboardGui") then child:Destroy() end
	end
	for _, child in ipairs(PlayerGui:GetChildren()) do
		if child:IsA("BillboardGui") and child:GetAttribute("YekuLegacyBubble") == true then child:Destroy() end
	end
	BubbleChatScreenGui = root
	return true
end

local function createChatBubbleMain(filePrefix, sliceRect)
	local image = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
	image.Name = "ChatBubble"
	image.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Slice
	image.SliceCenter = sliceRect
	image.Image = "rbxasset://textures/" .. tostring(filePrefix) .. ".png"
	image.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	image.BorderSizePixel = 0
	image.ZIndex = 1
	image.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
	image.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
	return image
end

local function createChatBubbleTail(position, size)
	local tail = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
	tail.Name = "ChatBubbleTail"
	tail.Image = "rbxasset://textures/ui/dialog_tail.png"
	tail.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	tail.BorderSizePixel = 0
	tail.ZIndex = 2
	tail.Position = position
	tail.Size = size
	return tail
end

local function createChatBubbleWithTail(filePrefix, position, size, sliceRect)
	local main = createChatBubbleMain(filePrefix, sliceRect)
	createChatBubbleTail(position, size).Parent = main
	return main
end

local function createScaledChatBubbleWithTail(filePrefix, frameScaleSize, position, sliceRect)
	local main = createChatBubbleMain(filePrefix, sliceRect)
	local frame = Instance.new("Frame")
	frame.Name = "ChatBubbleTailFrame"
	frame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	frame.SizeConstraint = Enum.SizeConstraint.RelativeXX
	frame.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 1, 0)
	frame.Size = UDim2.new(frameScaleSize, 0, frameScaleSize, 0)
	frame.Parent = main
	createChatBubbleTail(position, UDim2.new(1, 0, 0.5, 0)).Parent = frame
	return main
end

local function createBubbleWithTail(bubbleColor)
	local asset = BubbleAssets[bubbleColor] or BubbleAssets[BubbleColor.WHITE]
	return createChatBubbleWithTail(
		asset.image,
		UDim2.new(0.5, -(CHAT_BUBBLE_TAIL_WIDTH / 2), 1, asset.inset and -1 or 0),
		UDim2.new(0, CHAT_BUBBLE_TAIL_WIDTH, 0, CHAT_BUBBLE_TAIL_HEIGHT),
		asset.slice
	)
end

local function createScaledBubbleWithTail(bubbleColor, frameScaleSize, position)
	local asset = BubbleAssets[bubbleColor] or BubbleAssets[BubbleColor.WHITE]
	return createScaledChatBubbleWithTail(asset.image, frameScaleSize, position, asset.slice)
end

local function getBubbleHorizontalPadding(_bubbleColor)
	return CHAT_BUBBLE_WIDTH_PADDING
end

local function createBubbleText(message, _bubbleColor)
	local text = Instance.new("TextLabel")
	text.Name = "BubbleText"
	text.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	text.BorderSizePixel = 0
	text.ZIndex = 5
	text.Position = UDim2.new(0, CHAT_BUBBLE_WIDTH_PADDING / 2, 0, 0)
	text.Size = UDim2.new(1, -CHAT_BUBBLE_WIDTH_PADDING, 1, 0)
	text.Font = CHAT_BUBBLE_FONT
	text.TextSize = CHAT_BUBBLE_FONT_SIZE_INT
	text.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.None
	text.ClipsDescendants = false
	text.TextWrapped = true
	text.Text = message
	text.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(64, 64, 64)
	text.TextStrokeTransparency = 1
	text.Visible = true
	return text
end

local function createSmallTalkBubble()
	local small = scalingTemplates[BubbleColor.WHITE]:Clone()
	small.Name = "SmallTalkBubble"
	small.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, -40)
	small.Visible = false

	local text = createBubbleText("...", BubbleColor.WHITE)
	text.Name = "BubbleText"
	text.TextScaled = true
	text.TextWrapped = false
	text.Visible = true
	text.Parent = small
	return small
end

local function getCameraPosition()
	local camera = Workspace.CurrentCamera
	return camera and camera.CFrame.Position or Vector3.new()
end

local function getOriginKey(origin)
	if not origin or typeof(origin) ~= "Instance" then return nil end
	if origin:IsA("Model") then return origin end
	local model = origin:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")
	return model or origin
end

local function getAdornee(origin)
	if not origin or typeof(origin) ~= "Instance" then return nil end
	if origin:IsA("BasePart") then return origin end
	if origin:IsA("Model") then
		local head = origin:FindFirstChild("Head")
		if head and head:IsA("BasePart") then return head end
		if origin.PrimaryPart then return origin.PrimaryPart end
		return origin:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)
	end
	local model = origin:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")
	if model then return getAdornee(model) end
	return nil
end

local function isLocalOrigin(origin)
	local character = LocalPlayer and LocalPlayer.Character
	return character ~= nil and origin ~= nil
		and (origin == character or (typeof(origin) == "Instance" and origin:IsDescendantOf(character)))
end

local function createBillboard(origin)
	local adornee = getAdornee(origin)
	if not adornee or not BubbleChatScreenGui then return nil end

	local billboard = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
	billboard.Name = "YekuLegacyBubbleBillboard"
	billboard:SetAttribute("YekuLegacyBubble", true)
	billboard.Adornee = adornee
	billboard.Size = UDim2.new(0, BILLBOARD_MAX_WIDTH, 0, BILLBOARD_MAX_HEIGHT)
	billboard.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 2.25, 0)
	billboard.Enabled = bubbleGuiAllowed and bubbleChatCoreGuiAllowed and bubbleChatAllAllowed
	billboard.Parent = PlayerGui

	local frame = Instance.new("Frame")
	frame.Name = "BillboardFrame"
	frame.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
	frame.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, -0.5, 0)
	frame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	frame.Parent = billboard

	local state = {
		Origin = origin,
		BillboardGui = billboard,
		Frame = frame,
		Lines = {},
		SmallTalkBubble = nil,
		Pruning = false,
	}
	state.SmallTalkBubble = createSmallTalkBubble()
	state.SmallTalkBubble.Parent = frame
	billboardByOrigin[origin] = state
	return state
end

local function getState(origin)
	local key = getOriginKey(origin)
	if not key then return nil end
	local state = billboardByOrigin[key]
	if state and state.BillboardGui and state.BillboardGui.Parent then
		local adornee = getAdornee(origin)
		if adornee then state.BillboardGui.Adornee = adornee end
		return state
	end
	return createBillboard(key)
end

local function setBubbleVisibility(state, distance)
	local billboard = state.BillboardGui
	if not billboard or not billboard.Parent then return end
	local localOrigin = isLocalOrigin(state.Origin)

	if distance &lt; NEAR_BUBBLE_DISTANCE then
		billboard.Size = UDim2.new(0, BILLBOARD_MAX_WIDTH, 0, BILLBOARD_MAX_HEIGHT)
		billboard.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, localOrigin and 2.25 or 3.0, 0)
		billboard.Enabled = bubbleGuiAllowed and bubbleChatCoreGuiAllowed and bubbleChatAllAllowed
		for _, line in ipairs(state.Lines) do
			if line.RenderBubble then line.RenderBubble.Visible = billboard.Enabled end
		end
		if state.SmallTalkBubble then state.SmallTalkBubble.Visible = false end
	elseif distance &lt; MAX_BUBBLE_DISTANCE then
		billboard.Size = UDim2.new(4, 0, 2.5, 0)
		billboard.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 3.5, 0)
		billboard.Enabled = bubbleGuiAllowed and bubbleChatCoreGuiAllowed and bubbleChatAllAllowed
		for _, line in ipairs(state.Lines) do
			if line.RenderBubble then line.RenderBubble.Visible = false end
		end
		if state.SmallTalkBubble then state.SmallTalkBubble.Visible = billboard.Enabled and #state.Lines &gt; 0 end
	else
		billboard.Enabled = false
	end
end

local function refreshAllBillboards()
	local cameraPosition = getCameraPosition()
	for origin, state in pairs(billboardByOrigin) do
		if not state.BillboardGui or not state.BillboardGui.Parent or not origin.Parent then
			if state.BillboardGui then pcall(function() state.BillboardGui:Destroy() end) end
			billboardByOrigin[origin] = nil
		else
			local adornee = getAdornee(origin)
			if adornee then
				state.BillboardGui.Adornee = adornee
				setBubbleVisibility(state, (adornee.Position - cameraPosition).Magnitude)
			else
				state.BillboardGui.Enabled = false
			end
		end
	end
end

local function relayout(state)
	if not state or not state.Lines then return end
	local queue = state.Lines
	local count = #queue
	if count &lt;= 1 then return end

	local newest = queue[count]
	local currentY = -(newest.Height or (newest.RenderBubble and newest.RenderBubble.Size.Y.Offset) or 0)
	for index = count - 1, 1, -1 do
		local line = queue[index]
		local bubble = line and line.RenderBubble
		if bubble and bubble.Parent then
			local height = line.Height or bubble.Size.Y.Offset
			local target = UDim2.new(
				bubble.Position.X.Scale,
				bubble.Position.X.Offset,
				1,
				currentY - height - CHAT_BUBBLE_TAIL_HEIGHT
			)
			bubble:TweenPosition(target, Enum.EasingDirection.Out, Enum.EasingStyle.Bounce, 0.1, true)

			local tail = bubble:FindFirstChild("ChatBubbleTail")
			if tail then tail:Destroy() end
			local text = bubble:FindFirstChild("BubbleText")
			if text and text:IsA("TextLabel") then text.TextTransparency = 0.5 end
			currentY -= height + CHAT_BUBBLE_TAIL_HEIGHT
		end
	end
end

local function fadeBubble(line)
	local bubble = line and line.RenderBubble
	if not bubble or not bubble.Parent then return end
	local text = bubble:FindFirstChild("BubbleText")
	local tail = bubble:FindFirstChild("ChatBubbleTail")
	local elapsed = 0

	while bubble.Parent and bubble.ImageTransparency &lt; 1 and elapsed &lt; 2 do
		local dt = RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
		elapsed += dt
		local fadeAmount = dt * CHAT_BUBBLE_FADE_SPEED
		bubble.ImageTransparency = math.min(1, bubble.ImageTransparency + fadeAmount)
		if text and text.Parent then
			text.TextTransparency = math.min(1, text.TextTransparency + fadeAmount)
		end
		if tail and tail.Parent then
			tail.ImageTransparency = math.min(1, tail.ImageTransparency + fadeAmount)
		end
	end
	if bubble.Parent then bubble:Destroy() end
end

local function pruneExpired(state)
	if not state or state.Pruning or not (state.Lines[1] and state.Lines[1].Expired) then return end
	state.Pruning = true

	task.spawn(function()
		while state.Lines[1] and state.Lines[1].Expired do
			local line = state.Lines[1]
			fadeBubble(line)
			if state.Lines[1] == line then
				table.remove(state.Lines, 1)
			end
			line.RenderBubble = nil
			relayout(state)
		end

		state.Pruning = false
		if #state.Lines == 0 then
			if state.BillboardGui then pcall(function() state.BillboardGui:Destroy() end) end
			billboardByOrigin[state.Origin] = nil
		elseif state.Lines[1] and state.Lines[1].Expired then
			pruneExpired(state)
		end
	end)
end

local function renderBubble(origin, message, bubbleColor, isLocal)
	if not bubbleChatEnabled() then return end
	if not origin or not origin.Parent then return end

	message = sanitizeMessage(message)
	if message == "" then return end

	local state = getState(origin)
	if not state then return end

	local template = bubbleTemplates[bubbleColor] or bubbleTemplates[BubbleColor.WHITE]
	if not template then return end
	local bubble = template:Clone()
	bubble.Visible = false
	local text = createBubbleText(message, bubbleColor)
	text.Parent = bubble
	bubble.Parent = state.Frame

	local maxTextWidth = math.max(1, BILLBOARD_MAX_WIDTH - CHAT_BUBBLE_WIDTH_PADDING - 2)
	local textBounds = TextService:GetTextSize(
		text.Text,
		CHAT_BUBBLE_LAYOUT_FONT_SIZE_INT,
		CHAT_BUBBLE_FONT,
		Vector2.new(maxTextWidth, BILLBOARD_MAX_HEIGHT)
	)
	local widthScale = math.clamp((textBounds.X + CHAT_BUBBLE_WIDTH_PADDING + 2) / BILLBOARD_MAX_WIDTH, 0.1, 1)
	local numberOfLines = math.max(textBounds.Y / CHAT_BUBBLE_LAYOUT_FONT_SIZE_INT, 1)
	local height = math.clamp(numberOfLines * CHAT_BUBBLE_LINE_HEIGHT, CHAT_BUBBLE_LINE_HEIGHT, BILLBOARD_MAX_HEIGHT)

	bubble.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
	bubble.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 1, 0)

	local line = {
		Origin = origin,
		Message = message,
		RenderBubble = bubble,
		WidthScale = widthScale,
		Height = height,
		Expired = false,
		CreatedAt = os.clock(),
		Lifetime = isLocal and lerpLength(message, 8, 15) or lerpLength(message, 12, 20),
	}
	table.insert(state.Lines, line)

	bubble:TweenSizeAndPosition(
		UDim2.new(widthScale, 0, 0, height),
		UDim2.new((1 - widthScale) / 2, 0, 1, -height),
		Enum.EasingDirection.Out,
		Enum.EasingStyle.Elastic,
		0.1,
		true,
		function()
			if bubble.Parent then text.Visible = true end
		end
	)

	-- Apply LOD after creating the new bubble so it is visible when near.
	refreshAllBillboards()
	relayout(state)

	task.delay(line.Lifetime, function()
		if not initialized or not line.RenderBubble then return end
		line.Expired = true
		pruneExpired(state)
	end)
end

local function findPlayer(playerName)
	if type(playerName) ~= "string" then return nil end
	for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
		if player.Name == playerName then return player end
	end
	return nil
end

local function isPlayerMessageSuccessful(message)
	local ok, status = pcall(function() return message.Status end)
	if not ok or status == nil then return true end
	local statuses = Enum.TextChatMessageStatus
	return not statuses or status == statuses.Success
end

local function getOriginContainer(origin)
	if not origin or typeof(origin) ~= "Instance" then return nil end
	if origin:IsA("Model") then return origin end
	if origin:IsA("BasePart") then
		return origin:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model") or origin
	end
	return nil
end


-- Player.Chatted is a compatibility fallback for clients where the selected
-- TextChatService/legacy event transport does not reach the custom renderer.
-- All player transports pass through this short dedupe window so a single line
-- cannot create two overlapping bubbles.
local recentPlayerMessages = {}
local lastPlayerMessageCleanup = 0
local function renderPlayerMessageOnce(player, message, bubbleColor)
	if not player or type(message) ~= "string" or message == "" then return false end
	if player == LocalPlayer and not showOwnFilteredMessage() then return false end
	local now = os.clock()
	if now - lastPlayerMessageCleanup &gt; 10 then
		for key, timestamp in pairs(recentPlayerMessages) do
			if now - timestamp &gt; 2 then recentPlayerMessages[key] = nil end
		end
		lastPlayerMessageCleanup = now
	end
	local key = tostring(player.UserId) .. "\0" .. message
	local previous = recentPlayerMessages[key]
	if previous and now - previous &lt; 0.65 then return false end
	recentPlayerMessages[key] = now
	if not player.Character then return false end
	renderBubble(player.Character, message, bubbleColor or BubbleColor.WHITE, player == LocalPlayer)
	return true
end

local function onTextChatMessage(message)
	if not bubbleChatEnabled() or not message or not isPlayerMessageSuccessful(message) then return end

	local source, text = nil, nil
	local readOK = pcall(function()
		source = message.TextSource
		text = message.Text
	end)
	if not readOK or not source or type(text) ~= "string" or text == "" then return end

	local player
	local playerOK = pcall(function()
		player = Players:GetPlayerByUserId(source.UserId)
	end)
	if not playerOK or not player or not player.Character then return end
	if player == LocalPlayer and not showOwnFilteredMessage() then return end

	renderPlayerMessageOnce(player, text, BubbleColor.WHITE)
end

local function legacyBubbleColor(messageData)
	local extra = type(messageData.ExtraData) == "table" and messageData.ExtraData or {}
	local color = extra.BubbleColor or extra.ChatColor
	if color == Enum.ChatColor.Blue then return BubbleColor.BLUE end
	if color == Enum.ChatColor.Red then return BubbleColor.RED end
	if color == Enum.ChatColor.Green then return BubbleColor.GREEN end
	return BubbleColor.WHITE
end

local function onUnfilteredLegacyMessage(messageData)
	if not bubbleChatEnabled() or type(messageData) ~= "table" then return end
	if not messageTypeAllowed(messageData) or type(messageData.Message) ~= "string" then return end

	local player = findPlayer(messageData.FromSpeaker)
	if not player or not player.Character then return end

	-- Never render another player's unfiltered text. The local echo is handled
	-- here only when the historical setting requests the unfiltered local echo.
	if not messageData.IsFiltered or messageData.FromSpeaker == LocalPlayer.Name then
		if messageData.FromSpeaker ~= LocalPlayer.Name or showOwnFilteredMessage() then return end
	end
	renderPlayerMessageOnce(player, messageData.Message, BubbleColor.WHITE)
end

local function onFilteredLegacyMessage(messageData)
	if not bubbleChatEnabled() or type(messageData) ~= "table" then return end
	if not messageTypeAllowed(messageData) or type(messageData.Message) ~= "string" then return end

	local player = findPlayer(messageData.FromSpeaker)
	if not player or not player.Character then return end
	if player == LocalPlayer and not showOwnFilteredMessage() then return end

	renderPlayerMessageOnce(player, messageData.Message, legacyBubbleColor(messageData))
end

local function onGameChatMessage(origin, message, color)
	if not bubbleChatEnabled() or typeof(origin) ~= "Instance" then return end
	if not (origin:IsA("Model") or origin:IsA("BasePart")) then return end
	if type(message) ~= "string" or message == "" then return end

	local normalizedOrigin = getOriginContainer(origin) or origin
	local bubbleColor = BubbleColor.WHITE
	if color == Enum.ChatColor.Blue then
		bubbleColor = BubbleColor.BLUE
	elseif color == Enum.ChatColor.Red then
		bubbleColor = BubbleColor.RED
	elseif color == Enum.ChatColor.Green then
		bubbleColor = BubbleColor.GREEN
	end
	renderBubble(normalizedOrigin, message, bubbleColor, isLocalOrigin(normalizedOrigin))
end

local function onTextChatBubbleDisplayed(adornee, textChatMessage)
	if not bubbleChatEnabled() or typeof(adornee) ~= "Instance" then return end
	if not (adornee:IsA("Model") or adornee:IsA("BasePart")) then return end

	local messageText
	local ok = pcall(function()
		messageText = textChatMessage and textChatMessage.Text
	end)
	if not ok or type(messageText) ~= "string" or messageText == "" then return end

	local origin = getOriginContainer(adornee) or adornee
	if not (origin:IsA("Model") or origin:IsA("BasePart")) then return end
	local player = origin:IsA("Model") and Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(origin) or nil
	if player then
		renderPlayerMessageOnce(player, messageText, BubbleColor.WHITE)
	else
		renderBubble(origin, messageText, BubbleColor.WHITE, isLocalOrigin(origin))
	end
end

local function connectSignal(signal, callback)
	if not signal then return nil end
	local ok, connection = pcall(function()
		if signal.Connect then return signal:Connect(callback) end
		if signal.connect then return signal:connect(callback) end
		error("Signal does not expose Connect/connect")
	end)
	return ok and connection or nil
end

local function disableNativeBubbles()
	local config = TextChatService:FindFirstChild("BubbleChatConfiguration")
		or TextChatService:FindFirstChildOfClass("BubbleChatConfiguration")
	if not config then return end

	if nativeBubbleConfiguration ~= config then
		if nativeBubbleConnection then
			pcall(function() nativeBubbleConnection:Disconnect() end)
		end
		nativeBubbleConfiguration = config
		nativeBubbleConnection = connectSignal(config:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Enabled"), function()
			if nativeBubbleConfiguration == config and config.Enabled then
				pcall(function() config.Enabled = false end)
			end
		end)
	end
	pcall(function() config.Enabled = false end)
end

local function addMessageConnection(signal, callback)
	local connection = connectSignal(signal, callback)
	if connection then table.insert(messageConnections, connection) end
	return connection
end

function module.Initialize(NewChatBackend)
	if initialized then return true end
	if not ensureBubbleScreenGui() then return false end
	loadBundledChatSettings()
	initialized = true
	local function updateBubbleCoreGuiPermission(changedType, changedEnabled)
		local chatOK, chatEnabled = pcall(function() return StarterGui:GetCoreGuiEnabled(Enum.CoreGuiType.Chat) end)
		if chatOK then bubbleChatCoreGuiAllowed = chatEnabled ~= false end
		if changedType == Enum.CoreGuiType.Chat then bubbleChatCoreGuiAllowed = changedEnabled == true end
		if changedType == Enum.CoreGuiType.All then bubbleChatAllAllowed = changedEnabled == true end
		bubbleGuiAllowed = bubbleChatCoreGuiAllowed and bubbleChatAllAllowed
		local allowed = bubbleGuiAllowed
		if BubbleChatScreenGui then BubbleChatScreenGui.Enabled = allowed end
		if not allowed then
			for origin, state in pairs(billboardByOrigin) do
				if state.BillboardGui then pcall(function() state.BillboardGui:Destroy() end) end
				billboardByOrigin[origin] = nil
			end
		end
	end
	updateBubbleCoreGuiPermission()
	local coreGuiSignalOK, coreGuiSignal = pcall(function() return StarterGui.CoreGuiChangedSignal end)
	if coreGuiSignalOK and coreGuiSignal then
		table.insert(messageConnections, coreGuiSignal:Connect(function(coreGuiType, enabled)
			if coreGuiType == Enum.CoreGuiType.Chat or core
